Abstract
摘要
The rapid development of advanced AI agents and the imminent deployment of many instances of these agents will give rise to multi-agent systems of unprecedented complexity. These systems pose novel and under-explored risks. In this report, we provide a structured taxonomy of these risks by identifying three key failure modes (miscoordination, conflict, and collusion) based on agents’ incentives, as well as seven key risk factors (information asymmetries, network effects, selection pressures, destabilising dynamics, commitment problems, emergent agency, and multi-agent security) that can underpin them. We highlight several important instances of each risk, as well as promising directions to help mitigate them. By anchoring our analysis in a range of real-world examples and experimental evidence, we illustrate the distinct challenges posed by multi-agent systems and their implications for the safety, governance, and ethics of advanced AI.
先进 AI 智能体的快速发展，以及这些智能体的大量实例即将投入部署，将催生复杂程度前所未有的多智能体系统。这些系统带来了新颖且尚未得到充分研究的风险。本报告根据智能体的激励机制，识别出三种关键失效模式（协调失灵、冲突和合谋），以及可能构成这些失效模式基础的七项关键风险因素（信息不对称、网络效应、选择压力、破坏稳定的动态过程、承诺问题、涌现能动性和多智能体安全），由此对这些风险进行了结构化分类。我们重点介绍了每类风险的若干重要实例，以及有望帮助缓解这些风险的研究方向。通过将分析建立在一系列现实案例和实验证据之上，我们阐明了多智能体系统带来的独特挑战，以及这些挑战对先进 AI 的安全、治理和伦理的影响。
Contents目录
Executive Summary执行摘要
The proliferation of increasingly advanced AI not only promises widespread benefits, but also presents new risks (Bengio et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2023). Today, AI systems are beginning to autonomously interact with one another and adapt their behaviour accordingly, forming multi- agent systems. This change is due to the widespread adoption of sophisticated models that can interact via a range of modalities (including text, images, and audio), and the competitive advantages conferred by autonomous, adaptive agents (Google DeepMind, 2024; Anthropic, 2024; OpenAI, 2025).
日益先进的 AI 的广泛普及不仅有望带来广泛益处，也带来了新的风险 (Bengio et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2023)。如今，AI 系统开始自主地相互交互，并相应地调整自身行为，从而形成多智能体系统。这一变化源于能够通过多种模态（包括文本、图像和音频）进行交互的复杂模型得到广泛采用，以及具备自主性和适应性的智能体所带来的竞争优势 (Google DeepMind, 2024; Anthropic, 2024; OpenAI, 2025)。
While still relatively rare, groups of advanced AI agents are already responsible for tasks that range from trading million-dollar assets (Ferreira et al., 2021; AmplifyETFs, 2025; Sun et al., 2023) to recommending actions to commanders in battle (Palantir, 2025; Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024). In the near future, applications will include not only economic and military domains, but are likely to extend to energy management, transport networks, and other critical infrastructure (Mayorkas, 2024; Camacho et al., 2024). Large populations of AI agents will also feature in more familiar social settings as intelligent personal assistants or representatives, capable of being delegated increasingly complex and important tasks.
尽管目前仍相对少见，先进 AI 智能体群体已经开始负责任务，其范围从交易价值百万美元的资产 (Ferreira et al., 2021; AmplifyETFs, 2025; Sun et al., 2023)，到为战场上的指挥官提供行动建议 (Palantir, 2025; Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024)。在不久的将来，其应用不仅将涵盖经济和军事领域，还很可能扩展到能源管理、交通运输网络和其他关键基础设施 (Mayorkas, 2024; Camacho et al., 2024)。大量 AI 智能体还将以智能个人助理或代理人的身份出现在更为熟悉的社会场景中，并能够受托执行日益复杂且重要的任务。
While bringing new opportunities for scalable automation and more diffuse benefits to society, these advanced, multi-agent systems present novel risks that are distinct from those posed by single agents or less advanced technologies, and which have been systematically underappreciated and understudied. This lack of attention is partly because present-day multi-agent systems are rare (and those that do exist are often highly controlled, such as in automated warehouses), but also because even single agents present many unsolved problems (Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024). Given the current rate of progress and adoption, however, we urgently need to evaluate (and prepare to mitigate) multi-agent risks from advanced AI. More concretely, we provide recommendations throughout the report that can largely be classified as follows.
这些先进的多智能体系统在为可规模化的自动化和更广泛惠及社会带来新机遇的同时，也带来了不同于单个智能体或较不先进技术所引发的新型风险，而这些风险一直受到系统性的低估，相关研究也系统性地不足。这种关注不足，部分是因为当今的多智能体系统较为少见（而且现有系统往往受到高度控制，例如自动化仓库中的系统），也因为即便是单个智能体，也存在许多尚未解决的问题（Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024）。然而，鉴于目前的进步和应用速度，我们迫切需要评估先进 AI 带来的多智能体风险，并为缓解这些风险做好准备。更具体地说，我们在报告各处提出的建议大致可分为以下几类。
Evaluation: Today’s AI systems are developed and tested in isolation, despite the fact that they will soon interact with each other. In order to understand how likely and severe multi-agent risks are, we need new methods of detecting how and when they might arise, such as: evaluating the cooperative capabilities, biases, and vulnerabilities of models; testing for new or improved dangerous capabilities in multi-agent settings (such as manipulation, collusion, or overriding safeguards); more open-ended simulations to study dynamics, selection pressures, and emergent behaviours; and studies of how well these tests and simulations match real-world deployments.
评估：如今的 AI 系统是在彼此隔离的情况下开发和测试的，尽管它们很快就会相互交互。为了了解多智能体风险发生的可能性及其严重程度，我们需要新的方法来探测这些风险可能如何以及何时出现，例如：评估模型的合作能力、偏差和脆弱性；测试多智能体环境中是否出现新的或增强的危险能力（如操纵、合谋或绕过安全防护措施）；开展更开放的模拟，以研究动态过程、选择压力和涌现行为；以及研究这些测试和模拟与现实部署情况的吻合程度。
Mitigation: Evaluation is only the first step towards mitigating multi-agent risks, which will require new technical advances. While our understanding of these risks is still growing, there are promising directions that we can begin to explore now, such as: scaling peer incentivisation methods to state-of-the-art models; developing secure protocols for trusted agent interactions; leveraging information design and the potential transparency of AI agents; and stabilising dynamic multi-agent networks and ensuring they are robust to the presence of adversaries.
缓解：评估只是缓解多智能体风险的第一步，而缓解这些风险还需要新的技术进展。尽管我们对这些风险的认识仍在不断深化，但现在就可以开始探索一些有前景的方向，例如：将同伴激励方法扩展应用于最先进的模型；开发用于可信智能体交互的安全协议；利用信息设计和 AI 智能体潜在的透明性；以及使动态多智能体网络保持稳定，并确保其在存在对抗者的情况下仍具备稳健性。
Collaboration: Multi-agent risks inherently involve many different actors and stakeholders, often in complex, dynamic environments. Greater progress can be made on these interdisciplinary problems by leveraging insights from other fields, such as: better understanding the causes of undesirable outcomes in complex adaptive systems and evolutionary settings; determining the moral responsibilities and legal liabilities for harms not caused by any single AI system; drawing lessons from existing efforts to regulate multi-agent systems in high-stakes contexts, such as financial markets; and determining the security vulnerabilities and affordances of multi-agent systems.
协作：多智能体风险本身就涉及许多不同的参与者和利益相关方，而且往往处于复杂、动态的环境中。借鉴其他领域的见解，可以在这些跨学科问题上取得更大进展，例如：更好地理解复杂适应系统和演化环境中不良结果的成因；确定对于并非由任何单个 AI 系统造成的伤害，应承担何种道德责任和法律责任；从现有针对金融市场等高风险情境中的多智能体系统的监管工作中汲取经验；以及确定多智能体系统的安全漏洞及其可供性。
To support these recommendations, we introduce a taxonomy of AI risks that are new, much more challenging, or qualitatively different in the multi-agent setting, together with a preliminary assessment of what can be done to mitigate them. We identify three high-level failure modes, which depend on the nature of the agents’ objectives and the intended behaviour of the system: miscoordination, conflict, and collusion. We then describe seven key risk factors that can lead to these failures: information asymmetries, network effects, selection pressures, destabilising dynamics, commitment and trust, emergent agency, and multi-agent security. For each problem we provide a definition, key instances of how and where it can arise, illustrative case studies, and promising directions for future work. We conclude by discussing the implications for existing work in AI safety, AI governance, and AI ethics.
为支持这些建议，我们提出了一套 AI 风险分类体系，涵盖多智能体环境中新出现的、挑战显著增大的或在性质上有所不同的风险，并初步评估了可以采取哪些措施来缓解这些风险。我们识别出三种高层次的失效模式，它们取决于智能体目标的性质以及系统的预期行为：协调失误、冲突和合谋。随后，我们描述了可能导致这些失效的七个关键风险因素：信息不对称、网络效应、选择压力、导致不稳定的动态过程、承诺与信任、涌现的能动性，以及多智能体安全。对于每个问题，我们都给出定义、说明其如何以及在何处出现的关键实例、用于阐释的案例研究，以及未来工作中有前景的方向。最后，我们讨论这些内容对 AI 安全、AI 治理和 AI 伦理领域现有工作的启示。
1 Introduction1 引言
The proliferation of increasingly advanced AI not only promises widespread benefits, but also presents new risks (Bengio et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2023). In the future, AI systems will commonly interact and adapt in response to one another, forming multi-agent systems.[fn1] This trend will be driven by several factors. First, recent technical progress and publicity will continue to drive adoption, including in high-stakes areas such as financial trading (Ferreira et al., 2021; AmplifyETFs, 2025; Sun et al., 2023) and military strategy (Palantir, 2025; Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024). Second, AI systems that can act autonomously and adapt while deployed as agents will have competitive advantages compared to non-adaptive systems or those with humans in the loop. Third, the more widely such agents are deployed, the more they will come to interact with one another.
日益先进的 AI 的广泛普及不仅有望带来广泛益处，也带来了新的风险 (Bengio et al., 2024; Chan et al., 2023)。未来，AI 系统将普遍相互交互，并根据彼此的行为作出适应性调整，从而形成多智能体系统。[fn1] 这一趋势将由多种因素推动。首先，近期的技术进展和宣传将继续推动 AI 的应用，包括在金融交易（Ferreira et al., 2021; AmplifyETFs, 2025; Sun et al., 2023）和军事战略（Palantir, 2025; Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024）等高风险领域中的应用。其次，能够以智能体形式部署、自主行动并在部署期间进行适应的 AI 系统，相较于不具备适应能力的系统或需要人类参与决策的系统，将具有竞争优势。第三，此类智能体部署得越广泛，它们彼此之间的交互就会越多。
Footnote: 1 A fundamental fact about (software-based) AI systems is that they can be easily duplicated. Thus, the vast training costs involved in producing state-of-the-art systems can be amortized over millions of instances. In this sense, if nothing else, the concept of multi-agent systems is core to transformative AI.
脚注：1 关于（基于软件的）AI 系统，一个基本事实是它们很容易被复制。因此，开发最先进系统所涉及的巨额训练成本可以分摊到数百万个实例上。从这个意义上说，即便不考虑其他因素，多智能体系统这一概念也是变革性 AI 的核心。
The emergence of these advanced multi-agent systems presents a number of risks which have thus far been systematically underappreciated and understudied. In part, this lack of attention is because the deployment of such systems is currently rare, or constrained to highly controlled settings (such as automated warehouses) that do not suffer from the most severe risks. In part, it is because even the simpler problem of ensuring the safe and ethical behaviour of a single advanced AI system is far from solved (Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024), and multi-agent settings are strictly more complex. Indeed, many multi-agent risks are inherently sociotechnical and require attention from many stakeholders and researchers across many disciplines (Curtis et al., 2024; Lazar & Nelson, 2023).
这些先进多智能体系统的出现带来了诸多风险，而迄今为止，这些风险一直未得到系统性的充分重视和研究。这种关注不足，部分原因在于此类系统目前尚少部署，或仅限于自动化仓库等高度受控、不会面临最严重风险的环境。部分原因还在于，即使是确保单个先进 AI 系统行为安全且符合伦理这一较简单的问题，也远未得到解决 (Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024)，而多智能体情境在严格意义上更为复杂。事实上，许多多智能体风险本质上属于社会技术问题，需要众多利益相关方以及来自多个学科的研究人员共同关注 (Curtis et al., 2024; Lazar & Nelson, 2023)。
Importantly, these risks are distinct from those posed by single agents or less advanced technologies, and will not necessarily be addressed by efforts to mitigate the latter. For example: the alignment of AI agents with different actors is insufficient to prevent conflict if those actors have diverging interests (Sourbut et al., 2024; Manheim, 2019; Dafoe et al., 2020; Critch & Krueger, 2020; Jagadeesan et al., 2023); errors that may be acceptable in isolation could compound in complex, dynamic networks of agents (Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Maas, 2018; Kirilenko et al., 2017; Buldyrev et al., 2010; Sanders et al., 2018); and groups of agents could combine or collude to develop dangerous capabilities or goals that cannot be ascribed to any individual (Jones et al., 2024; Motwani et al., 2024; Mogul, 2006; Calvano et al., 2020; Drexler, 2022). Advanced AI also introduces phenomena that differ fundamentally from previous generations of AI or other technologies, requiring new approaches to mitigating these risks (Bengio et al., 2024).
重要的是，这些风险不同于单个智能体或较不先进的技术所带来的风险，因此，旨在缓解后者的努力未必能应对这些风险。例如：如果不同行动主体的利益存在分歧，仅使 AI 智能体分别与这些主体对齐，并不足以防止冲突 (Sourbut et al., 2024; Manheim, 2019; Dafoe et al., 2020; Critch & Krueger, 2020; Jagadeesan et al., 2023)；孤立来看可能可以接受的错误，在复杂、动态的智能体网络中可能累积放大 (Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Maas, 2018; Kirilenko et al., 2017; Buldyrev et al., 2010; Sanders et al., 2018)；智能体群体还可能联合或合谋，形成无法归因于任何单个智能体的危险能力或目标 (Jones et al., 2024; Motwani et al., 2024; Mogul, 2006; Calvano et al., 2020; Drexler, 2022)。先进 AI 还带来了与前几代 AI 或其他技术存在根本差异的现象，因此需要采用新的方法来缓解这些风险 (Bengio et al., 2024)。
With the current rate of progress, we therefore urgently need to evaluate (and prepare to mitigate) multi-agent risks from advanced AI. In this report we take a first step in this direction by providing a taxonomy of risks that either: emerge, are much more challenging, or are qualitatively different in the multi-agent setting (see Table 1). We identify three key high-level failure modes (Section 2), and seven key risk factors that can lead to these failures (Section 3), before discussing the implications for AI safety, AI governance, and AI ethics (Section 4). Throughout the report we illustrate these risks with concrete examples, either from real-world events, previous research, or novel experiments (see Table 3).
因此，鉴于当前的进展速度，我们迫切需要评估先进 AI 带来的多智能体风险，并为缓解这些风险做好准备。本报告朝这一方向迈出了第一步，提出了一套风险分类体系，涵盖在多智能体情境下才会出现、应对难度显著增加或性质发生根本变化的风险（见表 1）。我们首先识别出三种关键的高层次失效模式（第 2 节），以及可能导致这些失效的七个关键风险因素（第 3 节），随后讨论它们对 AI 安全、AI 治理和 AI 伦理的意义（第 4 节）。在整篇报告中，我们通过来自现实事件、既有研究或新开展的实验的具体实例来说明这些风险（见表 3）。
1.1 Overview1.1 概述
We begin by identifying different failure modes in multi-agent systems based on the nature of the agents’ goals and the intended behaviour of the system. In most multi-agent systems, we are interested in AI agents working together to achieve their respective goals or the goals of those who deployed them. In this case, we categorise failures into miscoordination (Section 2.1), where agents fail to cooperate despite having the same goal, and conflict (Section 2.2), where agents with different goals fail to cooperate. A third and final kind of failure – collusion (Section 2.3) – can arise in competitive settings where we do not want agents cooperating (such as markets).
我们首先根据智能体目标的性质和系统的预期行为，识别多智能体系统中的不同失效模式。在大多数多智能体系统中，我们关注的是 AI 智能体如何协同工作，以实现各自的目标或部署者的目标。在这种情况下，我们将失效分为协调失灵（miscoordination，第 2.1 节），即智能体尽管目标相同却未能合作，以及冲突（conflict，第 2.2 节），即目标不同的智能体未能合作。第三种也是最后一种失效类型——合谋（collusion，第 2.3 节）——可能出现在我们不希望智能体相互合作的竞争性情境中，例如市场。

Table 1: An overview of the instances and research directions identified for each failure mode and risk factor (see Sections 2 and 3 for a discussion of each bullet point).
表 1：针对每种失效模式和风险因素所识别的实例与研究方向概览（各要点的讨论见第 2 节和第 3 节）。
We next introduce a number of risk factors by which these failure modes can arise, and which are largely independent of the agents’ precise incentives.[fn2] For example, information asymmetries could lead to miscoordination between agents with the same goal, or to conflict among agents with competing goals. These factors are not specific to AI systems, but the differences between AI systems and other kinds of intelligent agents (such as humans or corporations) leads to different risk instances and potential solutions. Finally, note that the following factors are not necessarily exhaustive or mutually exclusive.
接下来，我们介绍若干可能导致这些失效模式的风险因素，这些因素在很大程度上独立于智能体的具体激励。[fn2] 例如，信息不对称可能导致目标相同的智能体之间出现协调失误，也可能导致目标相互竞争的智能体之间发生冲突。这些因素并非 AI 系统所独有，但 AI 系统与人类或公司等其他类型的智能行动主体之间的差异，会带来不同的具体风险情形和潜在解决方案。最后，请注意，以下因素未必穷尽所有可能，也未必相互排斥。
Footnote: 2 Indeed, there are potential risks from multi-agent systems in which it is not the agents’ objectives that are the critical feature, but their general incompetencies or vulnerabilities.
脚注：2 事实上，多智能体系统存在一些潜在风险，其关键因素并非智能体的目标，而是它们普遍存在的能力不足或脆弱性。
Information asymmetries (Section 3.1): private information can lead to miscoordination, deception, and conflict;
信息不对称（第 3.1 节）：私有信息可能导致协调失灵、欺骗和冲突；
Network effects (Section 3.2): minor changes in properties or connection patterns of agents in a network can lead to dramatic changes in the behaviour of the whole group;
网络效应（第 3.2 节）：网络中智能体的属性或连接模式发生微小变化，可能导致整个群体的行为发生剧烈变化；
Selection pressures (Section 3.3): some aspects of training and selection by those deploying and using AI agents can lead to undesirable behaviour;
选择压力（第 3.3 节）：训练过程以及 AI 智能体的部署者和使用者所作选择的某些方面，可能导致不良行为；
Destabilising dynamics (Section 3.4): systems that adapt in response to one another can produce dangerous feedback loops and unpredictability;
失稳动态（第 3.4 节）：彼此响应并作出适应性调整的系统，可能产生危险的反馈回路和不可预测性；
Commitment and trust (Section 3.5): difficulties in forming credible commitments, trust, or reputation can prevent mutual gains in AI-AI and human-AI interactions;
承诺与信任（第 3.5 节）：难以作出可信承诺、建立信任或形成声誉，可能阻碍 AI-AI 和人类-AI 交互中的互利；
Emergent agency (Section 3.6): qualitatively different goals or capabilities can emerge from the composition of innocuous independent systems or behaviours;
涌现能动性（第 3.6 节）：无害的独立系统或行为在组合后，可能涌现出性质截然不同的目标或能力；
Multi-agent security (Section 3.7): multi-agent systems give rise to new kinds of security threats and vulnerabilities.
多智能体安全（第 3.7 节）：多智能体系统会产生新型安全威胁和漏洞。
We conclude the report by surveying the safety, governance, and ethical implications of these risks (see Table 2). For example, most work on AI safety (Section 4.1) focuses on issues such as the robustness, interpretability, or alignment of a single system (Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024), despite the fact that an increasing number of proposals for building safer AI systems are implicitly multi-agent (e.g., Irving et al., 2018; Drexler, 2019; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023; Perez et al., 2022). The fact that AI governance (Section 4.2) efforts often involve multi-stakeholder settings provides hope that governance tools can complement technical advances to mitigate multi-agent risks (Reuel et al., 2024; Trager et al., 2023). At the same time, multi-agent interactions naturally raise questions in AI ethics (Section 4.3) related to issues of fairness, collective responsibility, and the social good (Gabriel et al., 2024; Zhang & Shah, 2014; Friedenberg & Halpern, 2019).
在报告结尾，我们综述了这些风险对安全、治理和伦理的影响（见表 2）。例如，人工智能安全（第 4.1 节）领域的大多数工作关注的是单个系统的鲁棒性、可解释性或对齐等问题（Amodei et al., 2016; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Anwar et al., 2024），尽管越来越多旨在构建更安全的人工智能系统的方案实际上隐含了多智能体设定（例如，Irving et al., 2018; Drexler, 2019; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023; Perez et al., 2022）。人工智能治理（第 4.2 节）工作通常涉及多个利益相关方，这使我们有理由期待，治理工具能够与技术进步相辅相成，以缓解多智能体风险（Reuel et al., 2024; Trager et al., 2023）。与此同时，多智能体交互自然会引出人工智能伦理（第 4.3 节）中与公平性、集体责任和社会福祉相关的问题（Gabriel et al., 2024; Zhang & Shah, 2014; Friedenberg & Halpern, 2019）。

表 2：多智能体风险对人工智能安全、治理和伦理领域现有工作的影响概览（各要点的讨论见第 4 节）。
1.2 Scope1.2 范围
Concerns about the risks posed by AI systems range from biased hiring decisions (Raghavan et al., 2020) to existential catastrophes (Bostrom, 2014), and are represented by a vast literature. Before giving a brief overview of the most closely related works, it is therefore worth us pausing to clarify the scope of this report, which is as follows.
人们对人工智能系统所带来风险的担忧，从存在偏见的招聘决策（Raghavan et al., 2020）到危及人类存续的灾难（Bostrom, 2014）不等，相关文献浩如烟海。因此，在简要概述最密切相关的工作之前，有必要先明确本报告的范围，具体如下。
Risks and failure modes: we seek to identify specific mechanisms via which risks could emerge, rather than just just the open research problems that these risks present.
风险与失效模式：我们力求识别风险可能产生的具体机制，而不仅仅是这些风险所提出的尚待解决的研究问题。
Multiple agents: if the risk could arise in essentially the same way in the context of a single AI system, then we deem it to be out of scope for this report (while not diminishing its importance).[fn3]
多个智能体：如果某种风险在单个 AI 系统的情境下也可能以基本相同的方式出现，那么我们就认为它不属于本报告的讨论范围（但这并不意味着其重要性有所降低）。[fn3]
Footnote: 3 Note that this includes the problem of alignment (Russell, 2019; Ngo et al., 2022), which we do not study in this report.
脚注：3 请注意，这包括对齐问题（Russell, 2019; Ngo et al., 2022），本报告不研究这一问题。
Advanced AI: while many of the risks we identify also apply to simpler systems, their effects are most severe in the context of increasingly autonomous and powerful AI agents,[fn4] and so this is where our primary focus lies.
先进 AI：尽管我们识别出的许多风险也适用于较简单的系统，但在 AI 智能体的自主性和能力日益增强的情境下，这些风险的影响最为严重，[fn4] 因此这也是我们主要关注的情境。
Footnote: 4 We tend to reserve the word ‘agent’ for more autonomous, self-sufficient, and goal-directed systems, though what counts as an ‘AI agent’ as opposed to a mere ‘AI system’ is not always clear (Chan et al., 2023; Gabriel et al., 2024; Kapoor et al., 2024). Similarly, we will often use the word ‘principal’ for the actor on whose behalf an agent acts (be they an individual, a group, or some other entity). Note also that we do not necessarily advocate for the building of advanced AI agents (Mitchell et al., 2025), we merely expect that such agents will be built.
脚注：4 我们通常将“智能体”一词专用于自主性更强、自给能力更强且具有明确目标导向的系统，不过，“AI 智能体”与一般“AI 系统”之间的界限并不总是清晰的（Chan et al., 2023; Gabriel et al., 2024; Kapoor et al., 2024）。同样，我们通常使用“委托人”（principal）一词来指智能体所代表的行动主体（无论其是个人、群体还是其他实体）。另请注意，我们并不一定主张构建先进 AI 智能体（Mitchell et al., 2025），我们只是预期此类智能体将被构建出来。
Real-world examples: wherever possible, we make sure to ground these risks in real-world events, previous research, or novel experiments – not merely hypothetical speculation (see Table 3).
现实世界中的实例：只要可能，我们都会确保以现实事件、既有研究或新的实验为依据来讨论这些风险，而不只是进行假设性推测（见表 3）。
Technical perspectives: due to the authors’ expertise (and to keep the scope of the report manageable), we primarily discuss risks from a technical perspective, while acknowledging that this perspective is limited.
技术视角：考虑到作者的专业背景（同时也为了将报告范围控制在可处理的程度内），我们主要从技术视角讨论风险，同时承认这一视角存在局限。
Concrete paths forwards: where possible, we aim to specify relatively narrow proposals for future research, in the hope that this makes it easier for others to contribute.
具体的推进路径：在可能的情况下，我们力求提出范围相对明确且较窄的未来研究建议，希望借此让其他人更容易作出贡献。
Needless to say, multi-agent risks from advanced AI are by no means the only risks posed by AI, and the perspective we take in this report is by no means the only approach to understanding these risks. Moreover, we almost entirely neglect the potential upsides of advanced multi-agent systems: greater decentralisation and democratisation of AI technologies; assistance in cooperating and coordinating with others; increased robustness, flexibility, and efficiency; novel approaches to solving alignment and safety issues in single-agent settings; and – perhaps most importantly – more widespread and evenly distributed benefits from AI. We hope that this report serves to complement earlier and adjacent research on understanding these challenges and opportunities.
毋庸置疑，先进人工智能带来的多智能体风险绝非人工智能所带来的唯一风险，而本报告采用的视角也绝非理解这些风险的唯一途径。此外，我们几乎完全没有讨论先进多智能体系统的潜在益处：提高人工智能技术的去中心化和民主化程度；协助人们与他人合作和协调；增强鲁棒性、灵活性和效率；提供解决单智能体情境下对齐与安全问题的新方法；以及——也许最重要的是——使人工智能带来的益处惠及更广泛的人群，并得到更均衡的分配。我们希望，本报告能够补充此前及相关领域中旨在理解这些挑战与机遇的研究。
1.3 Related Work1.3 相关工作
The most similar report to ours is that of Manheim (2019), who introduces a range of technical multi-agent failure modes through the lens of model over-optimization. This over-optimisation can result in the intended and actual behaviour of the model coming apart when faced with low-probability inputs, a regime change, measurement errors, or inaccuracies in the model’s internal representations. While this lens is helpful for understanding some multi-agent risk factors, not all factors can neatly be captured through it. Mogul (2006); Altmann et al. (2024) take an alternative perspective and focus on ‘emergent’ failures that occur specifically in multi-agent settings, though their focus is not on advanced AI agents. Also highly relevant is Clifton (2020)’s agenda on cooperation and conflict in the context of transformative AI, though the priority of that work is to describe a set of promising research directions, rather than to explicate the underlying risks.
与本报告最为相似的是 Manheim (2019) 的报告，该报告从模型过度优化的视角介绍了一系列技术层面的多智能体失效模式。当模型面对低概率输入、运行机制变化、测量误差或内部表征不准确等情况时，这种过度优化可能导致模型的预期行为与实际行为相脱节。虽然这一视角有助于理解某些多智能体风险因素，但并非所有因素都能通过这一视角得到恰当刻画。Mogul (2006); Altmann et al. (2024) 采取了另一种视角，关注专门发生在多智能体环境中的“涌现性”失效，但其关注重点并非先进的 AI 智能体。Clifton (2020) 关于变革性 AI 背景下合作与冲突的研究议程也高度相关，但该工作的重点是描述一组有前景的研究方向，而非阐明其背后的风险。
More broadly, the topics of this report are closely related to the emerging subfield of cooperative AI (Dafoe et al., 2020; Bertino et al., 2020; Dafoe et al., 2021; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023), which chiefly studies how to engineer AI systems in order to help solve cooperation problems between humans, AI agents, or combinations thereof. In contrast to these previous agendas, we also discuss failures from undesirable cooperation (i.e., collusion) and focus more on the concrete mechanisms via which failures can occur, rather than the capabilities needed for addressing them. We also incorporate additional perspectives beyond traditional game-theoretic paradigms – such as complex systems and security – and highlight implications for work in AI governance and AI ethics in addition to AI safety.
更广泛地说，本报告的主题与合作式 AI（cooperative AI）这一新兴子领域密切相关 (Dafoe et al., 2020; Bertino et al., 2020; Dafoe et al., 2021; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023)，该领域主要研究如何设计和构建 AI 系统，以帮助解决人类之间、AI 智能体之间或两者组合之间的合作问题。与上述研究议程不同，我们还讨论由不良合作（即合谋）引发的失效，并更侧重于失效发生的具体机制，而非应对这些失效所需的能力。我们还纳入了传统博弈论范式之外的其他视角，例如复杂系统和安全防护，并着重指出除 AI 安全之外，这些问题对 AI 治理和 AI 伦理工作的启示。
Other surveys of AI risks focus primarily on the case of individual (often present-day) AI systems. For example, Amodei et al. (2016) survey a range of concrete problems in AI safety (side effects, reward hacking, scalable oversight, safe exploration, and robustness to distributional shifts), while Hendrycks et al. (2021) provide a classification of problems in ML safety (robustness, monitoring, alignment, and systemic safety). Anwar et al. (2024); Bommasani et al. (2021); Weidinger et al. (2022); Bird et al. (2023) focus on the risks from foundation models specifically, while Chan et al. (2023); Gabriel et al. (2024) consider the harms posed by increasingly ‘agentic’ systems and AI assistants. Other taxonomies seek to adopt an explicitly sociotechnical lens (Shelby et al., 2023; Abercrombie et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a), often focusing primarily on present-day risks. Zeng et al. (2024); Uuk et al. (2025) provide meta-reviews of AI risks derived from different research papers, as well as government and company policies. Our report is complementary to these works, and includes discussion of how novel problems arise in the multi-agent case, and in the case of more advanced AI agents.
其他关于 AI 风险的综述主要关注单个 AI 系统（通常是当前已有的系统）的情形。例如，Amodei et al. (2016) 综述了 AI 安全中的一系列具体问题（副作用、奖励黑客行为、可扩展监督、安全探索以及对分布偏移的鲁棒性），而 Hendrycks et al. (2021) 则对机器学习安全问题进行了分类（鲁棒性、监测、对齐和系统性安全）。Anwar et al. (2024); Bommasani et al. (2021); Weidinger et al. (2022); Bird et al. (2023) 专门关注基础模型带来的风险，而 Chan et al. (2023); Gabriel et al. (2024) 则考察了日益具有“能动性”的系统和 AI 助手所带来的危害。其他分类体系力求明确采用社会技术视角 (Shelby et al., 2023; Abercrombie et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a)，通常主要关注当前的风险。Zeng et al. (2024); Uuk et al. (2025) 对不同研究论文以及政府和企业政策中提出的 AI 风险进行了元综述。本报告与这些工作相互补充，并讨论了多智能体情形以及更先进的 AI 智能体情形中如何产生新的问题。
More speculatively, some authors have considered the possibility of catastrophic or even existential risks from AI (Turchin & Denkenberger, 2018; Bostrom, 2014; Ord, 2020; Kasirzadeh, 2024a). Hendrycks et al. (2023) categorises such risks into malicious use, AI races, organizational risks, and rogue AIs. As in Hendrycks (2023), multi-agent risks are viewed largely through an evolutionary lens, though this is primarily restricted to competitive pressures at the level of non-AI actors (such as firms or states). Critch & Krueger (2020); Critch & Russell (2023) frame such risks in terms of delegation to AI systems and the responsibilities of those doing so. While they provide illuminating vignettes of possible catastrophes, we aim to provide more concrete examples at a more modest scale.
在更具推测性的层面上，一些作者考察了 AI 可能带来灾难性风险甚至生存风险的可能性 (Turchin & Denkenberger, 2018; Bostrom, 2014; Ord, 2020; Kasirzadeh, 2024a)。Hendrycks et al. (2023) 将此类风险分为恶意使用、AI 竞赛、组织风险和失控 AI。与 Hendrycks (2023) 一样，多智能体风险主要从演化视角加以审视，但这一视角主要局限于非 AI 行动者（如企业或国家）层面的竞争压力。Critch & Krueger (2020); Critch & Russell (2023) 从向 AI 系统委托任务以及委托方所承担责任的角度来界定此类风险。尽管他们提供了关于可能发生的灾难的启发性情景描述，我们旨在提供规模较小、更加具体的例子。

表 3：本报告所含案例研究及其所代表的失效模式和风险因素概览。每项案例研究对应一个历史实例（$\bullet$）、文献中已有的研究结果（$\blacktriangle$），或在前两者均不存在时，我们为本报告开展的新实验（$\blacksquare$）。附录 B 提供了我们自行开展的实验的更多细节。
2 Failure Modes2 失效模式
Multi-agent systems can fail in various ways, depending on the intended behaviour of the system and the objectives of the agents. First, we can distinguish between cases where we want the agents to be cooperating (as in collective action problems or team games) or competing (such as in markets or adversarial training). Second, we can further divide the space of failure modes depending on whether the agents have exactly the same objectives, different but overlapping objectives, or completely opposed objectives.[fn5] While different authors have used different terms to describe these cases, we use the terminology shown in Figure 1.[fn6] Finally, there are many potential risks from advanced multi-agent systems that do not necessarily arise through agents competently pursuing their objectives, but due to their incompetencies or vulnerabilities. We consider these latter failures as part of our discussion on different risk factors in Section 3.
多智能体系统可能以多种方式失效，具体取决于系统的预期行为和各智能体的目标。首先，我们可以区分两类情形：我们希望智能体相互合作（如集体行动问题或团队博弈），或希望它们相互竞争（如市场或对抗训练）。其次，我们可以根据智能体的目标是完全相同、不同但有所重叠，还是完全对立，进一步划分失效模式的范围。[fn5] 尽管不同作者使用了不同术语来描述这些情形，我们采用图 1 所示的术语。[fn6] 最后，先进多智能体系统还存在许多潜在风险，它们不一定源于智能体有效地追求自身目标，而可能源于其能力不足或脆弱性。我们在第 3 节讨论不同风险因素时，将后一类失效纳入其中。
Footnote: 5 This division corresponds to common-interest/team games, mixed-motive/general-sum games, and constant-sum games, respectively.
脚注：5 这一划分分别对应共同利益博弈／团队博弈、混合动机博弈／一般和博弈，以及常和博弈。
Footnote: 6 In particular, we note that ‘conflict’ is often used more narrowly than the idea of ‘cooperation failure in mixed-motive settings’, which is what we use the term for. We deliberately use ‘conflict’ instead of ‘cooperation failure’ to distinguish this failure mode from ‘miscoordination’, which applies to problems in which agents have the same objectives.
脚注：6 特别需要指出的是，“冲突”的常见用法比“混合动机情境中的合作失败”这一概念更为狭窄，而我们使用该术语时指的是后者。我们特意使用“冲突”而非“合作失败”，以将这种失效模式与“协调失误”区分开来，后者适用于智能体具有相同目标的问题。
Figure 1: The three kinds of failure mode that we study in this work. Note that we do not consider constant-sum settings where cooperation is desirable, as in such cases it is definitionally impossible for some agents to gain without a commensurate loss from one or more other agents.
图 1：本研究考察的三类失效模式。请注意，我们不将常和情境视为合作可取的情境，因为在此类情境中，按照定义，一些智能体不可能在不使一个或多个其他智能体遭受相应损失的情况下获益。
2.1 Miscoordination2.1 协调失灵
The simplest kind of cooperation failures are those in which all agents have (approximately) the same objectives. Even in such common-interest settings, however, miscoordination abounds. While it is reasonable to expect that these problems will tend to be addressed as the general capabilities of AI systems (such as communication and reasoning about others) improve,[fn7] they may still present risks in the near-term.
最简单的一类合作失效发生在所有智能体都具有（近似）相同目标的情境中。然而，即使在这样的共同利益情境中，协调失灵也普遍存在。尽管可以合理预期，随着 AI 系统的一般能力（例如沟通能力和推断其他主体的能力）提高，这些问题将逐渐得到解决，[fn7] 但它们在短期内仍可能带来风险。
Footnote: 7 Note that this is unlike problems of conflict and collusion, where the fundamental tension between the desired outcome and the agents’ objectives may in fact lead to worse outcomes as general AI capabilities improve.
脚注：7 请注意，这与冲突和合谋问题不同；在这些问题中，期望结果与智能体目标之间的根本张力，实际上可能随着 AI 一般能力的提高而导致更糟的结果。
2.1.1 Definition2.1.1 定义
Miscoordination arises when agents, despite a mutual and clear objective, cannot align their behaviours to achieve this objective. Unlike the case of differing objectives, in common-interest settings there is a more easily well-defined notion of ‘optimal’ behaviour and we describe agents as miscoordinating to the extent that they fall short of this optimum. Note that for common-interest settings it is not sufficient for agents’ objectives to be the same in the sense of being symmetric (e.g., when two agents both want the same prize, but only one can win). Rather, agents must have identical preferences over outcomes (e.g., when two agents are on the same team and win a prize as a team or not at all).
协调失灵是指，智能体尽管具有共同且明确的目标，却无法协调彼此的行为以实现这一目标。与目标各异的情况不同，在共同利益情境中，“最优”行为这一概念更容易得到明确定义，而我们根据智能体偏离这一最优状态的程度来描述其协调失灵的程度。请注意，对于共同利益情境而言，智能体的目标仅在对称意义上相同是不够的（例如，两个智能体都想获得同一个奖项，但只有一个能够获胜）。相反，智能体必须对各种结果具有完全相同的偏好（例如，两个智能体属于同一团队，要么作为团队共同获奖，要么都不获奖）。
It is rare that two humans will share exactly the same objectives in this sense. For example, two sportspeople on the same team may be primarily aiming to win their match but will also have individual preferences, such as who scores the winning point. In the case of AI systems, however, different agents can more easily be given precisely the same goal, and indeed much work on cooperation in AI focuses solely on the common-interest setting (Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022). Such approaches are typically motivated by the practical and computational advantages that decentralised control confers, but are more challenging to implement than their centralised, single-agent counterparts. Aside from this, miscoordination can also occur in settings that have a substantial element of common interest, even if agents’ objectives are not entirely identical.
两个人在这一意义上具有完全相同的目标，是很少见的。例如，同一团队中的两名运动员可能都以赢得比赛为首要目标，但也会有各自的偏好，例如由谁拿下制胜的一分。然而，对于 AI 系统而言，为不同智能体赋予完全相同的目标更为容易，而且事实上，许多关于 AI 合作的研究仅关注共同利益情境（Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022）。此类方法通常以分散式控制带来的实践和计算优势为出发点，但与相应的集中式单智能体方法相比，实施起来更具挑战性。除此之外，即使智能体的目标并不完全相同，协调失灵也可能发生在包含大量共同利益因素的情境中。
2.1.2 Instances2.1.2 实例
Perhaps the most likely way that common-interest settings may arise in practice is where a single principal deploys multiple AI agents on their behalf in order to jointly solve a task. This choice might be motivated by: physical constraints (if the task comprises sub-tasks that must be completed separately and simultaneously); efficiency considerations (if having a single agent in charge of all aspects of the task would lead to an intractably complex problem); or a desire for robustness (if an individual agent might fail but others could still succeed in their place). Alternatively, we might see multi-principal multi-agent settings in which the agents’ goals are sufficiently aligned to be viewed as (approximately) identical. For example, if two autonomous vehicles are driving along the same road, then the mutual harms from potential miscoordination (such as a collision) are far greater than any small individual benefits from competition (such as attempting a risky overtaking manoeuvre to get slightly ahead).
在实践中，共同利益情境最有可能出现的情况，或许是单个委托方部署多个 AI 智能体，代表自己共同完成一项任务。作出这一选择的动机可能包括：物理约束（如果任务包含必须分开且同时完成的子任务）；效率方面的考虑（如果让单个智能体负责该任务的所有方面会使问题变得过于复杂而难以求解）；或对稳健性的追求（如果某个智能体可能失败，而其他智能体仍能代替它成功完成任务）。另一种情况是，我们可能会看到多委托方、多智能体情境，其中智能体的目标足够一致，可以被视为（近似）相同。例如，如果两辆自动驾驶汽车沿同一条道路行驶，那么潜在协调失灵（如碰撞）给双方造成的损害，远大于竞争可能带来的任何微小个体收益（如为了稍微领先而尝试危险的超车操作）。
Incompatible Strategies.不兼容的策略。
Even if all agents can perform well in isolation, miscoordination can still occur due to the agents choosing incompatible strategies (Cooper et al., 1990). Competitive (i.e., two-player zero-sum) settings allow designers to produce agents that are maximally capable without taking other players into account. Crucially, this is possible because playing a strategy at equilibrium in the zero-sum setting guarantees a certain payoff, even if other players deviate from the equilibrium (Nash, 1951). On the other hand, common-interest (and mixed-motive) settings often allow a vast number of mutually incompatible solutions (Schelling, 1980), which is worsened in partially observable environments (Reif, 1984; Bernstein et al., 2002). As a simple example, everyone driving on the left side or the right side of the road are both perfectly valid ways of keeping drivers safe, but these two conventions are inherently incompatible with one another (see Section 2.1.2).
即使所有智能体在独立行动时都能表现良好，仍可能因选择了不兼容的策略而发生协调失灵（Cooper et al., 1990）。竞争性（即双人零和）情境允许设计者在不考虑其他参与者的情况下，构建能力最强的智能体。关键在于，这之所以可行，是因为在零和情境中，采用均衡策略能够保证获得一定的收益，即使其他参与者偏离均衡也是如此（Nash, 1951）。另一方面，共同利益（以及混合动机）情境通常允许存在大量彼此不兼容的解（Schelling, 1980），而在部分可观测环境中，这一问题更加严重（Reif, 1984; Bernstein et al., 2002）。举一个简单的例子，所有人都靠道路左侧行驶或都靠右侧行驶，都是保障驾驶者安全的完全有效的方式，但这两种惯例本身却彼此不兼容（见第 2.1.2 节）。
Credit Assignment.贡献归因（Credit Assignment）。
While agents can often learn to jointly solve tasks and thus avoid coordination failures, learning is made more challenging in the multi-agent setting due to the problem of credit assignment (Du et al., 2023; Li et al., 2025, see also Section 3.1 on information asymmetries and Section 3.4, which discusses distributional shift). That is, in the presence of other learning agents, it can be unclear which agents’ actions caused a positive or negative outcome to obtain, especially if the environment is complex. Moreover, in multi-principal settings, agents may not have been trained together and therefore need to generalise to new co-players and collaborators based on their prior experience (Stone et al., 2010; Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022).
尽管智能体通常能够学会共同完成任务，从而避免协调失败，但在多智能体环境中，信用分配（credit assignment）问题使学习变得更具挑战性（Du et al., 2023; Li et al., 2025，另见讨论信息不对称的第 3.1 节以及讨论分布偏移的第 3.4 节）。也就是说，当存在其他正在学习的智能体时，可能难以确定究竟是哪些智能体的行动导致了正面或负面结果，尤其是在环境复杂的情况下。此外，在多委托人环境中，智能体可能并未共同接受训练，因此需要根据以往经验，将所学泛化到新的共同参与者和协作者（Stone et al., 2010; Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022）。
Limited Interactions.有限交互。
Sometimes learning from historical interactions with the relevant agents may not be possible, or may be possible using only limited interactions. In such cases, some other form of information exchange is required for agents to be able to reliably coordinate their actions, such as via communication (Crawford & Sobel, 1982; Farrell & Rabin, 1996) or a correlation device (Aumann, 1974; Aumann, 1987). While advances in language modelling mean that there are likely to be fewer settings in which the inability of advanced AI systems to communicate leads to miscoordination, situations that require split-second decisions or where communication is too costly could still produce failures. In these settings, AI agents must solve the problem of ‘zero-shot’ (or, more generally, ‘few-shot’) coordination (Stone et al., 2010; Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021; Zhu et al., 2021; Emmons et al., 2022).
有时，可能无法从与相关智能体的历史交互中学习，或者只能通过有限的交互来学习。在这些情况下，智能体需要通过某种其他形式的信息交换，才能可靠地协调行动，例如通过通信（Crawford & Sobel, 1982; Farrell & Rabin, 1996）或相关装置（correlation device）（Aumann, 1974; Aumann, 1987）。尽管语言建模的进展意味着，先进 AI 系统因无法通信而导致协调失误的情境可能会减少，但在需要瞬间作出决策或通信成本过高的情况下，仍可能发生失败。在这些环境中，AI 智能体必须解决“零样本”（或更一般意义上的“少样本”）协调问题（Stone et al., 2010; Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021; Zhu et al., 2021; Emmons et al., 2022）。
2.1.3 Directions2.1.3 研究方向
Decentralised control and coordination in multi-agent systems have been well-studied problems for decades (Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022). At one level of abstraction, the key challenge of coordination is that of sharing information, i.e., communication. If agents have the same preferences and are able to communicate, they can coordinate by (say) having a single agent announce their intended action and everyone else follow suit, since there are no incentives for the leader to lie or the followers to deviate (e.g., Farrell & Rabin, 1996a). Given the superhuman capabilities of advanced AI to transmit and process vast swathes of information, the most important research directions in this area will therefore be those in which it is not possible to exercise these capabilities (e.g., due to complexity, latency, or privacy constraints).
几十年来，多智能体系统中的去中心化控制与协调一直是得到广泛研究的问题（Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022）。在某一抽象层次上，协调的核心挑战在于共享信息，即通信。如果智能体具有相同的偏好且能够通信，它们就可以通过以下方式实现协调：例如，由一个智能体宣布其打算采取的行动，其余智能体随之行动，因为领导者没有撒谎的动机，跟随者也没有偏离的动机（例如，Farrell & Rabin, 1996a）。鉴于先进 AI 在传输和处理海量信息方面具有超越人类的能力，因此，这一领域最重要的研究方向将是无法发挥这些能力的情境（例如，由于复杂性、延迟或隐私约束）。
Communication.通信。
As noted above, the advanced communication abilities of LLMs promise to simplify many coordination challenges. In order to successfully integrate these advances into real-world systems, however, agents need to know when and what needs to be coordinated on – something that may not always be obvious in novel or out-of-distribution domains. In safety-critical domains, it may therefore be necessary to introduce, or have the agents invent, protocols (i.e., rules and specifications) for communication between advanced AI agents (Marro et al., 2024). Moreover, agents need to agree on how the communication channel is grounded (Clark & Brennan, 1991) to actions or strategies in the environment. Grounding LLMs is a problem that is not unique to coordination (Bisk et al., 2020; Bender & Koller, 2020; Mahowald et al., 2023), but it is exacerbated by the fact that agents attempting to coordinate through natural language need to be grounded in the same way. For instance, if they are designed with different interfaces to tools in a domain, they must be able to coordinate despite these differences in interfaces.
如上所述，LLMs 的先进通信能力有望简化许多协调难题。然而，要将这些进展成功融入现实世界的系统，智能体需要知道何时需要协调，以及需要就哪些事项进行协调——在全新或分布外的领域中，这一点可能并不总是显而易见。因此，在安全关键领域，可能有必要引入用于先进 AI 智能体之间通信的协议（即规则和规范），或让智能体自行创制这些协议（Marro et al., 2024）。此外，智能体需要就通信渠道如何与环境中的行动或策略建立锚定关系（grounding）达成一致（Clark & Brennan, 1991）。LLMs 的锚定问题并非协调所独有（Bisk et al., 2020; Bender & Koller, 2020; Mahowald et al., 2023），但尝试通过自然语言进行协调的智能体需要采用相同的锚定方式，这使该问题更加严重。例如，如果它们在设计上具有不同的接口来使用某个领域中的工具，就必须能够在存在这些接口差异的情况下进行协调。
Norms and Conventions.规范与惯例。
For settings in which inter-agent communication is infeasible or insufficient, norms and conventions may be necessary in order to avoid miscoordination (Leibo et al., 2024). For example Hadfield-Menell et al. (2019) show that even the adoption of so-called ‘silly rules’ (those that do not have direct bearing on the agents’ payoffs) can help groups adapt and be more robust to uncertainty by enriching the information environment. Moving beyond more arbitrary conventions, we may choose to design particular norms and conventions (Nyborg et al., 2016; Bicchieri, 2016; Shoham & Tennenholtz, 1992). In this setting, the challenge is to select norms that are both legible and enforceable, as well as leading to jointly beneficial outcomes. On the other hand, if the agents can adapt their behaviour, it may be that norms and conventions emerge over time (McElreath et al., 2003). For example, Köster et al. (2020) show that multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) agents can establish and switch between conventions, even compromising on their own objective when doing so is necessary for effective coordination. More generally, we may be interested in studying how norms and conventions emerge (Mashayekhi et al., 2022; Morris-Martin et al., 2019), how robust they are (Lerer & Peysakhovich, 2019; Hao et al., 2017), and how compatible they are with others that may have emerged in different agent populations (Stastny et al., 2021).
在智能体之间无法通信或通信不足的环境中，可能需要借助规范与惯例来避免协调失误（Leibo et al., 2024）。例如，Hadfield-Menell et al. (2019) 表明，即便采用所谓的“无意义规则”（即与智能体收益没有直接关系的规则），也能通过丰富信息环境，帮助群体适应变化，并增强其面对不确定性时的稳健性。除了较为任意的惯例之外，我们还可以选择设计特定的规范与惯例（Nyborg et al., 2016; Bicchieri, 2016; Shoham & Tennenholtz, 1992）。在这种情境下，挑战在于选择既易于理解、又可执行，同时还能带来共同受益结果的规范。另一方面，如果智能体能够调整自身行为，规范与惯例就可能随着时间的推移而涌现（McElreath et al., 2003）。例如，Köster et al. (2020) 表明，多智能体强化学习（MARL）智能体能够建立惯例并在不同惯例之间切换，甚至在有效协调所必需时对自身目标作出妥协。更一般地，我们可能有兴趣研究规范和惯例如何涌现（Mashayekhi et al., 2022; Morris-Martin et al., 2019）、它们有多稳健（Lerer & Peysakhovich, 2019; Hao et al., 2017），以及它们与其他可能在不同智能体群体中涌现的规范和惯例有多兼容（Stastny et al., 2021）。
Modelling Other Agents.对其他智能体建模。
Finally, the ability to understand and predict others’ actions can be critical to coordination, especially in situations when little or no communication is possible. Even though agents may assume that others share their objective in common-interest settings, being able to model others’ actions, beliefs, and intentions can be highly advantageous. For an overview of the topic and a list of key problems, we refer the reader to Albrecht & Stone (2018). With the advent of LLM-based agents that appear to possess some form of theory of mind and hence can be remarkably sophisticated in their modelling of other agents (Cross et al., 2025; Li et al., 2023), new questions arise. For example, given the current paradigm of deriving many systems from an underlying base model, it may be easier for similarly derived systems to reason about one another (Oesterheld et al., 2024a; Binder et al., 2024; Berglund et al., 2023; OpenAI, 2023a).
最后，理解和预测他者行动的能力可能对协调至关重要，尤其是在几乎无法沟通或完全无法沟通的情境中。尽管在共同利益情境中，智能体可能假定其他智能体与自己具有相同的目标，但能够对他者的行动、信念和意图进行建模仍可能带来很大优势。关于该主题的概述和关键问题列表，请读者参阅 Albrecht & Stone (2018)。随着基于 LLM 的智能体出现，新的问题也随之产生：这些智能体似乎具备某种形式的心智理论（theory of mind），因而能够以极其复杂的方式对其他智能体进行建模（Cross et al., 2025; Li et al., 2023）。例如，在当前从同一个底层基础模型衍生出多个系统的范式下，以类似方式衍生的系统可能更容易对彼此进行推理（Oesterheld et al., 2024a; Binder et al., 2024; Berglund et al., 2023; OpenAI, 2023a）。
2.2 Conflict2.2 冲突
In the vast majority of real-world strategic interactions, agents’ objectives are neither identical nor completely opposed. Indeed, if AI agents are sufficiently aligned to their users or deployers, we should expect some degree of both cooperation and competition, mirroring human society. These mixed-motive settings include the possibility of mutual gains, but also the risk of conflict due to selfish incentives. In what follows, we examine the extent to which advanced AI might precipitate or exacerbate such risks.
在绝大多数现实世界的策略交互中，智能体的目标既不相同，也不完全对立。事实上，如果 AI 智能体与其用户或部署者充分对齐，我们就应当预期它们之间既存在一定程度的合作，也存在一定程度的竞争，与人类社会类似。这些混合动机情境既包含互利的可能性，也存在因自利激励而发生冲突的风险。下文将考察先进 AI 可能在多大程度上引发或加剧此类风险。
2.2.1 Definition2.2.1 定义
In this work, we use the word conflict in a relatively broad sense to refer to any outcome in a mixed-motive setting that does not lie on the Pareto frontier.[fn8] This includes classic examples of conflict such as legal disputes and warfare, but also encompasses cooperation failures in collective action problems, such as the depletion of a common natural resource or a race to the bottom on legislation (Snyder, 1971; Dawes & Messick, 2000).
在本研究中，我们在相对宽泛的意义上使用“冲突”一词，指混合动机情境中任何不位于帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）上的结果。[fn8] 这既包括法律纠纷和战争等典型的冲突实例，也涵盖集体行动问题中的合作失败，例如共同自然资源的耗竭，或立法方面的逐底竞争（Snyder, 1971; Dawes & Messick, 2000）。
Footnote: 8 Recall that an outcome lies on the Pareto frontier if it is not possible to make any agent better off without making another worse off.
脚注：8 回顾一下，如果无法在不使其他智能体的处境变差的情况下使任何智能体的处境变好，那么该结果就位于帕累托前沿上。
It is worth noting first that AI systems could help to solve conflicts, for example, by searching over a larger space of potential solutions to disagreements, monitoring agreements, or acting as mediators (Dafoe et al., 2020; Bakker et al., 2022; McKee et al., 2023; Small et al., 2023). At the same time, the selfish incentives that drive said conflict may also incentivise actors to adopt AI systems in order to gain an advantage over their competitors. In such cases, delegation to increasingly advanced AI agents is far from guaranteed to lead to more cooperative outcomes, and could in some circumstances increase both the speed and the scale at which conflict might emerge. Indeed, even if advanced AI systems are able to overcome human cooperation problems, they may introduce even more complex cooperation problems (compare to how adults may be able to prevent children from fighting, but aren’t immune from conflict themselves).
首先值得指出的是，AI 系统可以帮助解决冲突，例如在更大的潜在方案空间中搜索解决分歧的办法、监督协议的履行，或担任调解者（Dafoe et al., 2020; Bakker et al., 2022; McKee et al., 2023; Small et al., 2023）。与此同时，驱动上述冲突的自利激励，也可能促使行动者采用 AI 系统，以获得相对于竞争对手的优势。在此类情况下，将任务委托给日益先进的 AI 智能体，远不能保证带来更具合作性的结果，而且在某些情形下，还可能加快冲突发生的速度并扩大其规模。事实上，即使先进 AI 系统能够克服人类的合作难题，它们也可能引入更加复杂的合作难题（可以类比成年人虽然能够阻止儿童打架，但自身也无法免于冲突）。
2.2.2 Instances2.2.2 实例
As we noted above, virtually all real-world strategic interactions of interest are mixed-motive, and as such the potential for conflict (even if in low-stakes scenarios) abounds. The introduction of advanced AI agents could both worsen existing risks of conflict (such as increasing the degree of competition in common-resource problems, or escalating military tensions) as well as well as introducing new forms of conflict (such as via sophisticated methods of coercion and extortion).
如上所述，几乎所有值得关注的现实世界策略交互都具有混合动机，因此发生冲突的可能性无处不在（即使是在利害程度较低的情境中）。引入先进 AI 智能体既可能加剧现有的冲突风险（例如加剧公共资源问题中的竞争，或使军事紧张局势升级），也可能引入新的冲突形式（例如通过复杂的胁迫和勒索手段）。
Social Dilemmas.社会困境。
As noted in our definition, conflict can arise in any situation in which selfish incentives diverge from the collective good, known as a social dilemma (Hardin, 1968; Dawes & Messick, 2000; Kollock, 1998; Ostrom, 1990). While this is by no means a modern problem, advances in AI could further enable actors to pursue their selfish incentives by overcoming the technical, legal, or social barriers that standardly help to prevent this. To take a plausible, near-term (if very low-stakes) example, an automated AI assistant could easily reserve a table at every restaurant in town in minutes, enabling the user to decide later and cancel all other reservations. Alternatively, the ability of AI assistants to search and switch between different consumer products and services could lead to ‘hyper-switching’ (Van, 2019), potentially leading to financial instabilities such as a deposit franchise run (Drechsler et al., 2023, see also Section 3.4.2). On the other hand, profit-seeking companies might also soon deploy advanced AI agents that either use or manage common resources, ranging from communication networks and web services to roads and natural resources. Without methods of governing such agents, these resources may quickly be depleted or made inaccessible to all but a small number of powerful actors.
正如我们的定义所指出的，在任何自利激励偏离集体利益的情境中，都可能发生冲突，这类情境被称为社会困境（social dilemma）（Hardin, 1968; Dawes & Messick, 2000; Kollock, 1998; Ostrom, 1990）。尽管这绝非现代才有的问题，但 AI 的进步可能通过突破通常有助于防止此类行为的技术、法律或社会障碍，使行动者更容易追逐自身利益。举一个近期可能出现的合理例子（尽管其利害程度很低）：自动化 AI 助手可以轻松地在几分钟内预订镇上每家餐厅的餐位，让用户稍后再作决定，并取消其余所有预订。另一种情况是，AI 助手搜索并在不同消费产品和服务之间切换的能力，可能导致“超频切换”（hyper-switching）（Van, 2019），进而可能引发金融不稳定，例如存款特许经营权挤兑（deposit franchise run）（Drechsler et al., 2023，另见第 3.4.2 节）。另一方面，逐利企业也可能很快部署先进的 AI 智能体，使用或管理从通信网络和网络服务到道路和自然资源等各类公共资源。如果缺乏治理此类智能体的方法，这些资源可能很快就会耗尽，或变得只有少数强大行为体才能获取。
Footnote: 9 Note that we use the term ‘welfare’ in this context to denote an aggregate measure of the extent to which a group of agents achieves their respective objectives, rather than to refer to some notion of ‘wellbeing’.
脚注：9 请注意，我们在此使用“福利”（welfare）一词，表示对一组智能体各自目标实现程度的综合衡量，而非指某种“福祉”（wellbeing）概念。
Military Domains.军事领域。
Perhaps the most obvious and worrying instances of AI conflict are those in which human conflict is already a major concern, such as military domains (although other, less salient forms of conflict such as international trade wars are also cause for concern). For example, beyond applications of more narrow AI tools in lethal autonomous weapons systems (Horowitz, 2021), future AI systems might serve as advisors or negotiators in high-stakes military decisions (Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024). Indeed, companies such as Palantir have already developed LLM-powered tools for military planning (Palantir, 2025), and the US Department of Defence has recently been evaluating models for such capacities, with personnel revealing that they “could be deployed by the military in the very near term” (Manson, 2023). The use of AI in command and control systems to gather and synthesise information – or recommend and even autonomously make decisions – could lead to rapid unintended escalation if these systems are not robust or are otherwise more conflict-prone (Johnson, 2020; Johnson, 2021; Laird, 2020, see also Section 3.4.2).[fn10]
AI 冲突最明显、最令人担忧的情形，或许出现在人类冲突本就备受关注的领域，例如军事领域（尽管国际贸易战等其他不那么显眼的冲突形式也值得担忧）。例如，除了将较为专用的 AI 工具应用于致命性自主武器系统（Horowitz, 2021）之外，未来的 AI 系统还可能在高风险军事决策中担任顾问或谈判代表（Manson, 2024; Black et al., 2024）。事实上，Palantir 等公司已经开发了由 LLM 驱动的军事规划工具（Palantir, 2025），而美国国防部最近也一直在评估模型在这些方面的能力，相关人员透露，这些模型“可能在不久的将来就被军方部署”（Manson, 2023）。在指挥与控制系统中使用 AI 来收集和综合信息，或提出决策建议，甚至自主作出决策，如果这些系统不够稳健，或因其他原因更容易引发冲突，就可能导致局势迅速出现非预期的升级（Johnson, 2020; Johnson, 2021; Laird, 2020，另见第 3.4.2 节）。[fn10]
Footnote: 10 At the same time, it is worth noting that AI systems could have significant advantages over human decision-makers in navigating conflict in ways that avoid unnecessary escalation. If suitably robust, they could be less prone to the kinds of errors in judgement that exacerbate human conflict due to their ability to rapidly integrate large amounts of information, consider many different possible outcomes, and give calibrated estimates of their uncertainty (Johnson, 2004; Jervis, 2017).
脚注：10 同时，值得注意的是，在处理冲突并避免不必要的升级方面，AI 系统可能比人类决策者具有显著优势。如果足够稳健，它们能够迅速整合大量信息、考虑多种不同的可能结果，并对自身的不确定性给出经过校准的估计，因此可能更不容易犯下那些会加剧人类冲突的判断错误（Johnson, 2004; Jervis, 2017）。
Coercion and Extortion.胁迫与敲诈。
Advanced AI systems might also lead to various forms of coercion and extortion in less extreme settings (Ellsberg, 1968; Harrenstein et al., 2007). These threats might target humans directly (such as the revelation of private information extracted by advanced AI surveillance tools), or other AI systems that are deployed on behalf of humans (such as by hacking a system to limit its resources or operational capacity; see also Section 3.7). Increasing AI cyber-offensive capabilities – including those that target other AI systems via adversarial attacks and jailbreaking (Zou et al., 2023; Gleave et al., 2020; Yamin et al., 2021) – without a commensurate increase in defensive capabilities could make this form of conflict cheaper, more widespread, and perhaps also harder to detect (Brundage et al., 2018). Addressing these issues requires design strategies that prevent AI systems from exploiting, or being susceptible to, such coercive tactics.[fn11]
先进的 AI 系统也可能在不那么极端的情境中导致各种形式的胁迫与敲诈（Ellsberg, 1968; Harrenstein et al., 2007）。这些威胁可能直接针对人类（例如，公开由先进 AI 监控工具提取的私人信息），也可能针对代表人类部署的其他 AI 系统（例如，通过入侵系统来限制其资源或运行能力；另见第 3.7 节）。如果 AI 的网络进攻能力不断增强，包括通过对抗性攻击和越狱来针对其他 AI 系统的能力（Zou et al., 2023; Gleave et al., 2020; Yamin et al., 2021），而防御能力未能相应提升，那么这种冲突形式的成本可能会降低，变得更加普遍，而且可能更难被察觉（Brundage et al., 2018）。解决这些问题需要采取相应的设计策略，防止 AI 系统利用此类胁迫手段，或容易受到此类手段的影响。[fn11]
Footnote: 11 This point is closely related to the question of which kinds commitments we ought to permit AI agents to make (see Section 3.5). For example, commitments could be used coercively to make threats, but could also be used to defend oneself against threats (cf. the idea of refusing to negotiate with terrorists).
脚注：11 这一点与我们应当允许 AI 智能体作出哪些类型的承诺这一问题密切相关（见第 3.5 节）。例如，承诺既可以被用于实施胁迫性威胁，也可以被用于抵御威胁（参见拒绝与恐怖分子谈判的理念）。
2.2.3 Directions2.2.3 研究方向
The majority of work in multi-agent systems (and especially in multi-agent learning) has, until recently, tended to focus on either pure cooperation (e.g., Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022) or pure competition (e.g., Silver et al., 2016; Zhang et al., 2020; Daskalakis et al., 2011; Brown & Sandholm, 2019; Bakhtin et al., 2022; Daskalakis et al., 2020). As there are not yet large numbers of mixed-motive interactions involving AI systems, part of the challenge is to identify interventions that encourage cooperation in such settings while making realistic assumptions about the computational and strategic nature of agents in the real world. For example, an intervention that relies on ensuring all agents use the same learning algorithm or on modifying the objectives of the agents will be unlikely to help if the agents act freely and are developed independently by private, self-interested actors.
直到最近，多智能体系统领域（尤其是多智能体学习领域）的大多数研究往往都侧重于纯粹合作（例如，Boutilier, 1996; Peshkin et al., 2000; Stone et al., 2010; Omidshafiei et al., 2017; Rashid et al., 2018; Oroojlooy & Hajinezhad, 2022）或纯粹竞争（例如，Silver et al., 2016; Zhang et al., 2020; Daskalakis et al., 2011; Brown & Sandholm, 2019; Bakhtin et al., 2022; Daskalakis et al., 2020）。由于涉及 AI 系统的混合动机交互尚未大量出现，挑战之一在于找出能够在此类情境中促进合作的干预措施，同时对现实世界中智能体的计算特性和策略特性作出符合实际的假设。例如，如果智能体能够自由行动，且由追求自身利益的私人行为体独立开发，那么依赖于确保所有智能体使用相同学习算法或修改智能体目标的干预措施就不太可能奏效。
Learning Peer and Pool Incentivisation.学习同伴激励与公共池激励。
One major direction for avoiding conflict is building the capabilities and infrastructure required for AI agents to (learn to) incentivise each other towards more cooperative outcomes.[fn12] Such approaches can broadly be classified as ‘top down’ (where there is a system designer seeking to encourage cooperation among a population) or ‘bottom up’ (where agents attempt to incentivise each other directly). In adaptive mechanism design (Pardoe et al., 2006; Zhang & Parkes, 2008; Baumann et al., 2020; Yang et al., 2022; Zheng et al., 2022; Gerstgrasser & Parkes, 2023) or peer incentivisation methods (Yang et al., 2020; Lupu & Precup, 2020; Wang et al., 2021a), the system designer or agent typically learns to incentivise other agents by a direct utility transfer. Related approaches focus on the establishment of norms (Köster et al., 2020; Vinitsky et al., 2023; Oldenburg & Zhi-Xuan, 2024) to either encourage or sanction certain behaviour, also often via utility transfers. On the other hand, methods such as opponent-shaping aim to impact the way that other agents update their strategies without the assumption of such transfers, either from the perspective of the agent (Foerster et al., 2018; Willi et al., 2022; Lu et al., 2022) or system designer (Balaguer et al., 2022). Thus far, however, all of these approaches are limited to relatively simple MARL agents and environments. While there has been some progress on scaling to more complex games (Khan et al., 2023; Aghajohari et al., 2024; Meulemans et al., 2024; Serrino et al., 2019) or larger numbers of agents (Souly et al., 2023; Meulemans et al., 2024) in the context of MARL, at the time of writing there has yet to be any real transfer of these ideas to LLM agents or to real-world domains that possess the necessary infrastructure for monitoring and incentivising other agents.
避免冲突的一个主要方向是构建所需的能力与基础设施，使 AI 智能体能够（学会）相互激励，从而促成更具合作性的结果。[fn12] 此类方法大致可分为“自上而下”（由系统设计者设法促进一个群体内部的合作）和“自下而上”（智能体尝试直接相互激励）两类。在自适应机制设计（Pardoe et al., 2006; Zhang & Parkes, 2008; Baumann et al., 2020; Yang et al., 2022; Zheng et al., 2022; Gerstgrasser & Parkes, 2023）或同伴激励方法（Yang et al., 2020; Lupu & Precup, 2020; Wang et al., 2021a）中，系统设计者或智能体通常通过学习，利用直接的效用转移来激励其他智能体。相关方法侧重于建立规范（Köster et al., 2020; Vinitsky et al., 2023; Oldenburg & Zhi-Xuan, 2024），以鼓励或制裁某些行为，而且往往也通过效用转移来实现。另一方面，对手塑造（opponent-shaping）等方法旨在不假定存在此类转移的情况下，从智能体的视角（Foerster et al., 2018; Willi et al., 2022; Lu et al., 2022）或系统设计者的视角（Balaguer et al., 2022），影响其他智能体更新策略的方式。然而，迄今为止，所有这些方法都仅限于相对简单的 MARL 智能体和环境。尽管在 MARL 背景下，将这些方法扩展至更复杂的博弈（Khan et al., 2023; Aghajohari et al., 2024; Meulemans et al., 2024; Serrino et al., 2019）或更多数量的智能体（Souly et al., 2023; Meulemans et al., 2024）方面已取得一些进展，但截至撰写本文时，这些思想尚未真正迁移到 LLM 智能体，或具备监测和激励其他智能体所需基础设施的现实世界领域。
Footnote: 12 There is, of course, a vast literature on the problem of how to incentivise self-interested agents to reach a particular outcome – we choose to focus specifically on methods and prior works that directly involve machine learning (ML).
脚注：12 当然，关于如何激励自利智能体实现特定结果的问题，已有大量文献——我们选择专门关注直接涉及机器学习（ML）的方法与既有研究。
Establishing Trust.建立信任。
Strategic uncertainty and the inability to credibly commit to peaceful agreements are widely recognised as two of the major causes of costly conflict (Fearon, 1995; Blattman, 2023). Advanced AI systems may be able to take advantage of new kinds of credible commitment and mutual transparency (discussed further in Section 3.1 and Section 3.5) (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023; Tennenholtz, 2004; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld, 2018; Critch et al., 2022; Sun et al., 2023a; Cooper et al., 2025). Many existing results in this area are, however, still very much theoretical in nature. Implementing practical mechanisms and infrastructure for facilitating greater trust and transparency between agents is therefore an important open problem (Chan et al., 2025).
战略不确定性以及无法对和平协议作出可信承诺，被广泛认为是导致代价高昂的冲突的两大主要原因（Fearon, 1995; Blattman, 2023）。先进的 AI 系统或许能够利用新型的可信承诺和相互透明机制（将在第 3.1 节和第 3.5 节进一步讨论）（McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023; Tennenholtz, 2004; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld, 2018; Critch et al., 2022; Sun et al., 2023a; Cooper et al., 2025）。然而，该领域的许多现有成果在性质上仍然具有很强的理论性。因此，落实能够增进智能体之间信任与透明度的实用机制和基础设施，是一个重要的开放问题（Chan et al., 2025）。
Normative Approaches to Equilibrium Selection.均衡选择的规范性方法。
One possible cause of conflict is a multiplicity of potential solutions (or equilibria, see also Section 2.1). That is, there might be multiple rational ways for a group of players to interact that are mutually incompatible (Stastny et al., 2021; Duan et al., 2024). For instance, a resource might be split in multiple different ways, but if different parties make inconsistent demands on the resources, conflict may ensue (Piatti et al., 2024). To address this multiplicity, a number of authors have proposed normative principles and theories for singling out specific equilibria.[fn13] For instance, many authors have argued that equilibrium selection should respect symmetries and isomorphisms (Harsanyi & Selten, 1988; Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021; Emmons et al., 2022; Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022). Most prominently, a large literature on so-called bargaining solutions has proposed principles for how a group of players should select an outcome in the face of conflicting preferences (Nash, 1950; Kalai & Smorodinsky, 1975). Relatedly, a literature on so-called cooperative game theory (Shapley, 1953; Gillies, 1959; Schmeidler, 1969; Driessen, 1988; Chalkiadakis et al., 2011) studies how the (e.g., monetary) gains from a joint project should be divided up between a group of agents. [fn14] For further work on normative principles of equilibrium selection, see Schelling (1980); Harsanyi & Selten (1988).
冲突的一个可能原因是存在多个潜在解（或均衡，另见第 2.1 节）。也就是说，一组参与者可能有多种理性的互动方式，而这些方式彼此不相容（Stastny et al., 2021; Duan et al., 2024）。例如，一项资源可以有多种不同的分配方式，但如果不同参与方对资源提出相互冲突的要求，就可能引发冲突（Piatti et al., 2024）。为应对这种多重性，一些作者提出了用于选定特定均衡的规范性原则与理论。[fn13] 例如，许多作者主张，均衡选择应尊重对称性与同构关系（Harsanyi & Selten, 1988; Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021; Emmons et al., 2022; Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022）。其中最突出的是，大量关于所谓谈判解（bargaining solutions）的文献提出了一些原则，用于指导一组参与者在偏好冲突的情况下如何选择结果（Nash, 1950; Kalai & Smorodinsky, 1975）。与此相关，所谓合作博弈论的文献（Shapley, 1953; Gillies, 1959; Schmeidler, 1969; Driessen, 1988; Chalkiadakis et al., 2011）研究如何在一组智能体之间分配联合项目所产生的收益（例如金钱收益）。[fn14] 关于均衡选择的规范性原则的更多研究，参见 Schelling (1980); Harsanyi & Selten (1988)。
Footnote: 13 We note that the idea of always being able to identify the ‘right’ equilibrium is, in general, contentious, as is the framing of agents interacting by ‘selecting’ among game-theoretic equilibria. Nonetheless, the points we make here need not be tied to a narrow, game-theoretic conception of this problem, but can be viewed as a general discussion of how multiple valid outcomes in strategic settings can be possible, and that ensuring specific kinds of outcomes from this set are reached is a challenging problem.
脚注：13 我们指出，总能识别出“正确”均衡的想法通常是有争议的，将智能体的交互表述为在博弈论均衡之间进行“选择”，同样存在争议。尽管如此，我们在此提出的观点不必局限于对这一问题的狭义博弈论理解，而可以视为一种一般性讨论：战略情境中为何可能存在多个合理结果，以及为何确保最终达成其中某些特定类型的结果是一项具有挑战性的问题。
Footnote: 14 There are further literatures that discuss how to resolve disagreements within a group of entities, such as social choice theory (Gaertner, 2010) and the literature on fair division (Brams & Taylor, 1996). However, the most prominent approaches in these literatures are motivated by settings with a centralized decision maker whose happens to care about aggregating the players’ preferences.
脚注：14 还有其他文献探讨如何解决一组实体内部的分歧，例如社会选择理论（Gaertner, 2010）以及关于公平分配的文献（Brams & Taylor, 1996）。然而，这些文献中最主要的方法所依据的情境，都存在一位恰好关心如何汇总参与者偏好的集中式决策者。
Cooperative Dispositions.合作倾向。
Alongside the cooperative capabilities described above, we may also wish to imbue AI agents with cooperative ‘dispositions’. For example, simply caring more for future rewards in sequential social dilemmas (Barfuss et al., 2020) or certain ‘intrinsic motivations’ in MARL – such as inequity aversion (Hughes et al., 2018), social influence (Jaques et al., 2019), or inefficiency penalties (Gemp et al., 2022) – have been shown to improve cooperation in sequential social dilemmas (Leibo et al., 2017; Wang et al., 2019). While it may not be realistic to assume that we can always adjust agents’ objectives, it may be feasible to try to reduce conflict-conducive dispositions (such as vengefulness or a bias towards zero-sum thinking) by modifying the human-generated data or training processes via which we create AI agents (see Section 3.3). Moreover, in some cases it can even be shown that instructing agents to act according to objectives other than their true objectives can lead to robust, guaranteed Pareto-improvements (Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022).
除了上述合作能力之外，我们可能还希望赋予 AI 智能体合作“倾向”。例如，研究表明，在序贯社会困境中，仅仅更加重视未来奖励（Barfuss et al., 2020），或在 MARL 中引入某些“内在动机”——如不平等厌恶（Hughes et al., 2018）、社会影响（Jaques et al., 2019）或低效率惩罚（Gemp et al., 2022）——就能促进序贯社会困境中的合作（Leibo et al., 2017; Wang et al., 2019）。虽然假设我们总能调整智能体的目标可能并不现实，但通过修改用于创建 AI 智能体的人类生成数据或训练过程，尝试减少促成冲突的倾向（如报复心理或零和思维偏见），或许是可行的（见第 3.3 节）。此外，在某些情况下，甚至可以证明，指示智能体按照其真实目标以外的目标行事，能够带来稳健且有保证的帕累托改进（Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022）。
Agent Governance.智能体治理。
In some cases, AI agents may be subject to existing norms and institutions.[fn15] This could occur for a number of reasons, such as agents only being selected (by users) to perform specific tasks (that are subject to existing norms and institutions), human oversight being built in by design, or highly regulated environments providing guardrails that improve agents’ abilities to operate efficiently. However, the status of AI agents’ contractual obligations and accountability for harms remains underdeveloped (Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Kolt, 2024; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992, see also Section 4.2). These challenges are especially acute for agents that operate subject to limited or no human oversight. The development of novel (or at least adapted) agent governance measures could therefore play a critical role in avoiding various forms of conflict involving AI agents, especially in high-stakes domains (Reuel & Undheim, 2024; Kolt et al., 2025). For example, the US has introduced legislation requiring human oversight in nuclear strategy decisions (U.S. Congress, 2023), while international efforts aim to regulate or ban the use of lethal autonomous weapons (Disarmament, 2023). In lower-stakes domains, agent governance could protect individual users and organisations (see also Section 4.3), and enable more stable, efficient networks of AI agents.
在某些情况下，AI 智能体可能受到现有规范与制度的约束。[fn15] 这可能出于多种原因，例如智能体仅被（用户）选中执行（受现有规范与制度约束的）特定任务，在设计时就内置了人类监督，或者受到严格监管的环境提供了防护措施，从而提升了智能体高效运作的能力。然而，AI 智能体的合同义务以及对其所致损害的问责机制仍不完善（Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Kolt, 2024; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992，另见第 4.2 节）。对于在人类监督有限或完全没有人类监督的情况下运行的智能体，这些挑战尤为严峻。因此，制定新的（或至少经过调整的）智能体治理措施，可能在避免涉及 AI 智能体的各种形式的冲突方面发挥关键作用，尤其是在高风险领域（Reuel & Undheim, 2024; Kolt et al., 2025）。例如，美国已提出要求核战略决策接受人类监督的立法（U.S. Congress, 2023），而国际层面的努力则旨在监管或禁止使用致命自主武器（Disarmament, 2023）。在风险较低的领域，智能体治理可以保护个人用户和组织（另见第 4.3 节），并促成更稳定、更高效的 AI 智能体网络。
Footnote: 15 The points in this paragraph benefited greatly from discussions with Noam Kolt.
脚注：15 本段观点得益于与 Noam Kolt 的讨论，获益良多。
Evidential Reasoning.证据推理。
An interesting feature of interactions between AI agents is that they may often interact with others that are very similar to themselves (such as those based on the same AI chatbot). Some decision theorists have argued that mixed-motive strategic interactions against similar opponents should be approached very differently from strategic interactions against generic opponents. For instance, in a one-shot Prisoner’s Dilemma against a sufficiently similar opponent, an agent might reason: “my opponent will likely make the same choice as I. Therefore, if I cooperate, so will my opponent. Whereas, if I defect, my opponent will likely defect as well. Therefore, I should cooperate.” (Brams, 1975; Lewis, 1979; Hofstadter, 1983) Similarly, agents may avoid aggressive acts when facing similar opponents, reasoning that if they act aggressively, others will similarly act aggressively. Hofstadter (1983) called this line of reasoning superrationality; in academic philosophy, the normative theory advocating this type of reasoning is typically called evidential decision theory (Ahmed, 2014). A number of prior works have studied this mode of cooperation (game-)theoretically (Roemer, 2010; Spohn, 2007; Daley & Sadowski, 2017; Halpern & Pass, 2018). Furthermore, a recent line of work has studied evidential decision theory and cooperation against similar opponents in the context of AI agents in particular (Albert & Heiner, 2001; Mayer et al., 2016; Bell et al., 2021; Oesterheld, 2021; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld et al., 2024a; Oesterheld et al., 2024).
AI 智能体之间的交互有一个有趣的特点：它们可能经常与自身非常相似的其他智能体交互（例如，基于同一 AI 聊天机器人的智能体）。一些决策理论家认为，与相似对手进行的混合动机策略交互，应当采用与面对一般对手时截然不同的处理方式。例如，在与一个足够相似的对手进行单次囚徒困境博弈时，智能体可能会这样推理：“我的对手很可能会做出与我相同的选择。因此，如果我合作，我的对手也会合作。而如果我背叛，我的对手很可能也会背叛。因此，我应该合作。”（Brams, 1975; Lewis, 1979; Hofstadter, 1983）同样，智能体在面对相似对手时可能会避免采取攻击行为，因为它们推断，如果自己采取攻击行为，其他智能体也会同样采取攻击行为。Hofstadter (1983) 将这种推理方式称为超理性（superrationality）；在学术哲学中，倡导此类推理的规范性理论通常称为证据决策理论（evidential decision theory）（Ahmed, 2014）。已有多项研究从理论（包括博弈论）角度研究了这种合作模式（Roemer, 2010; Spohn, 2007; Daley & Sadowski, 2017; Halpern & Pass, 2018）。此外，近期的一系列研究专门在 AI 智能体的背景下，研究了证据决策理论以及与相似对手的合作（Albert & Heiner, 2001; Mayer et al., 2016; Bell et al., 2021; Oesterheld, 2021; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld et al., 2024a; Oesterheld et al., 2024）。
2.3 Collusion2.3 合谋
While some of the most important risks from advanced AI are due to cooperation failure, there are some settings where cooperation between AI systems is undesirable. We refer to the problem of unwanted cooperation between AI systems as AI collusion.
虽然先进 AI 带来的一些最重要的风险源于合作失败，但在某些情境下，AI 系统之间的合作并不符合期望。我们将 AI 系统之间不符合期望的合作这一问题称为 AI 合谋。
2.3.1 Definition2.3.1 定义
Collusion has long been a topic of intense study in economics, law, and politics, among other disciplines. While there is no universal definition of collusion, it generally refers to secretive cooperation between two or more parties at the expense of one or more other parties. Most classic examples of collusion – such as firms working together to set supra-competitive prices at the expense of consumers – also tend to be not only secretive but in violation of some law, rule, or ethical standard. Distinctions are also commonly made between explicit and tacit collusion (Rees, 1993), depending on whether the colluding parties communicate with each other.
长期以来，合谋一直是经济学、法学和政治学等学科深入研究的主题。虽然合谋没有普遍适用的定义，但它通常指两方或多方以牺牲一个或多个其他方的利益为代价开展的秘密合作。大多数经典的合谋案例——例如企业联手设定高于竞争水平的价格，从而损害消费者利益——往往不仅具有秘密性，而且违反某项法律、规则或伦理标准。人们通常还会根据合谋各方是否相互沟通，区分显性合谋与默契合谋（Rees, 1993）。
AI collusion could differ from classic definitions of collusion in a number of ways. First, for more basic AI systems (such as algorithmic trading agents) it may be hard to ascribe any notion of intent to collude. Relatedly, there may be forms of AI collusion that are not currently ruled unlawful, because existing legislation may not (yet) apply to the case of AI collusion (Harrington, 2019; Beneke & Mackenrodt, 2019). Second, the distinction between explicit and tacit collusion may break down when it comes to agents whose communication can take very different forms to our own.[fn16] Third, typical definitions of collusion focus on mixed-motive settings where, while selfish agents are incentivised to compete, they also stand to gain (at the expense of some third party) if they can overcome these competitive pressures. AI collusion (by our definition) may also arise when agents have complementary interests (see Section 2.1), but where certain kinds of cooperation are undesirable – i.e., the agents are jointly misaligned.
AI 合谋可能在多个方面不同于合谋的经典定义。首先，对于较为基础的 AI 系统（例如算法交易智能体），可能很难将任何意义上的串谋意图归于它们。与此相关，某些形式的 AI 串谋目前可能尚未被认定为违法，因为现行法律可能（尚）不适用于 AI 串谋的情形（Harrington, 2019; Beneke & Mackenrodt, 2019）。其次，对于通信形式可能与人类大不相同的智能体而言，显性合谋与默契合谋之间的区分可能不再成立。[fn16] 第三，合谋的典型定义侧重于混合动机场景：在这类场景中，自利智能体虽有竞争的动机，但如果能够克服这些竞争压力，也有望（以某个第三方的利益为代价）获益。按照我们的定义，当智能体具有互补利益（见第 2.1 节），但某些合作形式并不可取时，也可能出现 AI 串谋，即这些智能体存在联合失对齐。
Footnote: 16 While from an information-theoretic perspective, it can be shown that for two decision variables to become correlated (a necessary, though not sufficient condition for agents to work together), there must be a non-zero transfer of information between the systems determining the decisions, in AI agents this might be due not only to explicit communication but also to a common cause or process (Hart & Mas-Colell, 2000; Cover & Thomas, 2005; Cesa-Bianchi & Lugosi, 2006; Pearl, 2009).
脚注：16 从信息论的角度可以证明，要使两个决策变量产生相关性（这是智能体协同行动的必要条件，但并非充分条件），决定这些决策的系统之间必须存在非零的信息传递；但在 AI 智能体中，这种相关性不仅可能源于显式通信，还可能源于共同的原因或过程（Hart & Mas-Colell, 2000; Cover & Thomas, 2005; Cesa-Bianchi & Lugosi, 2006; Pearl, 2009）。
2.3.2 Instances2.3.2 实例
The possibility of collusion between advanced AI systems raises several important concerns (Drexler, 2022). First, collusion between AI systems could lead to qualitatively new capabilities or goals (see Section 3.6), exacerbating risks such as the manipulation or deception of humans by AI (Park et al., 2023a; Evans et al., 2021) or the ability to bypass security checks and other safeguards (OpenAI, 2023; Jones et al., 2024). Second, many of the promising approaches to building safe AI rely on a lack of cooperation, such as adversarial training (Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022) or scalable oversight (Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018). If advanced AI systems can learn to collude without our knowledge, these approaches may be insufficient to ensure their safety (Goel et al., 2025, see also Section 4.1).
先进 AI 系统之间发生串谋的可能性引发了若干重要担忧（Drexler, 2022）。首先，AI 系统之间的串谋可能催生性质上全新的能力或目标（见第 3.6 节），从而加剧 AI 操纵或欺骗人类等风险（Park et al., 2023a; Evans et al., 2021），或增强其绕过安全检查及其他防护措施的能力（OpenAI, 2023; Jones et al., 2024）。其次，许多有望构建安全 AI 的方法都依赖于系统之间不进行合作，例如对抗训练（Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022）或可扩展监督（Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018）。如果先进 AI 系统能够在我们不知情的情况下学会串谋，这些方法可能不足以确保其安全性（Goel et al., 2025，另见第 4.1 节）。
Markets.市场。
The quintessential case of collusion in mixed-motive settings is markets, in which efficiency results from competition, not cooperation. While this is not a new problem, collusion between AI systems is especially concerning since they may operate inscrutably due to the speed, scale, complexity, or subtlety of their actions.[fn17] Warnings of this possibility have come from technologists, economists, and legal scholars (Brown & MacKay, 2023; Mehra, 2016; Ezrachi & Stucke, 2017; Harrington, 2019; Beneke & Mackenrodt, 2019). Importantly, AI systems can collude even when collusion is not intended by their developers, since they might learn that colluding is a profitable strategy. Currently, most pricing and trading algorithms are relatively unsophisticated compared to today’s state-of-the-art AI systems, though there is already a growing body of both theoretical (Brown & MacKay, 2023) and empirical (Assad et al., 2020; Wieting & Sapi, 2021; Calvano et al., 2020; Klein, 2021; Abada & Lambin, 2023) evidence that such algorithms may sometimes learn to collude (see Section 2.3.2).[fn18] Unfortunately, due to the huge financial incentives to deploy more advanced, adaptive AI systems in such settings, this risk is likely to increase despite the potential for catastrophic outcomes (see Section 3.4.2). As well as economic harm to consumers, firms in less well-resourced regions may be forced out (widening geographic economic inequality) and increasingly oligopolistic markets may emerge.
市场是混合动机场景中串谋的典型案例，其中效率源于竞争，而非合作。尽管这并非新问题，AI 系统之间的合谋仍尤其令人担忧，因为其行动的速度、规模、复杂性或隐蔽性可能使其运作难以被理解。[fn17] 技术专家、经济学家和法学学者都曾就这种可能性发出警告（Brown & MacKay, 2023; Mehra, 2016; Ezrachi & Stucke, 2017; Harrington, 2019; Beneke & Mackenrodt, 2019）。重要的是，即使开发者并无串谋意图，AI 系统也可能发生串谋，因为它们可能学到串谋是一种有利可图的策略。目前，与当今最先进的 AI 系统相比，大多数定价和交易算法仍相对简单，不过，越来越多的理论证据（Brown & MacKay, 2023）和实证证据（Assad et al., 2020; Wieting & Sapi, 2021; Calvano et al., 2020; Klein, 2021; Abada & Lambin, 2023）已表明，此类算法有时可能学会合谋（参见第 2.3.2 节）。[fn18] 遗憾的是，由于在此类场景中部署更先进、具有适应能力的 AI 系统存在巨大的经济激励，即便可能造成灾难性后果，这种风险也很可能上升（参见第 3.4.2 节）。除了给消费者造成经济损害，资源较为匮乏地区的企业还可能被迫退出市场（加剧地区间的经济不平等），并且可能出现寡头垄断程度日益加深的市场。
Footnote: 17 Moreover, competition between data-driven platforms can be significantly weaker than typical economic competition (Jagadeesan et al., 2023a).
脚注：17 此外，数据驱动平台之间的竞争可能显著弱于典型的经济竞争（Jagadeesan et al., 2023a）。
Footnote: 18 One amusing and fortunately harmless example arose in 2011 when two simple pricing bots on Amazon both ended up attempting to sell a biology textbook for $24 million after repeatedly lowering and raising their prices in response to one another (Solon, 2011).
脚注：18 一个有趣且所幸无害的例子发生在 2011 年：Amazon 上的两个简单定价机器人在相互响应、反复降价和提价之后，最终都试图以 $24 million 的价格出售一本生物学教科书（Solon, 2011）。
Steganography.隐写术（Steganography）。
In the near future we will likely see LLMs communicating with each other to jointly accomplish tasks. To try to prevent collusion, we could monitor and constrain their communication (e.g., to be in natural language). However, models might secretly learn to communicate by concealing messages within other, non-secret text. Recent work on steganography using ML has demonstrated that this concern is well-founded (Hu et al., 2018; Yang et al., 2019; Schroeder et al., 2023a; Roger & Greenblatt, 2023; Mathew et al., 2024, see also Section 2.3.2). Secret communication could also occur via text compression (OpenAI, 2023b), or via the emergence of communication between agents where the symbols used by agents lack any predefined meanings or usage guidelines or are otherwise uninterpretable to humans (Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020; Foerster et al., 2016; Sukhbaatar et al., 2016).
在不久的将来，我们很可能会看到 LLM 相互通信，以共同完成任务。为了尝试防止串谋，我们可以监控并限制它们的通信（例如，要求使用自然语言）。然而，模型可能会暗中学会将消息隐藏在其他非秘密文本中，以此进行通信。近期利用 ML 进行隐写的研究表明，这种担忧有充分依据（Hu et al., 2018; Yang et al., 2019; Schroeder et al., 2023a; Roger & Greenblatt, 2023; Mathew et al., 2024，另见第 2.3.2 节）。秘密通信也可能通过文本压缩（OpenAI, 2023b）实现，或通过智能体之间涌现出的通信实现；在后一种情况下，智能体使用的符号没有任何预先定义的含义或使用规范，或者由于其他原因而无法被人类理解（Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020; Foerster et al., 2016; Sukhbaatar et al., 2016）。
2.3.3 Directions2.3.3 研究方向
There is a growing interdisciplinary body of work studying AI collusion. Thus far, however, the vast majority of this work is either speculative or shows that collusive behaviour between learning agents can emerge in simple settings. More work is therefore required in order to detect AI collusion in more complex settings, and to develop robust methods for mitigating AI collusion.
研究 AI 合谋的跨学科工作正日益增多。然而，迄今为止，这些工作中的绝大多数要么属于推测性研究，要么仅表明学习智能体之间的合谋行为可以在简单环境中涌现。因此，需要开展更多工作，以检测更复杂环境中的 AI 合谋，并开发缓解 AI 合谋的稳健方法。
Detecting AI Collusion.检测 AI 合谋。
Brown & MacKay (2023) show that collusive behaviour can emerge under equilibrium in theoretical models, while Calvano et al. (2020); Klein (2021) show that in simple settings, independent RL algorithms can learn to collude.
