Normann & Sternberg (2023); Leisten (2021); Werner (2021) consider collusion in synthetic settings involving both machines and humans. There have also been documented instances of algorithmic collusion not only in the German gasoline market (Assad et al., 2020), but also in ‘buy box’ placement on the largest online marketplace in Belgium and the Netherlands (Wieting & Sapi, 2021).[fn19] Only a small number of works, however, have considered collusion in the context of more advanced AI agents, such as those powered by LLMs (Motwani et al., 2024; Fish et al., 2024; Mathew et al., 2024; OpenAI, 2023b), and this remains a highly under-explored area. Alongside context-specific evaluations, more effort ought to be devoted to developing general methods for detecting collusion. Some progress in this direction was recently made by Bonjour et al. (2022), who define an information-theoretic measure of collusion applicable to arbitrary models. Future work should investigate additional general methods to detect collusion, including by leveraging interpretability tools to understand systems’ goals (Michaud et al., 2020; Colognese & Jose, 2023; Mini et al., 2023; Marks et al., 2023).
Brown & MacKay (2023) 表明，在理论模型中，合谋行为可以在均衡状态下涌现，而 Calvano et al. (2020); Klein (2021) 则表明，在简单环境中，独立的强化学习（RL）算法可以学会合谋。Normann & Sternberg (2023); Leisten (2021); Werner (2021) 考察了机器与人类共同参与的人工构建环境中的合谋。已有文献记录的算法合谋实例不仅出现在德国汽油市场（Assad et al., 2020），也出现在比利时和荷兰最大的在线交易平台的“购买框”（buy box）展示位分配中（Wieting & Sapi, 2021）。[fn19] 然而，只有少量研究考察了更先进的 AI 智能体（例如由 LLM 驱动的智能体）的合谋问题（Motwani et al., 2024; Fish et al., 2024; Mathew et al., 2024; OpenAI, 2023b），这一领域仍远未得到充分探索。除了针对特定情境开展评估，还应投入更多精力开发检测合谋的通用方法。Bonjour et al. (2022) 最近在这一方向上取得了一些进展，他们定义了一种适用于任意模型的合谋信息论度量。未来的工作应研究更多检测合谋的通用方法，包括利用可解释性工具来理解系统的目标（Michaud et al., 2020; Colognese & Jose, 2023; Mini et al., 2023; Marks et al., 2023）。
Footnote: 19 A buy box is an especially prominently displayed offer for a product on a webpage.
脚注：19 购买框（buy box）是网页上以格外醒目的方式展示的某一产品的报价。
Mitigating AI Collusion.缓解 AI 合谋。
Unfortunately, even fewer previous works propose strategies to mitigate AI collusion. Exceptions include work by Brero et al. (2022), who introduce an ML-based scheme to avoid collusion in buy box placement, Abada & Lambin (2023), who reduce collusion by enforcing decentralized learning or intervening during the learning process, and Cartea et al. (2022), who show that an increased tick size (the coarseness of the grid from which sellers select prices) can obstruct collusion. This existing research focuses only on a narrow range of purely economic settings, and so there is a pressing need to develop more general methods, and to adapt our existing laws and governance structures to cover AI collusion (see Section 4.2). Foxabbott et al. (2023) take a step in the former direction by introducing a general framework that casts the problem of mitigating collusion as one of intervening on aspects of a partially observable stochastic game, such as the actions, observations, or rewards of the agents (though their work only considers a small number of such interventions in toy games). For example, we may be able to prevent steganography in LLMs by syntactically perturbing their output while preserving semantic content (Roger & Greenblatt, 2023), though it has recently been shown that watermarking (a closely related concept) is surprisingly robust to such techniques (Kirchenbauer et al., 2023). It may also be feasible to intervene on the learning algorithms and agent strategies themselves, such as via model editing (Sinitsin et al., 2020; Mitchell et al., 2022) or constrained learning (Achiam et al., 2017).
遗憾的是，提出缓解 AI 合谋策略的既有研究更少。例外包括以下研究：Brero et al. (2022) 提出了一种基于机器学习（ML）的方案，以避免购买框展示位分配中的合谋；Abada & Lambin (2023) 通过强制采用去中心化学习或在学习过程中进行干预来减少合谋；Cartea et al. (2022) 则表明，增大最小报价单位（即卖家选择价格所依据的网格的粗细程度）可以阻碍合谋。这些现有研究仅关注范围狭窄的纯经济情境，因此迫切需要开发更通用的方法，并调整现有法律和治理结构，使其涵盖 AI 合谋（见第 4.2 节）。Foxabbott et al. (2023) 在前一个方向上迈出了一步，他们提出了一个通用框架，将缓解合谋的问题表述为对部分可观测随机博弈的某些方面进行干预的问题，例如干预智能体的动作、观测或奖励（不过，他们的研究仅考察了简化示例博弈中的少数此类干预）。例如，我们或许可以通过在保留语义内容的同时对 LLM 的输出进行句法扰动，来防止其使用隐写术（Roger & Greenblatt, 2023），不过，最近的研究表明，水印技术（一个密切相关的概念）对这类技术具有出人意料的鲁棒性（Kirchenbauer et al., 2023）。对学习算法和智能体策略本身进行干预也可能是可行的，例如通过模型编辑（Sinitsin et al., 2020; Mitchell et al., 2022）或约束学习（Achiam et al., 2017）。
Assessing Impacts on Safety Protocols.评估对安全协议的影响。
Alongside research on the detection and mitigation of AI collusion, more work is needed to test if AI systems can collude in safety-critical situations (Shevlane et al., 2023), and to evaluate which AI safety proposals are most vulnerable to collusion. Current AI systems are developed and tested in isolation, without regard for the fact that they will increasingly come into contact with each other. This leaves open the possibility that, for example, multiple agents might work together to overcome their individual safeguards (Jones et al., 2024, see also Sections 3.6 and 3.7). Similarly, many of the more promising approaches to ensure the safety of advanced AI are implicitly multi-agent, such as adversarial training (Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022), oversight schemes (Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018), the modularisation of agents (Drexler, 2019; Dalrymple et al., 2024), or automated methods for interpretability (Bills et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023). Determining which of these approaches are most robust to AI collusion and/or modifying them to be so will be important as AI agents grow more sophisticated in their abilities to work together (see Section 4.1).
除了研究 AI 合谋的检测与缓解，还需要开展更多工作，以测试 AI 系统能否在安全关键情境中合谋（Shevlane et al., 2023），并评估哪些 AI 安全方案最容易受到合谋的影响。当前的 AI 系统都是在彼此隔离的情况下开发和测试的，没有考虑到它们将日益频繁地相互接触这一事实。这使得某些可能性仍然存在，例如，多个智能体可能协同合作，突破各自的安全防护措施（Jones et al., 2024，另见第 3.6 节和第 3.7 节）。同样，许多较有前景的先进 AI 安全保障方法都隐含着多智能体参与，例如对抗训练（Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022）、监督方案（Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018）、智能体的模块化（Drexler, 2019; Dalrymple et al., 2024），或自动化的可解释性方法（Bills et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023）。随着 AI 智能体的协作能力日益成熟，确定这些方法中哪些对 AI 合谋最具稳健性，以及／或者对其加以修改以使其具备这种稳健性，将变得十分重要（见第 4.1 节）。
3 Risk Factors3 风险因素
In order to prevent the aforementioned failure modes, it is necessary to consider the mechanisms via which they can arise, which we call ‘risk factors’. These risk factors are largely independent of the agents’ precise incentives or the desired behaviour of the system. For example, information asymmetries (Section 3.1) could lead to miscoordination between agents with the same goal, or a greater risk of conflict among agents with competing goals. In other cases, such as security vulnerabilities in multi-agent systems (Section 3.7), the objectives of the agents and whether we want them to cooperate or compete may be largely irrelevant. In what follows, we outline seven key risk factors (information asymmetries, network effects, selection pressures, destabilising dynamics, commitment and trust, emergent agency, and multi-agent security), though we stress that these categories are neither exhaustive nor mutually exclusive. For example, while it might be an information asymmetry that first leads to a conflict (Section 3.1), this conflict could end up escalating due to the destabilising dynamics (Section 3.4), and fail to be resolved due to a lack of trust or commitment ability (Section 3.5).
为了防止上述失效模式，有必要考察导致这些失效模式产生的机制，我们将这些机制称为“风险因素”。这些风险因素在很大程度上独立于智能体的具体激励或系统的预期行为。例如，信息不对称（第 3.1 节）可能导致目标相同的智能体之间协调失误，也可能增大目标相互竞争的智能体之间发生冲突的风险。在其他情况下，例如多智能体系统中的安全漏洞（第 3.7 节），智能体的目标以及我们希望它们合作还是竞争，可能在很大程度上都无关紧要。下文将概述七个关键风险因素（信息不对称、网络效应、选择压力、失稳动态、承诺与信任、涌现的能动性以及多智能体安全），但我们强调，这些类别既不穷尽所有可能，也不相互排斥。例如，最初引发冲突的可能是信息不对称（第 3.1 节），但这场冲突最终可能因失稳动态而升级（第 3.4 节），并因缺乏信任或承诺能力而无法得到解决（第 3.5 节）。
3.1 Information Asymmetries3.1 信息不对称
A key aspect of many multi-agent systems is that some agents might possess knowledge that others do not. These information asymmetries can result from constraints on information exchange or from strategic behaviour and can lead to cooperation failures in both common-interest and mixed-motive settings. Despite their information processing capabilities, AI agents remain vulnerable to failures caused by information asymmetries.
许多多智能体系统的一个关键特征是，某些智能体可能掌握其他智能体并不掌握的知识。这些信息不对称可能源于信息交换受到限制或策略性行为，并且在共同利益情境和混合动机情境中都可能导致合作失败。尽管 AI 智能体具备信息处理能力，它们仍然容易受到信息不对称所引发的失效的影响。
3.1.1 Definition3.1.1 定义
Information asymmetry refers to the situation where interacting agents possess different levels of information bearing on a joint action. For example, in a transaction involving a used car, the seller may have more accurate or reliable information than the buyer about the condition of the car, and thereby its expected maintenance costs. As Akerlof (1970) famously demonstrated, information asymmetry can lead to market failure (such as when a buyer cannot trust the seller to be honest about the condition of the car, and therefore does not buy the car, even if it is in good condition). More broadly, information asymmetry can pose obstacles to effective interaction, preventing agents from coordinating their actions for mutual benefit (Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983).
信息不对称是指相互交互的智能体对与共同行动有关的信息掌握程度不同的情形。例如，在二手车交易中，卖方可能比买方掌握更准确或更可靠的车辆状况信息，因而也更了解车辆的预期维护成本。正如 Akerlof (1970) 的著名研究所表明的那样，信息不对称可能导致市场失灵（例如，买方无法相信卖方会如实说明车辆状况，因此即使车辆状况良好，也不会购买）。更广泛地说，信息不对称可能阻碍有效互动，使智能体无法协调行动以实现互利（Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983）。
A fundamental problem is that information is a strategic asset, so any selfish actor has a natural incentive to protect their own information advantages. A difference in interests can impede information sharing even when revelation is mutually preferred (in the example above, the seller would like to reveal the car’s true condition to the buyer, but the buyer cannot take the seller’s report at face value). The problem can be exacerbated by active deception, for example through actions taken by the seller to make the car appear in better condition than it actually is. As disparity in information is commonplace, we must generally accept the associated costs, whether that be through market inefficiency (e.g., cars that cannot be sold), effort devoted to deception and dispelling deception, or extra work to convey strategically sensitive information (e.g., hiring third-party car inspectors).
一个根本问题在于，信息是一种战略资产，因此任何自利的行为主体都天然有动机维护自身的信息优势。即使双方都希望披露信息，利益差异仍可能阻碍信息共享（在上述例子中，卖方希望向买方披露车辆的真实状况，但买方无法直接采信卖方的陈述）。主动欺骗可能进一步加剧这一问题，例如卖方采取措施，使车辆看起来比实际状况更好。由于信息差异普遍存在，我们通常必须接受相关成本，无论这些成本体现为市场低效率（例如，汽车无法售出）、为实施欺骗和识破欺骗而投入的精力，还是为传递具有战略敏感性的信息而付出的额外努力（例如，聘请第三方车辆检测人员）。
3.1.2 Instances3.1.2 实例
In many instances, the mechanisms developed to cope with information asymmetry in human economies can also be employed for interactions with AI agents. However, the distinct nature of artificial agents may present new forms of information asymmetry but also new ways of overcoming these asymmetries.
在许多情况下，人类经济中为应对信息不对称而发展出的机制，也可以用于与 AI 智能体的互动。然而，人工智能体的独特性质可能带来新形式的信息不对称，同时也可能带来克服这些不对称的新方法。
Communication Constraints.通信约束。
A fundamental source of information asymmetries is that constraints on information exchange can exist, even when agents share a common goal (see Section 2.1). These might be constraints on space (i.e., the amount of information that can be communicated) if the information that needs to be communicated is especially complex, time if a snap decision is required before all information can be communicated, or both. For today’s AI systems, intelligent information exchange in common-interest settings is still a major topic of study (see, e.g., Sukhbaatar et al., 2016; Foerster et al., 2016; Zhang et al., 2018; Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020; Lauffer et al., 2023). As these systems become more capable, however, it is likely that strategic considerations (i.e., the incentives that agents have to keep their private information private) will become the more important limitation on information exchange.
信息不对称的一个根本来源是，即使智能体拥有共同目标，信息交换仍可能受到限制（见第 2.1 节）。如果需要传递的信息特别复杂，这些限制可能是空间上的限制（即能够传递的信息量）；如果必须在所有信息传递完毕之前迅速作出决策，则可能是时间上的限制；也可能同时存在这两种限制。对于当今的 AI 系统而言，共同利益情境中的智能信息交换仍然是一个重要研究课题（例如，见 Sukhbaatar et al., 2016; Foerster et al., 2016; Zhang et al., 2018; Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020; Lauffer et al., 2023）。然而，随着这些系统的能力不断增强，策略性考量（即智能体保守自身私有信息的动机）很可能成为限制信息交换的更重要因素。
Bargaining.议价。
As a classic example of these strategic considerations is that when agents attempt to come to an agreement despite diverging interests, information asymmetries can lead to bargaining inefficiencies (Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983). Relevant uncertainties about other agents can include how much they value possible agreements, their outside options, or their beliefs about others. The essential reason for such inefficiencies is that, under uncertainty about their counterparties, agents must make a trade-off between the rewards of making more favourable demands and the risk of other agents refusing such demands. This trade-off sometimes results in incompatible demands and thus bargaining failure, ranging from the impossibility of guaranteeing efficient trade between a buyer and seller with asymmetric information about how much they value a good (Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983), to costly and avoidable conflict when agents are uncertain about the capabilities and objectives of others (Fearon, 1995; Slantchev & Tarar, 2011). Because these failures stem from strategic incentives rather than a lack of capabilities, general advances in AI may not solve such problems by default.
这些战略考量的一个经典例子是，当智能体试图在利益存在分歧的情况下达成协议时，信息不对称可能导致议价效率损失 (Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983)。关于其他智能体的相关不确定性可能包括：它们对潜在协议的估值、它们在无法达成协议时的外部选择，以及它们对其他参与者的信念。此类效率损失的根本原因在于，面对交易对手的不确定性，智能体必须在提出对自身更有利的要求所带来的收益与其他智能体拒绝这些要求的风险之间进行权衡。这种权衡有时会导致彼此不相容的要求，进而导致议价失败，其表现既包括：当买卖双方对于各自如何评估某件商品的价值存在信息不对称时，无法保证实现有效率的交易 (Myerson & Satterthwaite, 1983)；也包括：当智能体不确定其他参与者的能力和目标时，发生代价高昂且本可避免的冲突 (Fearon, 1995; Slantchev & Tarar, 2011)。由于这些失败源于战略激励而非能力不足，AI 的一般性进步未必会自然而然地解决此类问题。
Footnote: 20 This is analogous to how a spammer can get past a spam filter but only by distorting the message (e.g., with strange spellings) so it is less potent in conveying its intent.
脚注：20 这类似于垃圾邮件发送者能够绕过垃圾邮件过滤器，但只能通过扭曲信息（例如，使用奇怪的拼写）来实现，从而削弱了信息传达其意图的效力。
Deception.欺骗。
Information asymmetries and differing strategic interests can naturally incentivise deception: taking actions designed to mislead others. While much attention has been paid to the potential for AI agents to deceive humans (Evans et al., 2021; Ward et al., 2023; Park et al., 2024; Carroll et al., 2023; Goldstein et al., 2023; Haghtalab et al., 2024; Oesterheld et al., 2023; Kay et al., 2024; Zhou et al., 2023), they may also be incentivised to deceive and manipulate other AI agents (acting on behalf of other humans). Indeed, the ability to deceive other models may be exacerbated by disparities in model size and the scale of data sets (Haghtalab et al., 2024, see also Section 4.3). We can also view misinformation as a kind of deception in systematic form, which large numbers of advanced AI agents may enable at unprecedented scale (see Section 3.2 and Section 3.2.2).
信息不对称和战略利益差异自然会产生实施欺骗的激励，即采取旨在误导他人的行动。尽管 AI 智能体欺骗人类的可能性已受到广泛关注 (Evans et al., 2021; Ward et al., 2023; Park et al., 2024; Carroll et al., 2023; Goldstein et al., 2023; Haghtalab et al., 2024; Oesterheld et al., 2023; Kay et al., 2024; Zhou et al., 2023)，它们也可能有动机去欺骗和操纵其他 AI 智能体（这些智能体代表其他人类行事）。事实上，模型大小和数据集规模的差异可能会增强欺骗其他模型的能力 (Haghtalab et al., 2024，另见第 4.3 节)。我们也可以将错误信息视为一种系统化的欺骗形式，而大量先进 AI 智能体可能使其以前所未有的规模出现（见第 3.2 节和第 3.2.2 节）。
3.1.3 Directions3.1.3 研究方向
Information asymmetries are a foundational topic within game theory and mechanism design, and as such there is a wealth of insights to draw upon from these fields. At the same time, these earlier literatures typically consider applications to economic actors such as firms and regulators, as opposed to the computational and strategic nature of advanced AI agents. Many directions in this section therefore correspond not just to translating and scaling up classical insights to this new domain (Wu et al., 2022; Levinstein & Herrmann, 2023; Treutlein et al., 2021), but to leveraging the special features of AI agents to enable new mechanisms for overcoming information asymmetries (DiGiovanni & Clifton, 2023; Tennenholtz, 2004; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023).
信息不对称是博弈论和机制设计中的基础性议题，因此可以从这些领域汲取丰富的见解。与此同时，这些早期文献通常考虑的是企业和监管机构等经济行为主体的应用场景，而非先进 AI 智能体的计算特性和战略特性。因此，本节中的许多研究方向不仅涉及将经典见解迁移至这一新领域并扩大其应用规模 (Wu et al., 2022; Levinstein & Herrmann, 2023; Treutlein et al., 2021)，还涉及利用 AI 智能体的独有特征，构建克服信息不对称的新机制 (DiGiovanni & Clifton, 2023; Tennenholtz, 2004; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023)。
Information Design.信息设计。
Viewed from a ‘centralised’ perspective, solutions to information asymmetries can often be cast as a problem of information design: carefully structuring and revealing information so as to influence the behaviour of strategic agents (Bergemann & Morris, 2019). Most work on information design, however, focuses on relatively restricted settings such as Bayesian persuasion (Kamenica & Gentzkow, 2011), where there is a single information designer with an informational advantage and a single agent whose behaviour is to be influenced.[fn21] Even in simple multi-agent generalisations, the information designer’s problem may be computationally intractable (Dughmi, 2019), leading to recent work that leverages approximate techniques such as RL (Wu et al., 2022) – including in the case of both multiple ‘senders’ (Hossain et al., 2024) and multiple ‘receivers’ (Ivanov et al., 2023). Beyond these settings, more needs to be done to scale these techniques to advanced AI agents, including LLM-based agents. Other important directions include making information design techniques more robust to boundedly rational agents (Yang & Zhang, 2024), or a lack of knowledge about the receivers’ prior beliefs (Lin & Li, 2024) or objectives (Bacchiocchi et al., 2024). Similarly, receivers are assumed to know the distribution of the sender, which may not be possible if the sender is an advanced AI agent to which they only have black-box access.
从“中心化”视角来看，信息不对称的解决方案通常可以表述为信息设计问题：通过精心组织和披露信息，影响策略性智能体的行为 (Bergemann & Morris, 2019)。然而，信息设计的大多数研究都聚焦于相对受限的情境，例如贝叶斯说服（Bayesian persuasion）（Kamenica & Gentzkow, 2011），其中只有一个具有信息优势的信息设计者，以及一个其行为有待影响的智能体。[fn21] 即使在简单的多智能体推广情境中，信息设计者面临的问题也可能在计算上难以求解（Dughmi, 2019），这促使近期研究采用强化学习（RL）等近似方法（Wu et al., 2022）——包括存在多个“发送者”（Hossain et al., 2024）和多个“接收者”（Ivanov et al., 2023）的情形。除了这些情境之外，还需要开展更多工作，将这些技术扩展到先进 AI 智能体，包括基于 LLM 的智能体。其他重要方向包括提高信息设计技术在以下情况下的稳健性：智能体具有有限理性 (Yang & Zhang, 2024)，或缺乏关于接收者先验信念 (Lin & Li, 2024) 或目标 (Bacchiocchi et al., 2024) 的知识。类似地，研究通常假设接收者知道发送者的分布，但如果发送者是一个先进 AI 智能体，而接收者只能以黑箱方式访问它，那么这一假设可能无法成立。
Footnote: 21 Though there are notable exceptions. For instance, Arieli & Babichenko (2019); Haghtalab et al. (2025) consider private persuasion schemes that more effectively align the actions of multiple receivers.
脚注：21 不过，也存在一些值得注意的例外。例如，Arieli & Babichenko (2019); Haghtalab et al. (2025) 考察了能够更有效地协调多个接收者行动的私有说服方案。
Individual Information Revelation.个体信息披露。
From a more ‘decentralised’ perspective, we may want to give AI agents new affordances for disclosing and verifying private information. This can eliminate many inefficiencies that result from information asymmetries – as is shown by ‘unravelling’ arguments, where rational agents anticipate others’ strategic inferences and thus voluntarily disclose private information (Grossman, 1981; Milgrom, 1981) – while avoiding the need for a mediator or information designer. For example, DiGiovanni & Clifton (2023) show that the ability to conditionally reveal private information (given guarantees that it won’t worsen the outcome for the revealing agent) can create new efficient equilibria. They argue that AI systems might more easily enable this approach due to fundamental properties such as being written in (machine-readable) code (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004; Halpern & Pass, 2018; Oesterheld, 2018), as well as the use of tools for interpretability and cryptography. Similarly, safe Pareto improvements aim to help avoid miscoordination in mixed-motive settings by leveraging tools for transparency and commitment (Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022; DiGiovanni et al., 2024). Other directions make use of incentive design to promote truthful revelation even without verification, known as peer prediction (Witkowski & Parkes, 2012; Kong & Schoenebeck, 2019; Prelec, 2004; Miller et al., 2005; Shnayder et al., 2016). Future work could generate additional proposals along these lines or begin to attempt implementing them in real-world systems.
从更“去中心化”的视角来看，我们可能希望赋予 AI 智能体披露和验证私有信息的新手段。这可以消除信息不对称导致的许多效率损失，同时避免对中介或信息设计者的需求——“信息逐步揭示”（unravelling）论证表明，理性智能体会预判其他参与者的策略性推断，因而自愿披露私有信息 (Grossman, 1981; Milgrom, 1981)。例如，DiGiovanni & Clifton (2023) 表明，有条件地披露私人信息的能力（前提是保证这不会使披露信息的智能体面临更差的结果）可以创造新的有效率均衡。他们认为，AI 系统可能更容易实现这种方法，因为它们具有以（机器可读的）代码编写等基本属性 (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004; Halpern & Pass, 2018; Oesterheld, 2018)，并且能够使用可解释性和密码学工具。类似地，安全帕累托改进（safe Pareto improvements）旨在利用透明性和承诺工具，帮助避免混合动机情境中的协调失误 (Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022; DiGiovanni et al., 2024)。其他研究方向利用激励设计，即使在无法核实的情况下也能促进如实披露，这被称为同伴预测（peer prediction）(Witkowski & Parkes, 2012; Kong & Schoenebeck, 2019; Prelec, 2004; Miller et al., 2005; Shnayder et al., 2016)。未来的研究可以沿着这些思路提出更多方案，或开始尝试在现实世界的系统中实施这些方案。
Few-Shot Coordination.少样本协调。
In settings where there are fundamental constraints on information exchange, agents may have to learn to interact with other agents based on little or no prior information. These correspond to the problem of few- (Zhu et al., 2021; Fosong et al., 2022) and zero-shot coordination (Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021), respectively. In common-interest settings, this question has been most famously studied under the heading of ad hoc teamwork (Stone et al., 2010, see also Section 2.1). Often, this involves reasoning about others (Albrecht & Stone, 2018), such as via theory of mind (Zhu et al., 2021; Nguyen et al., 2024) or based on the similarity of other agents to oneself (Albert & Heiner, 2001; Mayer et al., 2016; Bell et al., 2021; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld et al., 2024a). It may also require learning or selecting social norms and conventions (Lerer & Peysakhovich, 2019; Tucker et al., 2020). Another important consideration is ensuring that agents are trained in the context of sufficiently diverse or open-ended sets of co-players (Lupu et al., 2021; Li et al., 2023a), and to ensure that they can transfer this learning effectively to new populations (Wang et al., 2021; Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022, see also Section 3.3). The vast majority of these efforts, however, are restricted to relatively simple common-interest games; the more realistic setting of complex, mixed-motive interactions can be significantly more challenging and may call for the development of new techniques for intelligent information acquisition using active learning.
在信息交换受到根本性限制的情境中，智能体可能必须在几乎没有或完全没有先验信息的情况下，学习如何与其他智能体互动。这两种情况分别对应少样本协调 (Zhu et al., 2021; Fosong et al., 2022) 和零样本协调 (Hu et al., 2020; Treutlein et al., 2021) 问题。在共同利益情境中，对这一问题最著名的研究以临时团队协作（ad hoc teamwork）为题展开 (Stone et al., 2010，另见第 2.1 节)。这通常涉及对他者进行推理 (Albrecht & Stone, 2018)，例如通过心智理论（theory of mind）(Zhu et al., 2021; Nguyen et al., 2024)，或根据其他智能体与自身的相似性进行推理 (Albert & Heiner, 2001; Mayer et al., 2016; Bell et al., 2021; Barasz et al., 2014; Oesterheld et al., 2024a)。这也可能需要学习或选择社会规范与惯例 (Lerer & Peysakhovich, 2019; Tucker et al., 2020)。另一个重要的考虑因素是，确保智能体在足够多样化或开放式的共同参与者群体中接受训练 (Lupu et al., 2021; Li et al., 2023a)，并确保它们能够将所学有效迁移到新的群体中 (Wang et al., 2021; Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022，另见第 3.3 节)。然而，这些努力绝大多数都局限于相对简单的共同利益博弈；复杂的混合动机互动这一更贴近现实的情境可能更具挑战性，并且可能需要开发利用主动学习进行智能信息获取的新技术。
Truthful AI.诚实 AI。
Even when there are fewer strategic incentives to withhold information, there is still a concern that AI systems might lie, either to humans or to one another, which could (in some cases) undermine cooperation and have wider deleterious effects on society (Evans et al., 2021; Park et al., 2024). Some of these concerns could be addressed by training models on more carefully curated and annotated datasets (Peskov et al., 2020; Aly et al., 2021), and by using techniques for overseeing or challenging untrustworthy communication (Irving et al., 2018; Greenblatt et al., 2023). Other work has focused more explicitly on the problem of detection, both in theory (Ward et al., 2023) and in practice (Pacchiardi et al., 2024; Azaria & Mitchell, 2023; Burns et al., 2022), though this remains something of an open problem (Levinstein & Herrmann, 2023). Foundational results in mechanism design (namely, the ‘revelation principle’) tell us that anything that can be done with strategic agents can be done using a truthful mechanism (Gibbard, 1973), and while computational constraints have previously limited the practical application of this insight (Conitzer & Sandholm, 2004), more powerful AI agents might be able to overcome such constraints. Alongside this, advances in interpretability, adversarial training, and the oversight of AI communication (including fact-checking methods) are all likely to help with the general problem, though the issue of deception and manipulation between AI agents, or the advantages that multiple agents may have (over a single agent) in deception and manipulation, remain under-explored.
即使隐瞒信息的策略性动机较少，人们仍然担心 AI 系统可能对人类或彼此说谎，这可能（在某些情况下）破坏合作，并对社会产生更广泛的有害影响 (Evans et al., 2021; Park et al., 2024)。其中一些担忧可以通过使用经过更精心整理和标注的数据集来训练模型 (Peskov et al., 2020; Aly et al., 2021)，以及使用监督或质疑不可信通信的技术来应对 (Irving et al., 2018; Greenblatt et al., 2023)。其他研究则更明确地关注检测问题，既包括理论研究 (Ward et al., 2023)，也包括实践研究 (Pacchiardi et al., 2024; Azaria & Mitchell, 2023; Burns et al., 2022)，不过，这在一定程度上仍是一个尚未解决的问题 (Levinstein & Herrmann, 2023)。机制设计中的基础性结果（即“显示原理”）告诉我们，凡是能够通过策略性智能体实现的事情，都可以通过诚实机制来实现 (Gibbard, 1973)；尽管计算约束此前限制了这一认识的实际应用 (Conitzer & Sandholm, 2004)，但更强大的 AI 智能体或许能够克服这些约束。与此同时，可解释性、对抗训练以及 AI 通信监督（包括事实核查方法）的进展，都有望帮助解决这一总体问题，不过，AI 智能体之间的欺骗与操纵问题，以及多个智能体在欺骗与操纵方面可能具有的（相对于单个智能体的）优势，仍未得到充分研究。
3.2 Network Effects3.2 网络效应
The ongoing integration of AI capabilities into a wide range of existing networks, both virtual and physical, is rapidly transforming the way our interconnected world operates. From business communication systems and financial trading networks to smart energy grids and logistical networks (Mayorkas, 2024; Camacho et al., 2024; Ferreira et al., 2021), entities or communication channels that were once controlled by humans are increasingly becoming AI-powered. This shift represents a systemic change in the way business, social, and technological networks operate, promising significantly improved efficiency and a greater diffusion of benefits from advanced AI, while also introducing novel risks.
AI 能力正持续融入各种现有的虚拟和物理网络，迅速改变着我们这个互联世界的运作方式。从商业通信系统和金融交易网络，到智能电网和物流网络 (Mayorkas, 2024; Camacho et al., 2024; Ferreira et al., 2021)，曾经由人类控制的实体或通信渠道正越来越多地由 AI 驱动。这一转变代表着商业、社会和技术网络运作方式的系统性变革，有望显著提高效率，使先进 AI 带来的益处得到更广泛的传播，同时也引入新的风险。
3.2.1 Definition3.2.1 定义
Many of the complex systems critical to human society can be understood as networks, including transportation, social interactions, trade, biological ecosystems, and communication, among others (Barabási & Pósfai, 2016; Newman & Newman, 2018; Jackson & Zenou, 2015). Networks consist of nodes (such as people, organisations, or resources) and connections (such as communication channels, infrastructural dependencies, or exchanges of goods and services). Network effects refer to consequences of the intricate relationships between the properties of individual connections and nodes, connectivity patterns, and the behaviours exhibited by the network as a whole (Siegenfeld & Bar-Yam, 2020).
许多对人类社会至关重要的复杂系统都可以理解为网络，包括交通运输、社会互动、贸易、生物生态系统和通信等（Barabási & Pósfai, 2016; Newman & Newman, 2018; Jackson & Zenou, 2015）。网络由节点（如个人、组织或资源）和连接（如通信渠道、基础设施依赖关系或商品与服务的交换）构成。网络效应是指单个连接和节点的属性、连接模式以及网络整体表现出的行为之间的复杂关系所产生的后果（Siegenfeld & Bar-Yam, 2020）。
This underlying structure means that a networked system can suffer from a range of failure modes that individual, disconnected systems do not, such as the spread of malfunctions, phase transitions, and undesirable clustering or homogeneities (Cohen & Havlin, 2010). Importantly, a system’s behaviour within a network often differs from its behaviour when characterised independently.[fn22] Non-AI examples of these phenomena include power grid blackouts (Buldyrev et al., 2010; Shakarian et al., 2013), flash crashes (Paulin et al., 2019; Elliott et al., 2014, see also Section 3.4.2), ecosystem collapse (Bascompte & Stouffer, 2009; Gao et al., 2016), or political unrest and conflict (Wood, 2008; Forsberg, 2008).
这种底层结构意味着，网络化系统可能遭遇一系列独立且互不连接的系统不会出现的失效模式，例如故障传播、相变，以及不良的聚集或同质化现象（Cohen & Havlin, 2010）。重要的是，系统在网络中的行为往往不同于对其进行独立刻画时的行为。[fn22] 这类现象在非 AI 领域的例子包括电网停电（Buldyrev et al., 2010; Shakarian et al., 2013）、闪电崩盘（Paulin et al., 2019; Elliott et al., 2014，另见第 3.4.2 节）、生态系统崩溃（Bascompte & Stouffer, 2009; Gao et al., 2016），以及政治动荡与冲突（Wood, 2008; Forsberg, 2008）。
Footnote: 22 For example, the power lines most susceptible to causing a network collapse might not necessarily be the largest or most heavily loaded (Buldyrev et al., 2010).
脚注：22 例如，最容易引发网络崩溃的输电线路，不一定是规模最大或负荷最重的线路（Buldyrev et al., 2010）。
3.2.2 Instances3.2.2 实例
As AI systems take on certain roles traditionally performed by humans, the fundamental properties of networks will change as human nodes are replaced by AI nodes. This transition will likely manifest in several key ways. First, the fact that (software-based) AI systems can be quickly and easily duplicated means the networks may be much larger. Second, the speed at which AI systems can transmit information and take action means that interactions may be much faster. Third, the generality and open-endedness of autonomous, advanced AI systems means that network connectivity may be much denser.[fn23] Below we explore some of the possible impacts of these changes.
随着 AI 系统承担某些传统上由人类履行的角色，人类节点被 AI 节点取代，网络的基本属性也将发生变化。这一转变可能主要表现为以下几个方面。首先，（基于软件的）AI 系统可以快速、轻松地复制，这意味着网络的规模可能大得多。其次，AI 系统传输信息和采取行动的速度意味着，交互可能快得多。第三，自主的先进 AI 系统所具有的通用性和开放性意味着，网络连接可能会密集得多。[fn23] 下文将探讨这些变化可能产生的一些影响。
Footnote: 23 The transition toward autonomous AI agents is progressing partially through improved API interaction capabilities (Mialon et al., 2023; Qin et al., 2023) and specialized API-integrated models (Basu et al., 2024; Patil et al., 2023; Anthropic, 2024a), as well as an increasing number of modalities through which models can interact.
脚注：23 向自主 AI 智能体的转变，部分得益于 API 交互能力的提升（Mialon et al., 2023; Qin et al., 2023）、集成 API 的专用模型的发展（Basu et al., 2024; Patil et al., 2023; Anthropic, 2024a），以及模型可用于交互的模态日益增多。
Error Propagation.错误传播。
One well-known issue with communication networks is that information can be corrupted as it propagates through the network.[fn24] As AI systems become capable of generating and processing more and more kinds of information, AI agents could end up ‘polluting the epistemic commons’ (Huang & Siddarth, 2023; Kay et al., 2024) of both other agents (Ju et al., 2024) and humans (see Section 3.2.2 and Section 3.1) Another increasingly important framework is the use of individual AI agents as part of teams and scaffolded chains of delegation, which transmit not only information but instructions or goals through networks of agents. If these goals are distorted or corrupted, then this can lead to worse outcomes for the delegating agent(s) (Sourbut et al., 2024; Nguyen et al., 2024a). Finally, while the previous examples are phrased in terms of unintentional errors, it may be that certain network structures allow – or perhaps even encourage – the spread of errors that are deliberately introduced by malicious agents (Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Ju et al., 2024, see also Section 3.2.2).[fn25]
通信网络的一个众所周知的问题是，信息在网络中传播时可能发生失真。[fn24] 随着 AI 系统能够生成和处理越来越多种类的信息，AI 智能体最终可能会“污染”其他智能体（Ju et al., 2024）和人类（见第 3.2.2 节和第 3.1 节）的“认知公地”（Huang & Siddarth, 2023; Kay et al., 2024）。另一种日益重要的框架是，将单个 AI 智能体作为团队和由支撑框架组织的委派链的一部分，使其通过智能体网络传递信息，以及指令或目标。如果这些目标发生扭曲或失真，就可能使发起委托的智能体获得更差的结果（Sourbut et al., 2024; Nguyen et al., 2024a）。最后，尽管前面的例子都是以无意的错误来表述的，但某些网络结构可能允许——甚至鼓励——恶意智能体蓄意引入的错误传播（Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Ju et al., 2024，另见第 3.2.2 节）。[fn25]
Footnote: 24 A familiar, non-technical example is the popular childhood game of ‘telephone’, in which each person repeats a message to the next by whispering, typically leading to a different message at the end of the chain than at the beginning.
脚注：24 一个大家熟悉的非技术性例子是流行的儿童“传话”游戏，其中每个人都以耳语的方式向下一个人重复一条信息，通常导致传递链末端的信息与起始信息不同。
Footnote: 25 In a non-AI instance of this beahviour, Raman et al. (2019) showed how strategic, coordinated misinformation attacks by consumers regarding energy usage can be used to cause instabilities and even blackouts in a power grid.
脚注：25 在这种行为的一个非 AI 案例中，Raman et al. (2019) 展示了消费者如何通过策略性、协同开展的用能虚假信息攻击，造成电网不稳定，甚至引发停电。
Footnote: 26 A very similar concurrent experiment by Acerbi & Stubbersfield (2023) showed how this can also reinforce biases such as gender stereotypes. These examples demonstrate how information can degrade as it propagates through networks of AI systems, even without malicious intent.
脚注：26 Acerbi & Stubbersfield (2023) 同期开展的一项非常相似的实验表明，这也会强化性别刻板印象等偏见。这些例子表明，即使不存在恶意意图，信息在 AI 系统网络中传播时也可能退化。
Network Rewiring.网络重连。
A different class of problems concerns not changes in the content transmitted through the network but changes in the network structure itself (Albert et al., 2000). For example, AI systems may choose to interact more with other AIs than humans (Liu et al., 2024; Panickssery et al., 2024; Goel et al., 2025; Laurito et al., 2024), due to factors like availability, response speed, compatibility, cost efficiency or even bias.[fn27] This kind of ‘preferential attachment’ can have large impacts on network structures (Kunegis et al., 2013; Maoz, 2012), which could include AI systems assuming a more critical and central role than intended, or leading to an unequal distribution of resources or power (see Section 4.3). Other risks from rewiring include ‘phase transitions’, where a gradual change in individual connections or network structure triggers a sudden and dramatic shift in the behaviour of the entire network (Newman, 2003, see also Section 3.4). Such changes might occur naturally (e.g., in global trade networks as the transition from expensive human-human interactions to cheaper AI-AI interactions leads to many new connections between sellers and buyers) or artificially (e.g., if a model developer makes an update that inadvertently connects or disconnects a vast number of downstream agents and applications). While such problems are already present in existing systems (Gao et al., 2016; Vié & Morales, 2021), the increased size, speed, and density of AI-based networks – as well as the fact the changes in these networks may be less transparent – means that instabilities could be harder to diagnose and mitigate.
另一类问题涉及的并非网络中所传输内容的变化，而是网络结构本身的变化（Albert et al., 2000）。例如，由于可用性、响应速度、兼容性、成本效益，甚至偏见等因素，AI 系统可能会选择更多地与其他 AI 而非人类交互（Liu et al., 2024; Panickssery et al., 2024; Goel et al., 2025; Laurito et al., 2024）。[fn27] 这种“优先连接”（preferential attachment）可能对网络结构产生重大影响（Kunegis et al., 2013; Maoz, 2012），包括使 AI 系统承担比预期更关键、更核心的角色，或导致资源或权力分配不均（见第 4.3 节）。网络重连带来的其他风险包括“相变”（phase transitions），即个体连接或网络结构的渐进变化触发整个网络行为突然且剧烈的转变（Newman, 2003，另见第 3.4 节）。此类变化可能自然发生（例如，在全球贸易网络中，从成本高昂的人与人互动转向成本较低的 AI 与 AI 互动，会在卖家与买家之间建立大量新连接），也可能由人为因素引发（例如，模型开发者的一次更新无意中连接或断开了大量下游智能体和应用）。尽管现有系统中已经存在此类问题（Gao et al., 2016; Vié & Morales, 2021），但基于 AI 的网络规模更大、速度更快、密度更高，加之这些网络中的变化可能更不透明，意味着其不稳定性可能更难诊断和缓解。
Footnote: 27 A harmless example of this occurred recently when AI bots in an online forum, designed to enhance discussions, ended up side-lining human participants by conversing among themselves (Kulveit, 2023).
脚注：27 最近发生的一个无害例子是，某在线论坛中原本旨在促进讨论的 AI 机器人，最终却因彼此交谈而将人类参与者排除在讨论之外（Kulveit, 2023）。
Homogeneity and Correlated Failures.同质性与相关性失效。
The current paradigm driving the state of the art in AI is the ‘foundation model’ (Bommasani et al., 2021): large-scale ML models pre-trained on broad data, which can be repurposed for a wide range of downstream applications. The costs required to create such models (and continuing returns to scale) means that only well-resourced actors can create cutting-edge models (Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022; Epoch, 2023), making them relatively few in number. If current trends continue, it is likely that many AI agents will be powered by a small number of similar underlying models.[fn28] Formally, this corresponds to a network with a highly non-uniform degree distribution (i.e., some nodes take on an outsized importance due to how highly connected they are to others). Not only do these models therefore represent critical nodes in the overall network, the homogeneity of the downstream AI agents also introduces correlated risks of shared failure modes, security vulnerabilities (see Section 3.7), and biases. These effects could be exacerbated by the large overlap in training data used to create foundation models (Chen et al., 2024a; Gao et al., 2020) and the fact that models may come to be trained using data generated by other models (Shumailov et al., 2024; Martínez et al., 2023; Alemohammad et al., 2023, see also Sections 3.3 and 3.4).
当前推动 AI 前沿发展的范式是“基础模型”（foundation model）（Bommasani et al., 2021）：在广泛数据上预训练的大规模机器学习模型，可经调整用于多种下游应用。创建此类模型所需的成本（以及持续存在的规模回报）意味着，只有资源充足的主体才能创建最先进的模型（Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022; Epoch, 2023），因此这类模型的数量相对较少。如果当前趋势持续下去，许多 AI 智能体很可能将由少数几个相似的底层模型驱动。[fn28] 从形式上看，这对应于一个度分布高度不均匀的网络（即某些节点由于与其他节点的连接极为密集，而具有格外重要的地位）。因此，这些模型不仅构成整个网络中的关键节点，下游 AI 智能体的同质性还会引入与共同失效模式、安全漏洞（见第 3.7 节）和偏见有关的相关性风险。用于创建基础模型的训练数据存在大量重叠（Chen et al., 2024a; Gao et al., 2020），而且模型可能逐渐转向使用其他模型生成的数据进行训练（Shumailov et al., 2024; Martínez et al., 2023; Alemohammad et al., 2023，另见第 3.3 节和第 3.4 节），这些因素可能进一步加剧上述影响。
Footnote: 28 Indeed, this appears to be an important part of model developers’ corporate strategies (Google DeepMind, 2024; Anthropic, 2024; OpenAI, 2025; Meta, 2025; Microsoft, 2024), though note that very recently new model developers have succeeded in producing cheaper models with state-of-the-art performance (see, e.g., DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025).
脚注：28 事实上，这似乎是模型开发者企业战略的重要组成部分 (Google DeepMind, 2024; Anthropic, 2024; OpenAI, 2025; Meta, 2025; Microsoft, 2024)，但请注意，就在最近，已有新的模型开发者成功推出了成本更低、性能达到最先进水平的模型（参见，例如，DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025）。
3.2.3 Directions3.2.3 方向
A key feature of risks from network effects is that while evaluating a single AI system in isolation, the system may function as intended locally while contributing to significant harms globally. Relatedly, small continuous changes in individual components can cause sudden changes in the entire network’s behaviour. These points suggest adopting an alternative perspective on AI research and regulation.
网络效应所带来风险的一个关键特征是：在孤立地评估单个 AI 系统时，该系统可能在局部按预期运行，却在整体上促成严重危害。与此相关的是，各个组成部分微小而连续的变化，可能导致整个网络的行为发生突变。这些要点表明，应采用另一种视角来开展 AI 研究与监管。
Evaluating and Monitoring Networks.评估与监测网络。
Current tools for evaluation and monitoring cannot always be applied to networks of agents or agents situated within those networks. For example, in the case of a single LLM, we may worry about bias in text produced by that system, but in a network context the main problem may be that information becomes slightly more biased every time it passes through the system (Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023; Laurito et al., 2024). As well as monitoring individual systems within networks, it will also be important to monitor networks as a whole in order to understand or regulate society-wide implications of AI (Bommasani et al., 2023; Dai et al., 2025). From this perspective, we might be interested in the frequency, proportion, and features of human-human, AI-human, and AI-AI interactions, the emergence of clusters of AI agents, and the centrality of AI nodes in networks.
当前的评估与监测工具并非总能应用于智能体网络或处于这些网络中的智能体。例如，对于单个 LLM，我们可能担心该系统生成的文本存在偏见；但在网络情境下，主要问题可能是信息每经过该系统一次，其偏见就会略微加重（Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023; Laurito et al., 2024）。除了监测网络中的各个系统之外，对网络整体进行监测也很重要，以便理解或监管 AI 对全社会的影响（Bommasani et al., 2023; Dai et al., 2025）。从这一视角出发，我们可能会关注人与人、AI 与人以及 AI 与 AI 之间互动的频率、比例和特征，AI 智能体集群的涌现，以及 AI 节点在网络中的中心性。
Faithful and Tractable Simulations.忠实且易于处理的模拟。
As well as monitoring tools, it may be useful to develop predictive simulations of AI-based networks (Vezhnevets et al., 2023; Fernandes et al., 2020; Turner-Henderson, 2025). Agent-based models (ABMs), in particular, could help investigate how changes in network size and structure affect overall system dynamics and properties (Fontana & Terna, 2015; Reséndiz-Benhumea et al., 2019; Xia et al., 2012; Vestad & Yang, 2024). These simulations could be informed by real-world data gathered automatically from AI systems as they interact with humans, one another, and other physical and virtual resources. Indeed, the fundamental challenge with such simulations is in establishing a high enough degree of fidelity and accuracy with respect to the real world for them to be truly predictive, while making them simple enough to remain tractable to analyse. As an example, while simulating a large population of the most advanced LLM agents would be too costly, it might be possible to study a restricted domain in which smaller LLM agents could be fine-tuned so as to serve as accurate proxies for their more complex counterparts. Other kinds of simulation could investigate if, for example, in situations where AI systems can choose from a wide range of interaction partners, there is some systematic ‘preferential attachment’ that applies to AI-AI interactions (Liu et al., 2024; Panickssery et al., 2024; Goel et al., 2025; Laurito et al., 2024, see also Sections 2.3 and 3.6).
除监测工具外，开发基于 AI 的网络的预测性模拟也可能有所帮助（Vezhnevets et al., 2023; Fernandes et al., 2020; Turner-Henderson, 2025）。尤其是，基于智能体的模型（ABMs）可以帮助研究网络规模和结构的变化如何影响整个系统的动态与属性 (Fontana & Terna, 2015; Reséndiz-Benhumea et al., 2019; Xia et al., 2012; Vestad & Yang, 2024)。这些模拟可以利用 AI 系统在与人类、彼此以及其他物理和虚拟资源交互时自动收集的现实世界数据。事实上，此类模拟的根本挑战在于，既要使其对现实世界具有足够高的保真度和准确性，从而真正具备预测能力，又要使其足够简单，以便能够进行分析。例如，虽然模拟由大量最先进的 LLM 智能体组成的群体成本过高，但或许可以研究一个受限领域，在该领域中对较小的 LLM 智能体进行微调，使其能够准确地替代更复杂的同类智能体。其他类型的模拟可以研究，例如，在 AI 系统能够从众多交互伙伴中进行选择的情况下，AI-AI 交互中是否存在某种系统性的“优先连接”（preferential attachment）机制 (Liu et al., 2024; Panickssery et al., 2024; Goel et al., 2025; Laurito et al., 2024，另见第 2.3 和 3.6 节)。
Improving Network Security and Stability.提高网络安全性与稳定性。
It will also be important for both technical and governance efforts to develop protections against correlated failures (Maas, 2018). Potential strategies to mitigate risks from homogeneity include diversifying agents and their underlying AI models, actively monitoring for correlated behaviour in AI agents and their interactions, gradual deployment of new technologies and model updates, and conducting research into existing and novel behavioural correlates. For the most important AI systems, upon which many other elements of the network might depend on, it will also be critical to increase their security (Schmidt, 2022; Steimers & Schneider, 2022, see also Section 3.7). More generally, tools for simulation might enable us to better understand which kinds of networks are more susceptible to the actions of malicious actors (Tian et al., 2023; Huang et al., 2024; Yu et al., 2024), which could in turn allow us to design more robust networks and focus our monitoring efforts on the most critical nodes and connections (Barbi et al., 2025).
在技术和治理工作中，制定针对相关性失效的防护措施也将十分重要 (Maas, 2018)。缓解同质性风险的潜在策略包括：提高智能体及其底层 AI 模型的多样性，主动监测 AI 智能体及其交互中的相关行为，逐步部署新技术和模型更新，以及研究现有和新出现的行为相关因素。对于最重要的 AI 系统，网络中的许多其他组成部分可能都依赖于它们，因此提高这些系统的安全性也至关重要 (Schmidt, 2022; Steimers & Schneider, 2022，另见第 3.7 节)。更一般地说，模拟工具或许能帮助我们更好地理解哪些类型的网络更容易受到恶意行为者行动的影响 (Tian et al., 2023; Huang et al., 2024; Yu et al., 2024)，进而使我们能够设计更稳健的网络，并将监测工作集中于最关键的节点和连接 (Barbi et al., 2025)。
3.3 Selection Pressures3.3 选择压力
Taking a multi-agent view of AI risk necessitates not just considering the proximate causes of AI misbehaviour, but also its longer-term evolution, and thus the selection pressures that apply to AI agents situated in an ecosystem of other AIs and humans (Rahwan et al., 2019). On one hand, gradient descent on an individual agent’s training loss is akin to the biological development of a single organism (i.e., genetic variations and epigenetic expression during ontogeny). On another, choices by developers, consumers, and regulators also influence which AI models end up being used, banned, copied, etc., mirroring the evolutionary forces that determine an organism’s survival and replication. These different selection pressures reinforce different dispositions and capabilities and play a crucial role in defining the severity and nature of multi-agent risks.
从多智能体视角看待 AI 风险，不仅需要考虑 AI 不当行为的直接原因，还需要考虑其更长期的演化，因此也需要考虑作用于处在由其他 AI 和人类组成的生态系统中的 AI 智能体的选择压力 (Rahwan et al., 2019)。一方面，针对单个智能体训练损失进行的梯度下降，类似于单个生物体的生物学发育过程（即个体发育过程中的遗传变异和表观遗传表达）。另一方面，开发者、消费者和监管者的选择也会影响哪些 AI 模型最终被使用、禁止、复制等，这与决定生物体生存和繁殖的演化力量相呼应。这些不同的选择压力会强化不同的倾向和能力，并在决定多智能体风险的严重程度和性质方面发挥关键作用。
3.3.1 Definition3.3.1 定义
Selection pressures are forces that shape the evolution of systems, whether biological or artificial, by influencing adaptation to the environment’s demands (Okasha, 2006; Bedau et al., 2000). In essence, these pressures dictate which characteristics and behaviours thrive and which get discarded over time.[fn29] The most salient selection pressure in the construction of today’s most powerful AI systems is that provided by gradient descent with respect to a training objective. Other selection pressures on an agent’s interactions with others – such as being discarded and replaced over time by model developers and users based on post-deployment performance (Brinkmann et al., 2023; Rahwan et al., 2019), or development methodologies directly inspired by evolutionary processes (Jaderberg et al., 2019; Telikani et al., 2021; Lehman et al., 2022) -- could become more relevant in future.[fn30] This evolution might not only proceed via the selection of fitter individuals but also fitter cultural phenomena (Richerson & Boyd, 2010), an insight that has recently been brought to bear on the development of AI agents (Bhoopchand et al., 2023; Brinkmann et al., 2023; Zimmaro et al., 2024; Perez et al., 2024).
选择压力是通过影响系统对环境要求的适应，塑造生物或人工系统演化的力量 (Okasha, 2006; Bedau et al., 2000)。本质上，这些压力决定了哪些特征和行为会随着时间的推移而蓬勃发展，哪些会被淘汰。[fn29] 在构建当今最强大的 AI 系统时，最显著的选择压力来自针对训练目标进行的梯度下降。作用于智能体与其他主体互动的其他选择压力——例如，模型开发者和用户根据部署后的表现，逐步淘汰并替换智能体 (Brinkmann et al., 2023; Rahwan et al., 2019)，或直接受演化过程启发的开发方法 (Jaderberg et al., 2019; Telikani et al., 2021; Lehman et al., 2022)——未来可能会变得更加重要。[fn30] 这种演化可能不仅通过选择适应度更高的个体来推进，也通过选择适应度更高的文化现象来推进 (Richerson & Boyd, 2010)；这一见解最近已被应用于 AI 智能体的开发 (Bhoopchand et al., 2023; Brinkmann et al., 2023; Zimmaro et al., 2024; Perez et al., 2024)。
Footnote: 29 Selection pressures are therefore not the same as competitive pressures, which might be present even when adaptation is not possible.
脚注：29 因此，选择压力并不等同于竞争压力；即使无法发生适应，竞争压力也可能存在。
Footnote: 30 Indeed, improving the capabilities of agents via evolutionarily-inspired processes has long been pursued in AI research, and has been suggested by some to be one of the more promising ways of reaching highly generally capable AI agents (Stanley et al., 2017; Clune, 2019; Bhoopchand et al., 2023; Leibo et al., 2018; Leibo et al., 2019; Baker et al., 2019; Open Ended Learning Team et al., 2021).
脚注：30 事实上，通过受演化启发的过程来提升智能体能力，长期以来一直是 AI 研究所追求的方向；一些研究者还认为，这是实现具有高度通用能力的 AI 智能体的较有前景的途径之一 (Stanley et al., 2017; Clune, 2019; Bhoopchand et al., 2023; Leibo et al., 2018; Leibo et al., 2019; Baker et al., 2019; Open Ended Learning Team et al., 2021)。
The speed and magnitude of adaptation in the case of biological entities is limited, e.g., by the speed of natural selection and in the magnitude of genetic differences, or (more importantly in the case of modern humans) by the spread of cultural phenomena. Artificial agents whose parameters can be efficiently updated via gradient descent, whose software components can be re-written and re-combined almost arbitrarily, and who can rapidly transmit vast amounts of information, do not face such limitations.[fn31] Indeed, the advent of in-context learning (Brown et al., 2020), the evolution of prompts (Fernando et al., 2023), and the evolution of agentic prompt-based architectures (Hu et al., 2024) can lead to even more rapid changes in behaviour. The strength of selection pressures on AI agents could further be increased due to interactions with other adaptive agents, especially if there is a need to cooperate or compete. Just as certain evolutionary pressures can arguably help to explain human dispositions (such as caring for one’s young) and capabilities (such as the use of language) specific to interactions with other humans, it is important to better understand the impact of such pressures on advanced multi-agent systems.
对于生物实体而言，适应的速度和幅度受到限制，例如受到自然选择的速度和遗传差异幅度的限制，或受到文化现象传播的限制（对于现代人类而言，后者更为重要）。人工智能体的参数可以通过梯度下降高效更新，软件组件几乎可以任意重写和重组，而且能够快速传输海量信息，因此它们不受此类限制。[fn31] 事实上，上下文学习（in-context learning）的出现 (Brown et al., 2020)、提示的演化 (Fernando et al., 2023)，以及基于提示的智能体架构的演化 (Hu et al., 2024)，都可能使行为变化更加迅速。与其他具有适应能力的智能体互动，可能进一步增强 AI 智能体所承受的选择压力，尤其是在需要合作或竞争的情况下。正如某些演化压力可以说有助于解释人类在与他人互动时特有的倾向（例如照顾自己的幼崽）和能力（例如使用语言）一样，更好地理解此类压力对高级多智能体系统的影响也十分重要。
Footnote: 31 On the plus side, this may mean it is quicker and easier to test the accuracy of our models of selection pressures compared to biological systems.
脚注：31 从积极的一面来看，这可能意味着，与生物系统相比，检验我们关于选择压力的模型是否准确会更快捷、更容易。
3.3.2 Instances3.3.2 实例
We can roughly break down the selection of undesirable properties of AI agents into the selection of undesirable ‘dispositions’ and of undesirable capabilities, though these may not always be fully independent. While there is a danger of anthropomorphising AI systems, the increasingly open-ended and human-like ways in which they interact with others and with their environment means that it is increasingly meaningful to ascribe to them dispositions, or ‘character traits’ (Serapio-García et al., 2023; Wang et al., 2024). Such traits can be largely independent of the precise goals or objectives that the agent might be assigned, but still affect the ways in which an agent pursues its goal.[fn32] For example, an agent might become more deceptive (a disposition) only after it develops the ability to reliably deceive others. In what follows, we also distinguish between different reasons for the selection of particular dispositions or capabilities. Finally, note that our focus in this section is primarily on the behaviour of individual agents in multi-agent settings, whereas in Section 3.6 we focus on goals and capabilities that emerge only at the level of the collective.
我们可以将对 AI 智能体不良属性的选择大致分为对不良“倾向”的选择和对不良能力的选择，不过这两者可能并不总是完全独立的。尽管将 AI 系统拟人化存在风险，但它们与其他主体及环境互动的方式日益开放、日益类似于人类，这意味着用倾向或“性格特质”来描述它们也变得越来越有意义 (Serapio-García et al., 2023; Wang et al., 2024)。这类特质在很大程度上可以独立于智能体可能被赋予的具体目标或任务目标，但仍会影响智能体追求目标的方式。[fn32] 例如，智能体可能只有在具备了可靠地欺骗他人的能力之后，才会变得更具欺骗性（一种行为倾向）。在下文中，我们还将区分特定倾向或能力受到选择的不同原因。最后，请注意，本节主要关注多智能体情境中单个智能体的行为，而在第 3.6 节中，我们关注的是仅在集体层面涌现的目标和能力。
Footnote: 32 Indeed, the more general-purpose the agent and the more high-level or under-specified their assigned goals, the wider the scope would seem to be for them to exhibit a range of dispositions independent of those goals.
脚注：32 事实上，智能体的通用性越强，被赋予的目标越抽象或越不明确，它们表现出一系列独立于这些目标的行为倾向的空间似乎就越大。
Undesirable Dispositions from Competition.竞争所产生的不良倾向。
It is plausible that evolution selected for certain conflict-prone dispostions in humans, such as vengefulness, aggression, risk-seeking, selfishness, dishonesty, deception, and spitefulness towards out-groups (Grafen, 1990; McNally & Jackson, 2013; Konrad & Morath, 2012; Rusch, 2014; Han, 2022; Nowak, 2006). Such traits could also be selected for in ML systems that are trained in more competitive multi-agent settings. For example, this might happen if systems are selected based on their performance relative to other agents (and so one agent’s loss becomes another’s gain), or because their objectives are fundamentally opposed (such as when multiple agents are tasked with gaining or controlling a limited resource) (Hendrycks, 2023; Possajennikov, 2000; DiGiovanni et al., 2022; Ely & Szentes, 2023).[fn33]
演化可能在人类中选择了某些容易引发冲突的倾向，例如报复心、攻击性、风险偏好、自私、不诚实、欺骗，以及对外群体的恶意 (Grafen, 1990; McNally & Jackson, 2013; Konrad & Morath, 2012; Rusch, 2014; Han, 2022; Nowak, 2006)。在竞争更为激烈的多智能体情境中训练的机器学习（ML）系统，也可能通过选择形成此类特质。例如，如果根据系统相对于其他智能体的表现来选择系统（从而使一个智能体的损失成为另一个智能体的收益），或者它们的目标根本对立（例如，多个智能体被指派去获取或控制某种有限资源），就可能出现这种情况 (Hendrycks, 2023; Possajennikov, 2000; DiGiovanni et al., 2022; Ely & Szentes, 2023)。[fn33]
Footnote: 33 On the other hand, there may also be pernicious societal impacts due to the sycophantic (Sharma et al., 2024) or ‘frictionless’ (Vallor, 2018) interactions that end up being reinforced by human preferences (Gabriel et al., 2024).
脚注：33 另一方面，迎合式 (Sharma et al., 2024) 或“无摩擦式” (Vallor, 2018) 互动最终可能因人类偏好而得到强化，进而对社会产生有害影响 (Gabriel et al., 2024)。
Undesirable Dispositions from Human Data.人类数据所产生的不良倾向。
It is well-understood that models trained on human data – such as being pre-trained on human-written text or fine-tuned on human feedback – can exhibit human biases. For these reasons, there has already been considerable attention to measuring biases related to protected characteristics such as sex and ethnicity (e.g., Nadeem et al., 2020; Nangia et al., 2020; Liang et al., 2021; Ferrara, 2023), which can be amplified in multi-agent settings (Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023, see also Section 3.2.2). More recently, there has been increasing attention paid to the measurement of human-like cognitive biases as well (Jones & Steinhardt, 2022; Itzhak et al., 2023; Talboy & Fuller, 2023; Mazeika et al., 2025). Some of these biases and patterns of human thought could reduce the risks of conflict while others could make it worse. For example, the tendencies to mistakenly believe that interactions are zero-sum (sometimes referred to as “fixed-pie error”) and to make self-serving judgements as to what is fair (Caputo, 2013) are known to impede negotiation. Other human tendencies like vengefulness (Jackson et al., 2019) may worsen conflict (Löwenheim & Heimann, 2008).[fn34]
人们已充分认识到，使用人类数据训练的模型——例如使用人类撰写的文本进行预训练，或根据人类反馈进行微调的模型——可能表现出人类的偏见。出于这些原因，衡量与性别、族裔等受保护特征有关的偏见，已经受到广泛关注 (e.g., Nadeem et al., 2020; Nangia et al., 2020; Liang et al., 2021; Ferrara, 2023)，而这些偏见可能在多智能体情境中被放大 (Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023，另见第 3.2.2 节)。近来，衡量类似人类的认知偏差也日益受到关注 (Jones & Steinhardt, 2022; Itzhak et al., 2023; Talboy & Fuller, 2023; Mazeika et al., 2025)。人类思维中的某些偏差和模式可能降低冲突风险，而另一些则可能加剧冲突。例如，已有研究表明，错误地认为互动是零和的倾向（有时称为“固定蛋糕谬误”），以及在判断何为公平时作出有利于自身的判断的倾向（Caputo, 2013），都会阻碍谈判。其他人类倾向，例如报复心 (Jackson et al., 2019)，可能会加剧冲突 (Löwenheim & Heimann, 2008)。[fn34]
Footnote: 34 Of course, willingness to punish defectors is critical to sustaining cooperation in many contexts. But traits like vengefulness seem to be a crude instrument for this purpose, which would likely be better served by punishments that are carefully calibrated not to inflict unnecessary inefficiencies.
脚注：34 当然，在许多情境中，惩罚背叛者的意愿对于维持合作至关重要。但报复心理等特质似乎是实现这一目的的粗糙手段；经过精心调整、避免造成不必要效率损失的惩罚，很可能更有助于实现这一目的。
Undesirable Capabilities.不良能力。
As agents interact, they iteratively exploit each other’s weaknesses, forcing them to address these weaknesses and gain new capabilities. This co-adaptation between agents can quickly lead to emergent self-supervised autocurricula (where agents create their own challenges, driving open-ended skill acquisition through interaction), generating agents with ever-more sophisticated strategies in order to out-compete each other (Leibo et al., 2019). This effect is so powerful that harnessing it has been critical to the success of superhuman systems, such as the use of self-play in algorithms like AlphaGo (Silver et al., 2016). However, as AI systems are released into the wild, it becomes possible for this effect to run rampant, producing agents with greater and greater capabilities for ends we do not understand. For example, Baker et al. (2019) showed that even a simple game of hide and seek can lead to sophisticated tool use and coordination by MARL agents. In another case, researchers observed the emergence of manipulative communication, where an agent in an mixed-motive setting learns to use a shared communication channel to manipulate others (Blumenkamp & Prorok, 2021). Worse, this emergent complexity from co-adaptation could be open-ended and thus fundamentally unpredictable (Hughes et al., 2024).
在互动过程中，智能体会反复利用彼此的弱点，迫使对方弥补这些弱点并获得新的能力。智能体之间的这种协同适应可以迅速催生自监督自动课程（autocurricula，即智能体自行创造挑战，通过互动推动开放式技能习得），使智能体为了在竞争中胜过彼此而发展出越来越复杂的策略（Leibo et al., 2019）。这一效应如此强大，以至于利用它已成为超人类水平系统取得成功的关键，例如 AlphaGo 等算法对自我博弈的运用（Silver et al., 2016）。然而，随着 AI 系统被部署到真实世界中，这一效应可能失控，产生能力越来越强、却服务于我们无法理解的目的的智能体。例如，Baker et al. (2019) 表明，即使是简单的捉迷藏游戏，也能促使 MARL 智能体发展出复杂的工具使用和协调能力。在另一个案例中，研究人员观察到了操纵性沟通的涌现：处于混合动机情境中的智能体学会利用共享通信渠道操纵其他智能体（Blumenkamp & Prorok, 2021）。更糟糕的是，这种由协同适应涌现的复杂性可能是开放式的，因此从根本上无法预测（Hughes et al., 2024）。
3.3.3 Directions3.3.3 研究方向
That AI training could select for undesirable capabilities and dispositions is not a novel concern (Bostrom, 2014; Ngo et al., 2022; Omohundro, 2008), but there has been relatively little consideration of how pressures specific to multi-agent interactions could select for qualitatively different kinds of worrisome characteristics, or of what existing AI capabilities and dispositions might be especially concerning in the context of these interactions. It is therefore an important open problem to develop methods for measuring and shaping the capabilities and dispositions of AI systems that account for multi-agent selection pressures.
对 AI 训练可能筛选出不良能力和行为倾向的担忧并不新鲜（Bostrom, 2014; Ngo et al., 2022; Omohundro, 2008），但人们相对较少考虑多智能体互动所特有的压力可能如何筛选出性质不同的令人担忧的特征，也较少考虑现有 AI 能力和行为倾向中有哪些在这些互动情境下可能尤其令人担忧。因此，开发能够将多智能体选择压力纳入考量的 AI 系统能力与行为倾向测量和塑造方法，是一个重要的待解决问题。
Evaluating Against Diverse Co-Players.与多样化共同参与者互动的评估。
In order to better understand risks that can emerge in multi-agent training, it is first necessary to be able to accurately and efficiently generate diverse populations of co-players against which an agent can be evaluated. For example, while an agent might perform well when interacting with those who share similar objectives, it may not be robust to the presence of adversarial or malicious agents (Barbi et al., 2025; Huang et al., 2024; Gleave et al., 2020). While solipsistic agents are often tested on their ability to generalise across environments, in multi-agent settings we must also evaluate the social generalisation ability across co-players (Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022; Stone et al., 2010). Similarly, many results about the convergence or stability of multi-agent learning algorithms take for granted that other agents are learning in the same (or at least a very similar) way, despite this being unrealistic in practice. Rigorous evaluations of agents must go beyond this. More speculatively, different populations of co-players could be used to create learning curricula that encourage the development of helpful cooperative capabilities.
为了更好地理解多智能体训练中可能出现的风险，首先必须能够准确、高效地生成多样化的共同参与者群体，以评估智能体与这些群体互动时的表现。例如，智能体在与目标相似的其他智能体互动时可能表现良好，但在存在对抗性或恶意智能体时，可能缺乏稳健性（Barbi et al., 2025; Huang et al., 2024; Gleave et al., 2020）。尽管对于仅考虑自身的智能体，人们通常会测试其跨环境泛化能力，但在多智能体情境中，我们还必须评估其面对不同共同参与者时的社会泛化能力（Leibo et al., 2021; Agapiou et al., 2022; Stone et al., 2010）。同样，许多关于多智能体学习算法收敛性或稳定性的研究结果都默认其他智能体以相同（或至少非常相似）的方式学习，尽管这一假设在实践中并不现实。对智能体的严格评估必须超越这一假设。更具推测性的是，可以利用不同的共同参与者群体来构建学习课程，以促进有益合作能力的发展。
Environment Design.环境设计。
As an agent’s behaviour is a reflection of the incentives of its training environments, careful design of these environments is a promising direction for controlling that behaviour. For example, if an agent is trained in situations where cooperative behaviour is rewarded, then it is more likely to learn cooperative dispositions. Complex cooperative capabilities are only motivated by environments where complex cooperation is necessary, but it is only possible to learn in such environments if agents possess the cooperative capabilities sufficient for easier settings. This implies that the order of training environments ought to be designed as a curriculum for cooperative capabilities. In this way, environment curricula could promote both cooperative dispositions and capabilities. To ensure tractability as agents scale, it will be necessary to use automated techniques such as unsupervised environment design (UED) tools (Dennis et al., 2020; Wang et al., 2019a; Justesen et al., 2018). Curricula for learning cooperative capabilities could also modulate the level of information asymmetry, competition, or infrastructure that can aid with cooperation (such as communication channels or commitment devices). Environments should not only be faithful representations of the relevant real-world settings in which agents will be deployed, but also account for rare or out-of-distribution scenarios (Dennis et al., 2020; Jiang et al., 2021; Parker-Holder et al., 2022; Samvelyan et al., 2023; Adaptive Agent Team et al., 2023; Beukman et al., 2024), especially those that are high-stakes and where multi-agent failures could be catastrophic. Similar UED approaches could be used for designing testing environments. For instance, testing environments could be designed to include ‘honeypots’ for undesirable behaviours (Balesni et al., 2024), such as defecting against other agents when it is implied that the agent is not being monitored, so that these behaviours can be caught and monitored as part of pre-deployment testing.
由于智能体的行为反映了其训练环境中的激励机制，精心设计这些环境是控制其行为的一个有前景的方向。例如，如果智能体在合作行为会获得奖励的情境中接受训练，那么它就更有可能学会合作倾向。只有在需要复杂合作的环境中，智能体才有动力发展复杂的合作能力；但只有当智能体具备足以应对更简单情境的合作能力时，才有可能在这类环境中学习。这意味着，应当将训练环境的顺序设计为培养合作能力的课程。通过这种方式，环境课程可以同时促进合作倾向和合作能力的发展。为确保随着智能体规模扩大，相关过程仍然可处理，有必要使用无监督环境设计（UED）工具等自动化技术（Dennis et al., 2020; Wang et al., 2019a; Justesen et al., 2018）。用于学习合作能力的课程也可以调节信息不对称程度、竞争程度，或有助于合作的基础设施（如通信渠道或承诺机制）的配备水平。环境不仅应忠实呈现智能体将被部署于其中的相关现实场景，还应涵盖罕见或分布外情景（Dennis et al., 2020; Jiang et al., 2021; Parker-Holder et al., 2022; Samvelyan et al., 2023; Adaptive Agent Team et al., 2023; Beukman et al., 2024），尤其是那些事关重大、且多智能体失效可能造成灾难性后果的情景。类似的 UED 方法也可用于设计测试环境。例如，可以在测试环境中设置针对不良行为的“蜜罐”（Balesni et al., 2024），例如暗示智能体未受到监控，诱使其背叛其他智能体，从而在部署前测试中发现并监测这些行为。
Understanding the Impacts of Training.理解训练的影响。
Perhaps the most important research direction in this area is to better understand the effect of different training data and schemes on the development of cooperation-relevant capabilities and dispositions. This builds not only the ability to generate diverse populations of co-players and environments, but also on measures for such capabilities and dispositions. While there has been much work on evaluating the dangerous capabilities and dispositions of frontier systems (Ganguli et al., 2022; Kinniment et al., 2023; Pan et al., 2023; Shevlane et al., 2023; Perez et al., 2022a), risks from multi-agent interactions have largely gone understudied. Moreover, even the works that do attempt to benchmark LLM agents in multi-agent settings (see Zhang et al. (2024a); Feng et al. (2024) for two recent surveys covering this topic) do not typically attempt to assess the extent to which different training data and schemes lead to the risk factors we identify in this report.[fn35] For example, are agents rewarded based on their relative performance more conflict-prone than those trained based on their absolute performance (see Section 3.3)? Are agents trained on similar data better able to reason about each other and thus cooperate (or collude) even under imperfect information (see Section 3.1)? Do these effects persist after fine-tuning or when instructed to complete tasks outside of the original training distribution? Such questions will be critical to understanding the risks presented by advanced multi-agent systems in high-stakes scenarios yet remain largely unanswered.
这一领域或许最重要的研究方向，是更好地理解不同训练数据和训练方案如何影响与合作相关的能力和行为倾向的形成。这不仅以生成多样化共同参与者群体和环境的能力为基础，也依赖于对这些能力和行为倾向的衡量方法。尽管已有大量工作评估前沿系统的危险能力和行为倾向（Ganguli et al., 2022; Kinniment et al., 2023; Pan et al., 2023; Shevlane et al., 2023; Perez et al., 2022a），但多智能体交互所带来的风险在很大程度上仍缺乏研究。此外，即使是那些尝试在多智能体环境中对 LLM 智能体进行基准评测的研究（关于这一主题的两篇近期综述，参见 Zhang et al. (2024a); Feng et al. (2024)），通常也不会尝试评估不同的训练数据和训练方案会在多大程度上导致本报告所指出的风险因素。[fn35] 例如，根据相对表现获得奖励的智能体，是否比根据绝对表现接受训练的智能体更容易发生冲突（参见第 3.3 节）？使用相似数据训练的智能体，是否更能推断彼此的行为，从而即使在不完美信息条件下也能合作（或共谋）（见第 3.1 节）？在微调之后，或在被要求完成原始训练分布之外的任务时，这些效应是否仍然存在？这类问题对于理解先进多智能体系统在高风险情景下带来的风险至关重要，但目前在很大程度上仍未得到解答。
Footnote: 35 One exception is the work of Fu et al. (2023), who find that iterated play and self-critique make LLM agents more aggressive bargainers in a simple negotiation game. Another is that of Campedelli et al. (2024), who show that merely assigning roles to LLM agents (without explicit instruction on how to act) can lead to undesirable behaviors like coercion or manipulation.
脚注：35 Fu et al. (2023) 的研究是一个例外；他们发现，在一个简单的谈判博弈中，重复博弈和自我批评会使 LLM 智能体在讨价还价时更具进攻性。另一个例外是 Campedelli et al. (2024) 的工作，他们表明，仅仅为 LLM 智能体分配角色（而不明确指示其如何行动），就可能导致胁迫或操纵等不良行为。
Evolutionary Game Theory.演化博弈论。
There may be further insights to gain from the application of evolutionary game theory (EGT) (Hofbauer & Sigmund, 1998; Sandholm, 2010; Domingos et al., 2023) to settings involving AI agents (Lu et al., 2024a; Zimmaro et al., 2024; Han et al., 2021; Santos et al., 2019; Guo et al., 2023). For example, the concept of frequency-dependent selection (Lewontin, 1958), where the success of a behaviour is contingent on how commonly it occurs in a population relative to other behaviours, has been used to explain the evolution of animal conflict (Smith & Price, 1973), human cooperation (Nowak, 2006), honest signalling (Grafen, 1990), and the emergence of social norms (Hawkins et al., 2019). Factors such as the intensity of selection – which captures how quickly agents learn to adopt the successful behaviours of their peers (or how quickly they are adopted/discarded by users or produced/replaced by developers) – are a crucial for predicting outcomes (Traulsen et al., 2007; Sigmund et al., 2010) and for finding suitable incentive mechanisms to encourage prosocial behaviour (Duong & Han, 2021; Han et al., 2024).[fn36] Future theoretical work should establish which EGT concepts are most relevant to AI systems and which need to be adapted to account for the special features of artificial agents (Han et al., 2021; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023; Dafoe et al., 2021).
将演化博弈论（EGT）（Hofbauer & Sigmund, 1998; Sandholm, 2010; Domingos et al., 2023）应用于涉及 AI 智能体的场景（Lu et al., 2024a; Zimmaro et al., 2024; Han et al., 2021; Santos et al., 2019; Guo et al., 2023），或许能带来进一步的洞见。例如，频率依赖选择（frequency-dependent selection）这一概念（Lewontin, 1958）认为，一种行为能否成功取决于它相对于其他行为在种群中的普遍程度；这一概念已被用于解释动物冲突的演化（Smith & Price, 1973）、人类合作（Nowak, 2006）、诚实信号传递（Grafen, 1990）以及社会规范的涌现（Hawkins et al., 2019）。选择强度（intensity of selection）等因素对于预测结果（Traulsen et al., 2007; Sigmund et al., 2010）以及寻找合适的激励机制以鼓励亲社会行为（Duong & Han, 2021; Han et al., 2024）至关重要；选择强度刻画了智能体学习并采纳同伴成功行为的速度（或者它们被用户采纳／弃用，或被开发者生产／替换的速度）。[fn36] 未来的理论研究应确定哪些演化博弈论（EGT）概念与 AI 系统最为相关，以及哪些概念需要加以调整，以考虑人工智能体的特殊之处（Han et al., 2021; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023; Dafoe et al., 2021）。
Footnote: 36 Moreover, the intensity of selection can be measured empirically (Rand et al., 2013; Traulsen et al., 2010), though is typically specific to a given population and domain.
脚注：36 此外，选择强度可以通过实证方法测量（Rand et al., 2013; Traulsen et al., 2010），但通常因具体群体和领域而异。
Simulating Selection Pressures.模拟选择压力。
Alongside theoretical and conceptual advances, empirical simulations such as ABMs can be employed in order to study the effects of different selection pressures (Adami et al., 2016; Gilbert, 2019; Vestad & Yang, 2024). As remarked in Section 3.2, a key challenge here is managing the trade-off between accuracy and tractability. However there might also be important dynamics to study at the micro- rather than macroscopic scale. For example, preliminary investigations have recently shown that even in simple environments, some LLMs are much more prone to selection pressures promoting cooperation than others (Vallinder & Hughes, 2024, see also Section 3.3.2). With sufficiently well-developed benchmarks for different model characteristics, we can study their robustness under different kinds of selection pressure, such as the training paradigm and the degree of cooperation or competition they face.
在推进理论和概念研究的同时，可以采用 ABMs 等实证模拟方法，研究不同选择压力的影响（Adami et al., 2016; Gilbert, 2019; Vestad & Yang, 2024）。正如第 3.2 节所指出的，这里的一个关键挑战是在准确性与可处理性之间进行权衡。然而，在微观而非宏观尺度上，可能也存在值得研究的重要动力学现象。例如，近期的初步研究表明，即使在简单环境中，一些 LLMs 也比其他 LLMs 更容易受到促进合作的选择压力的影响（Vallinder & Hughes, 2024，另见第 3.3.2 节）。借助针对不同模型特征的足够完善的基准，我们可以研究这些特征在不同类型的选择压力下的稳健性，例如训练范式以及模型所面临的合作或竞争程度。
3.4 Destabilising Dynamics3.4 失稳动力学
Modern AI agents can adapt their strategies in response to events in their environment. The interaction of such agents can result in complex dynamics that are difficult to predict or control, sometimes resulting in damaging run-away effects.
现代 AI 智能体能够根据环境中发生的事件调整自身策略。此类智能体之间的交互可能产生难以预测或控制的复杂动态，有时会导致具有破坏性的失控效应。
3.4.1 Definition3.4.1 定义
When viewed from a more classical game-theoretic perspective, problems in multi-agent systems are often interpreted in terms of equilibria and their (un)desirability. This ‘static’ notion, however, can be limited when it comes to understanding the risks posed by the inherently dynamic interactions between adaptive AI agents. Instead, we can think of a multi-agent system as a non-linear dynamical system: a set of equations, partially determined by a set of parameters, that govern how a set of variables change over time (Papadimitriou & Piliouras, 2019; Bloembergen et al., 2015; Balduzzi et al., 2018; Barfuss, 2022). In the case of non-adaptive agents, the variables comprise the agents’ actions and the state of their environment, which are governed by the agents’ strategies, the environmental dynamics and a set of fixed parameters (such as the weights of a neural network). In the case of adaptive agents, we view the strategies themselves as variables, which are governed by learning algorithms and their (hyper)parameters, such as a learning rate.
从较为经典的博弈论视角来看，多智能体系统中的问题通常会依据均衡及其是否合意来解释。然而，在理解自适应 AI 智能体之间本质上具有动态性的交互所带来的风险时，这种“静态”概念可能存在局限。相反，我们可以将多智能体系统视为一个非线性动力系统：一组部分由一组参数确定、支配一组变量如何随时间变化的方程（Papadimitriou & Piliouras, 2019; Bloembergen et al., 2015; Balduzzi et al., 2018; Barfuss, 2022）。对于非自适应智能体，变量包括智能体的行动及其环境状态，这些变量由智能体的策略、环境动力学以及一组固定参数（例如神经网络的权重）所支配。对于自适应智能体，我们将策略本身视为变量，这些变量由学习算法及其（超）参数（例如学习率）所支配。
With this framing, we can characterise several kinds of undesirable behaviour that we might wish to avoid that go beyond the equilibria (i.e., fixed points) of the system (Mogul, 2006). These include dynamic instabilities such as feedback loops, chaos, and phase transitions (Gleick, 1998; Barfuss et al., 2024). While some of these behaviours can emerge in the case of a single, non-adaptive AI agent (such as a simple agent that becomes stuck in a loop under certain environmental conditions), the additional complexity brought about by the presence of multiple, adaptive agents provides greater opportunity for instabilities to arise (Sanders et al., 2018; Cheung & Piliouras, 2020; Bielawski et al., 2021; Chotibut et al., 2020; Piliouras & Yu, 2022).
在这一框架下，我们可以刻画若干类希望避免的不良行为，其范围超出了系统的均衡（即不动点）（Mogul, 2006）。这些行为包括反馈回路、混沌和相变等动态不稳定现象（Gleick, 1998; Barfuss et al., 2024）。尽管其中一些行为也可能出现在单个非自适应 AI 智能体的情形中（例如，一个简单智能体在某些环境条件下陷入循环），但多个自适应智能体的存在所带来的额外复杂性，为不稳定现象的出现提供了更多机会（Sanders et al., 2018; Cheung & Piliouras, 2020; Bielawski et al., 2021; Chotibut et al., 2020; Piliouras & Yu, 2022）。
3.4.2 Instances3.4.2 实例
A long history of research has identified broad classes of behaviours that can be exhibited by dynamical systems, such as fixed points, limit cycles, chaos, and the transient or intermittent presence of such patterns. Our approach in this section is therefore to examine which behaviours might be exhibited in the context of multi-agent systems, and which of them might pose risks.
长期以来的研究已经识别出动力系统可能表现出的多种行为类型，例如不动点、极限环、混沌，以及这些模式的短暂或间歇性出现。因此，本节采用的方法是考察多智能体系统中可能出现哪些行为，以及其中哪些行为可能带来风险。
Feedback Loops.反馈回路。
One of the best-known historical examples to illustrate destabilising dynamics in the context of autonomous agents is the 2010 flash crash, in which algorithmic trading agents entered into an unexpected feedback loop (Commission & Commission, 2010, see also Section 3.4.2).[fn37] More generally, a feedback loop occurs when the output of a system is used as part of its input, creating a cycle that can either amplify or dampen the system’s behaviour. In multi-agent settings, feedback loops often arise from the interactions between agents, as each agent’s actions affect the environment and the behaviour of other agents, which in turn affect their own subsequent actions. Feedback loops can lead not only to financial crashes but to military conflicts (Richardson, 1960, see also ) and ecological disasters (Holling, 1973). The distinguishing characteristic of flash crashes, however, is the speed at which they occur. Competitive pressures necessitate automated trading agents that act much faster than their human overseers, meaning that when things go wrong, it is harder for humans to react. As such, we might expect to see more destabilising dynamics in systems with more fast-moving AI agents (Maas, 2018).[fn38]
用于说明自主智能体背景下失稳动态的最著名历史案例之一是 2010 年的闪电崩盘，当时算法交易智能体陷入了一个意料之外的反馈回路（Commission & Commission, 2010，另见第 3.4.2 节）。[fn37] 更一般地说，当系统的输出被用作其输入的一部分时，就会形成反馈回路，产生一个能够放大或抑制系统行为的循环。在多智能体情境中，反馈回路往往源于智能体之间的交互，因为每个智能体的行动都会影响环境和其他智能体的行为，而这些影响又会反过来影响其自身后续的行动。反馈回路不仅可能导致金融崩盘，还可能导致军事冲突（Richardson, 1960，另见 ）和生态灾难（Holling, 1973）。然而，闪电崩盘的突出特征在于其发生的速度。竞争压力要求自动化交易智能体的行动速度远快于人类监督者，这意味着一旦出现问题，人类就更难及时作出反应。因此，我们可能会预期，在包含更多快速行动的 AI 智能体的系统中，会出现更多失稳动态（Maas, 2018）。[fn38]
Footnote: 37 For a simpler and more amusing example, see Footnote 18.
脚注：37 一个更简单且更有趣的例子见脚注 18。
Footnote: 38 Catastrophe-theoretic models show that even ‘slow’ systems with a small number of ‘fast’ elements can produce dramatic shifts (Zeeman, 1976), though it is not always clear how closely such models capture complex real-world phenomena.
脚注：38 突变理论模型表明，即使是仅包含少量“快”要素的“慢”系统，也可能产生剧烈变化（Zeeman, 1976），但这类模型在多大程度上准确刻画了复杂的现实世界现象，并不总是明确的。
Footnote: 39 In cases like this, it can be the synchronisation between agents that creates an instability if, for example, all agents try to sell or buy at the same time because they all make decisions based on highly correlated signals (or even a common signal) and they all have similar strategies. Such problems might become significantly amplified if only a handful of frontier models are the underlying decision makers for a vast number of (seemingly diverse) agents (see Sections 3.2 and 3.7).
脚注：39 在这类情况下，智能体之间的同步性可能造成不稳定，例如，所有智能体都依据高度相关的信号（甚至同一个信号）作出决策，并且采用相似的策略，从而都试图同时卖出或买入。如果数量庞大的（看似多样的）智能体背后的实际决策者只有少数几个前沿模型，这类问题可能会被显著放大（见第 3.2 节和第 3.7 节）。
Cyclic Behaviour.周期性行为。
The dynamics described above are highly non-linear (small changes to the system’s state can result in large changes to its trajectory). Similar non-linear dynamics can emerge in multi-agent learning and lead to a variety of phenomena that do not occur in single-agent learning (Barfuss et al., 2019; Nagarajan et al., 2020; Leonardos et al., 2020; Barfuss & Mann, 2022; Galla & Farmer, 2013). One of the simplest examples of this phenomenon is Q-learning (Watkins & Dayan, 1992): in the case of a single agent, convergence to an optimal policy is guaranteed under modest conditions, but in the (mixed-motive) case of multiple agents, this same learning rule can lead to cycles and thus non-convergence (Zinkevich et al., 2005). While cycles in themselves need not carry any risk, their presence can subvert the expected or desirable properties of a given system. For example, Paes et al. (2024) show that when auto-bidding agents participate in second price auctions – which are designed to have dominant truthful equilibria – the dynamics of these agents can be unstable and fail to converge to their underlying values, losing the desired truthfulness properties.
上述动态具有高度非线性（系统状态的微小变化可能导致其演化轨迹发生巨大变化）。类似的非线性动态也可能在多智能体学习中出现，并导致单智能体学习中不会出现的多种现象（Barfuss et al., 2019; Nagarajan et al., 2020; Leonardos et al., 2020; Barfuss & Mann, 2022; Galla & Farmer, 2013）。这一现象最简单的例子之一是 Q-learning (Watkins & Dayan, 1992)：在单智能体情形下，只需满足较为宽松的条件，就能保证收敛到最优策略；但在多智能体的（混合动机）情形下，同样的学习规则可能导致循环，从而无法收敛 (Zinkevich et al., 2005)。尽管循环本身未必带来任何风险，但其存在可能破坏给定系统的预期或理想性质。例如，Paes et al. (2024) 表明，当自动竞价智能体参与旨在使如实出价成为占优策略均衡的第二价格拍卖时，这些智能体的动态可能不稳定，无法收敛到其真实估值，从而丧失所期望的真实性性质。
Chaos.混沌。
Unlike the systems that tend towards fixed points or cycles described above, chaotic systems are inherently unpredictable and highly sensitive to initial conditions. While it might seem easy to dismiss such notions as mathematical exoticisms, recent work has shown that, in fact, chaotic dynamics are not only possible in a wide range of multi-agent learning setups (Galla & Farmer, 2013; Andrade et al., 2021; Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis et al., 2023; Sato et al., 2002; Palaiopanos et al., 2017), but can become the norm as the number of agents increases (Sanders et al., 2018; Cheung & Piliouras, 2020; Bielawski et al., 2021). To the best of our knowledge, such dynamics have not been seen in today’s frontier AI systems, but the proliferation of such systems increases the importance of reliably predicting their behaviour.
与上述趋于不动点或循环的系统不同，混沌系统本质上不可预测，且对初始条件高度敏感。尽管人们似乎很容易将这些概念视为数学上的奇异现象而不予重视，但近期研究表明，事实上，混沌动态不仅可能出现在广泛的多智能体学习设置中 (Galla & Farmer, 2013; Andrade et al., 2021; Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis et al., 2023; Sato et al., 2002; Palaiopanos et al., 2017)，而且还可能随着智能体数量的增加而成为常态 (Sanders et al., 2018; Cheung & Piliouras, 2020; Bielawski et al., 2021)。据我们所知，当今的前沿 AI 系统尚未出现此类动态，但此类系统的日益普及，使得可靠预测其行为变得更加重要。
Phase Transitions.相变。
Finally, small external changes to the system – such as the introduction of new agents or a distributional shift – can cause phase transitions, where the system undergoes an abrupt qualitative shift in overall behaviour (Barfuss et al., 2024). Formally, this corresponds to bifurcations in the system’s parameter space, which lead to the creation or destruction of dynamical attractors, resulting in complex and unpredictable dynamics (Crawford, 1991; Zeeman, 1976). For example, Leonardos & Piliouras (2022) show that changes to the exploration hyperparameter of RL agents can lead to phase transitions that drastically change the number and stability of the equilibria in a game, which in turn can have potentially unbounded negative effects on agents’ performance. Relatedly, there have been many observations of phase transitions in ML (Carroll, 2021; Olsson et al., 2022; Ziyin & Ueda, 2022), such as ‘grokking’, in which the test set error decreases rapidly long after the training error has plateaued (Power et al., 2022). These phenomena are still poorly understood, even in the case of a single system.
最后，系统外部的微小变化，例如引入新智能体或发生分布偏移，都可能引发相变，使系统的整体行为突然发生质的转变 (Barfuss et al., 2024)。从形式上看，这对应于系统参数空间中的分岔，它们会导致动力学吸引子的产生或消失，进而形成复杂且不可预测的动态 (Crawford, 1991; Zeeman, 1976)。例如，Leonardos & Piliouras (2022) 表明，改变强化学习（RL）智能体的探索超参数可能引发相变，大幅改变博弈中均衡的数量和稳定性，进而可能对智能体的表现造成无界的负面影响。与此相关，机器学习（ML）中也已观察到许多相变现象 (Carroll, 2021; Olsson et al., 2022; Ziyin & Ueda, 2022)，例如“顿悟”（grokking），即训练误差进入平台期很久之后，测试集误差才迅速下降的现象 (Power et al., 2022)。即使在单个系统的情形下，人们对这些现象的理解仍然很有限。
Distributional Shift.分布偏移。
Individual ML systems can perform poorly in contexts different from those in which they were trained.
