A key source of these distributional shifts is the actions and adaptations of other agents (Papoudakis et al., 2019; Piliouras & Yu, 2022; Narang et al., 2023), which in single-agent approaches are often simply or ignored or at best modelled exogenously. Indeed, the sheer number and variance of behaviours that can be exhibited other agents means that multi-agent systems pose an especially challenging generalisation problem for individual learners (Agapiou et al., 2022; Leibo et al., 2021; Stone et al., 2010). While distributional shifts can cause issues in common-interest settings (see Section 2.1), they are more worrisome in mixed-motive settings since the ability of agents to cooperate depends not only on the ability to coordinate on one of many arbitrary conventions (which might be easily resolved by a common language), but on their beliefs about what solutions other agents will find acceptable. For example, training a negotiating agent on a distribution of counterparts with too little diversity in their negotiating tactics can lead to catastrophic overconfidence in high-stakes settings (cf. Stastny et al., 2021), which might already have little precedent in the training data. These issues may be aggravated by the fact that multi-agent systems can be highly dynamic (Papoudakis et al., 2019), as AI agents or their designers will be incentivised to continually adapt to the behaviour of other agents. These effects might also be exacerbated by the fact that models may come to be trained using data generated by other models (Shumailov et al., 2024; Martínez et al., 2023; Alemohammad et al., 2023, see also Section 3.3), though preliminary work suggests such concerns might be overblown (Gerstgrasser et al., 2024).
单个机器学习系统在不同于其训练环境的情境中可能表现不佳。这些分布偏移的一个关键来源是其他智能体的行动与适应性调整 (Papoudakis et al., 2019; Piliouras & Yu, 2022; Narang et al., 2023)，而单智能体方法往往直接忽略这些因素，或至多将其作为外生因素建模。事实上，其他智能体可能展现的行为数量庞大且差异显著，这意味着多智能体系统会给单个学习者带来尤其具有挑战性的泛化问题 (Agapiou et al., 2022; Leibo et al., 2021; Stone et al., 2010)。尽管分布偏移也会在共同利益情境中引发问题（见第 2.1 节），但在混合动机情境中更令人担忧，因为智能体的合作能力不仅取决于能否在众多任意约定中的某一个上达成协调（共同语言或许能轻易解决这一问题），还取决于它们对其他智能体会接受哪些解决方案的判断。例如，如果用于训练谈判智能体的对手分布在谈判策略上缺乏足够的多样性，就可能导致该智能体在高风险情境中过度自信，造成灾难性后果（参见 Stastny et al., 2021），而这类情境在训练数据中可能本就鲜有先例。多智能体系统可能具有高度动态性 (Papoudakis et al., 2019)，这一事实可能进一步加剧上述问题，因为 AI 智能体或其设计者会有动机不断适应其他智能体的行为。模型可能逐渐开始使用其他模型生成的数据进行训练，这一事实也可能加剧上述影响 (Shumailov et al., 2024; Martínez et al., 2023; Alemohammad et al., 2023，另见第 3.3 节)，不过初步研究表明，这类担忧可能被夸大了 (Gerstgrasser et al., 2024)。
3.4.3 Directions3.4.3 研究方向
With the deployment of advanced multi-agent systems comes the risk of destabilising dynamics in settings ranging from financial markets (Kirilenko et al., 2017) to power grids (Schäfer et al., 2018) to battlefields (Johnson, 2021a). So far, both theoretical and empirical work has primarily studied such dynamics in small, abstract games with simple AI systems and learning algorithms.[fn40] While this is an important first step, addressing the risks of destabilizing dynamics in real-world multi-agent AI systems will require a concerted interdisciplinary effort, bringing together expertise in AI safety, dynamical systems, game theory, and policy to develop robust solutions.
随着先进多智能体系统的部署，从金融市场 (Kirilenko et al., 2017)、电网 (Schäfer et al., 2018) 到战场 (Johnson, 2021a) 等各种场景，都面临着出现破坏稳定性的动态的风险。迄今为止，理论和实证研究主要是在采用简单 AI 系统和学习算法的小型抽象博弈中研究此类动态。[fn40] 尽管这是重要的第一步，但要应对现实世界多智能体 AI 系统中失稳动态的风险，仍需要跨学科的协同努力，汇集 AI 安全、动力系统、博弈论和政策方面的专业知识，以制定稳健的解决方案。
Footnote: 40 In one of the few examples involving foundation models, Fort (2023) recently provided a simple visual illustration of how the outputs of two foundation models – GPT-4(V) and DALLE-3 – can be either stable or unstable when placed in a loop, depending on their initial input.
脚注：40 在涉及基础模型的少数案例之一中，Fort (2023) 最近提供了一个简单的直观示例，展示了将两个基础模型——GPT-4(V) 和 DALLE-3——置于一个循环中时，它们的输出如何因初始输入的不同而呈现稳定或不稳定的状态。
Understanding Dynamics.理解动态。
The conditions under which multi-agent systems have undesirable dynamics might include properties of the underlying environment and objectives (Barfuss & Mann, 2022; Sanders et al., 2018), or the structure and hyperparameters of the learning algorithms (Barfuss et al., 2019; Leonardos & Piliouras, 2022; Barfuss & Meylahn, 2023). Important research directions include understanding if (and how) chaotic dynamics in idealised versions of stochastic learning algorithms extend to their real-world counterparts,[fn41] and how these dynamics are affected by the size and structure of the state-action space.
多智能体系统产生不良动态的条件可能包括底层环境和目标的属性 (Barfuss & Mann, 2022; Sanders et al., 2018)，或学习算法的结构和超参数 (Barfuss et al., 2019; Leonardos & Piliouras, 2022; Barfuss & Meylahn, 2023)。重要的研究方向包括：理解随机学习算法理想化版本中的混沌动态是否（以及如何）延伸至其现实世界版本，[fn41] 以及状态—动作空间的规模和结构如何影响这些动态。
Footnote: 41 Formally, chaos is only rigorously defined in deterministic dynamical systems.
脚注：41 从形式上说，混沌仅在确定性动力系统中有严格定义。
Monitoring and Stabilising Dynamics.监测与稳定动态。
Early work suggests that inducing new ‘conservation laws’ (Nagarajan et al., 2020) or ‘constants of motion’ (Piliouras & Wang, 2021) in multi-agent learning can result in more predictable dynamics. Future research should investigate how these approaches scale to larger systems and greater numbers of agents and could make use of existing results in areas such as theoretical ML (Sastry, 1999; Tuyls & Nowé, 2005; Bowling & Veloso, 2001; Bottou, 2010; Kushner & Yin, 2003), adaptive mechanism design (Pardoe et al., 2006; Zhang & Parkes, 2008; Baumann et al., 2020; Yang et al., 2022; Zheng et al., 2022; Gerstgrasser & Parkes, 2023), and mean-field games (Lasry & Lions, 2007; Huang et al., 2006). Both this work and that on understanding the dynamics of multi-agent learning would benefit greatly from the insights of other scientific communities, especially those working on other non-linear complex systems, and those engineering the largest and most powerful models (Barfuss et al., 2024).
早期研究表明，在多智能体学习中引入新的“守恒定律” (Nagarajan et al., 2020) 或“运动常数” (Piliouras & Wang, 2021)，可以使动态更具可预测性。未来的研究应考察如何将这些方法扩展到规模更大、智能体数量更多的系统，并可利用理论机器学习 (Sastry, 1999; Tuyls & Nowé, 2005; Bowling & Veloso, 2001; Bottou, 2010; Kushner & Yin, 2003)、自适应机制设计 (Pardoe et al., 2006; Zhang & Parkes, 2008; Baumann et al., 2020; Yang et al., 2022; Zheng et al., 2022; Gerstgrasser & Parkes, 2023) 和平均场博弈 (Lasry & Lions, 2007; Huang et al., 2006) 等领域的现有成果。这项研究以及旨在理解多智能体学习动态的研究，都将极大地受益于其他科学研究群体的见解，尤其是研究其他非线性复杂系统的群体，以及开发规模最大、能力最强模型的群体 (Barfuss et al., 2024)。
Regulating Adaptive Multi-Agent Systems.监管自适应多智能体系统。
In addition, regulation could be used to mandate the use of mechanisms that monitor and stabilise the dynamics of multi-agent systems in safety-critical areas. This could include, for example, enforced pauses in interactions between systems or reversions to previous strategies if the system behaviour escapes certain thresholds (Subrahmanyam, 2013, as in the 2010 flash crash, when trading was temporarily halted; see also). For the most important systems, there might even be a need to enforce the (de)synchronisation of model updates, to limit the size and frequency of learning updates, or to limit the number of agents interacting with one another (either via technical restrictions or using methods akin to congestion pricing). Relatedly, existing tools for auditing models often make use of static ‘model cards’ that indicate how the model was produced, its performance, and intended use cases (Mitchell et al., 2019), but this documentation applies to single trained systems that are frozen before deployment. To monitor the dynamics of multi-agent systems, including those that learn online, we will need to leverage new innovations such as ‘ecosystem graphs’ (Bommasani et al., 2023) and ‘reward reports’ (Gilbert et al., 2022), respectively.
此外，可以通过监管，强制要求在安全关键领域使用监测和稳定多智能体系统动态的机制。例如，这可以包括：当系统行为超出某些阈值时，强制暂停系统之间的交互，或恢复先前的策略 (Subrahmanyam, 2013，如在 2010 年闪电崩盘期间，交易曾被暂时中止；另见)。对于最重要的系统，甚至可能需要强制模型更新保持同步（或去同步），限制学习更新的幅度和频率，或限制相互交互的智能体数量（通过技术限制，或采用类似拥堵定价的方法）。与此相关，现有的模型审计工具通常使用静态“模型卡”，说明模型的生成方式、性能和预期用途 (Mitchell et al., 2019)，但这类文档适用于部署前已冻结的单个训练完成的系统。为了监测多智能体系统（包括在线学习的系统）的动态，我们需要分别利用“生态系统图” (Bommasani et al., 2023) 和“奖励报告” (Gilbert et al., 2022) 等新兴创新成果。
3.5 Commitment and Trust3.5 承诺与信任
In settings that require joint action in order to obtain a better outcome, inefficiencies can result whenever one or more actors cannot be trusted (perhaps due to strategic incentives, or due to their incompetence) to carry out their part of the plan. These inefficiencies can be reduced via credible commitments made by the untrusted parties. Unfortunately, the ability to make credible commitments is ‘dual-use’ and can therefore lead to new risks.
在需要采取联合行动才能获得更好结果的情境中，只要一个或多个行动者无法被信任会履行计划中自己负责的部分（可能是由于策略性激励，也可能是由于能力不足），就可能导致效率损失。这些效率损失可以通过不受信任的各方作出可信承诺来减少。遗憾的是，作出可信承诺的能力具有“双重用途”，因此可能带来新的风险。
3.5.1 Definition3.5.1 定义
An actor makes a commitment when they bind themselves to a course of action, such that reneging on that action would either be impossible or result in significant costs to themselves. A commitment is credible when other actors believe that the actor making the commitment will follow through with the actions they claim to have committed to. Credible commitments are useful in scenarios where trust is essential but hard to establish, such as in international treaties, economic policies, and contractual agreements.
当一个行动者约束自己采取某一行动方案，使得背弃该行动要么不可能，要么会给自身带来重大成本时，该行动者就作出了承诺。当其他行动者相信作出承诺的行动者会切实执行其声称已承诺采取的行动时，该承诺就是可信的。在信任至关重要却难以建立的情境中，例如国际条约、经济政策和合同协议，可信承诺十分有用。
Since credible commitments can often help in achieving desirable cooperative outcomes, we expect there will be incentives to build systems capable of making them. For example, an AI system can become more trustworthy by being credibly committed to erasing any private information revealed to it. In contrast, human beings or organisations cannot reliably forget at will and may later leak private information, whether intentionally or not (Carnegie & Carson, 2019). Autonomous AI agents themselves might also serve as credible commitment devices (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004), enabling actors to carry out actions based on potentially complex conditions and thus helping to solve problems with incomplete contracting (Schmitz, 2001). However, the ability of AI systems to make commitments can also backfire in correspondingly severe ways, preventing recourse in high-stakes scenarios and enabling extortion and brinkmanship.
由于可信承诺往往有助于实现理想的合作结果，我们预计，人们将有动力构建能够作出此类承诺的系统。例如，AI 系统可以通过可信地承诺删除向其披露的任何私人信息，变得更加值得信赖。相比之下，人类或组织无法可靠地按意愿遗忘信息，并且日后可能有意或无意地泄露私人信息 (Carnegie & Carson, 2019)。自主 AI 智能体本身也可能充当可信承诺机制（credible commitment devices）(McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004)，使行为主体能够根据可能很复杂的条件采取行动，从而帮助解决不完全契约问题 (Schmitz, 2001)。然而，AI 系统作出承诺的能力也可能以同样严重的方式产生反作用，在高风险情境中使补救无从进行，并助长敲诈和边缘政策。
3.5.2 Instances3.5.2 实例
As noted above, the ability to form commitments can both precipitate and mitigate risks. We therefore begin by considering risk instances that can arise due to a lack of trust, before turning to those that can arise via the very mechanisms that might be used to establish such trust.
如上所述，作出承诺的能力既可能引发风险，也可能缓解风险。因此，我们首先考察因缺乏信任而可能出现的风险实例，然后转向那些可能由用于建立这种信任的机制本身所引发的风险实例。
Inefficient Outcomes.低效率的结果。
Without careful planning and the appropriate safeguards, we may soon be entering a world overrun by increasingly competent and autonomous software agents, able to act with little restriction. The abilities of these agents to persuade, deceive, and obfuscate their activities, as well as the fact they can be deployed remotely and easily created or destroyed by their deployer, means that by default they may garner little trust (from humans or from other agents). Such a world may end up being rife with economic inefficiencies (Schmitz, 2001; Krier, 2023), political problems (Kreps & Kriner, 2023; Csernatoni, 2024), and other damaging social effects (Gabriel et al., 2024). Even if it is possible to provide assurances around the day-to-day performance of most AI agents, in high-stakes situations there may be extreme pressures for agents to defect against others, making trust harder to establish, and potentially leading to conflict (Fearon, 1995; Powell, 2006, see also Section 2.2).[fn42]
如果没有周密的规划和适当的保障措施，我们可能很快就会进入一个充斥着能力和自主性日益增强的软件智能体的世界，这些智能体能够几乎不受限制地采取行动。这些智能体具有说服、欺骗和掩盖自身活动的能力，而且可以被远程部署，并由其部署者轻易创建或销毁，这意味着，在默认情况下，它们可能很难获得人类或其他智能体的信任。这样的世界最终可能充斥着经济低效率问题 (Schmitz, 2001; Krier, 2023)、政治问题 (Kreps & Kriner, 2023; Csernatoni, 2024)，以及其他有害的社会影响 (Gabriel et al., 2024)。即使能够为大多数 AI 智能体的日常表现提供保证，在高风险情境下，智能体也可能面临背叛其他智能体的巨大压力，使信任更难建立，并可能导致冲突（Fearon, 1995; Powell, 2006，另见第 2.2 节）。[fn42]
Footnote: 42 A classic non-AI example is the hypothesis that a major contributor to World War I was Germany’s concerns about the rising power of Russia (Copeland, 2000). Conflict might have been avoided were Russia able to credibly commit not to expand its influence, but the absence of such an ability left Germany with fewer alternatives to conflict.
脚注：42 一个经典的非 AI 例子是这样一种假说：德国对俄罗斯实力不断增强的担忧，是导致第一次世界大战的重要因素之一（Copeland, 2000）。如果俄罗斯能够可信地承诺不扩大其影响力，冲突本可以避免，但由于缺乏这种能力，德国除了冲突之外的选择就更少了。
Threats and Extortion.威胁与敲诈。
A natural solution to problems of trust is to provide some kind of commitment ability to AI agents, which can be used to bind them to more cooperative courses of action. Unfortunately, the ability to make credible commitments may come with the ability to make credible threats, which facilitate extortion and could incentivize brinkmanship (see Section 2.2). For example, ransomware becomes more effective if the hacker can credibly commit to restore the victim’s data upon receiving payment, and coercion using AI-controlled weapons could become more frequent if actors gain the ability to make credible threats conditional on complicated demands (see also Section 3.5.2). More generally, an agent could use commitment devices to shift risks or costs to others, allowing it to behave irresponsibly.[fn43] In other cases, it might be the agent that commits to an inflexible (cooperative) course of action which can be exploited by others who can adapt their strategies to this commitment.[fn44] On the other hand, if used carefully, the ability to commit generally strictly empowers the committing agent (Stengel & Zamir, 2010; Letchford et al., 2013).
解决信任问题的一种自然办法，是赋予 AI 智能体某种承诺能力，以此约束它们采取更具合作性的行动路线。遗憾的是，作出可信承诺的能力可能伴随着发出可信威胁的能力，而后者会助长敲诈，并可能激励行为主体采取边缘政策（见第 2.2 节）。例如，如果黑客能够可信地承诺在收到付款后恢复受害者的数据，勒索软件就会变得更加有效；如果行为主体能够针对复杂要求是否得到满足而发出可信的条件性威胁，利用 AI 控制的武器实施胁迫的情况就可能变得更加频繁（另见第 3.5.2 节）。更一般地说，智能体可以利用承诺机制将风险或成本转嫁给他者，从而使自己能够采取不负责任的行为。[fn43] 在其他情况下，作出承诺的智能体可能将自己束缚于一种缺乏灵活性的（合作）行动路线，而其他智能体则可以针对这一承诺调整策略，从而利用它。[fn44] 另一方面，如果谨慎使用，作出承诺的能力通常会严格增强承诺方智能体的能力（Stengel & Zamir, 2010; Letchford et al., 2013）。
Footnote: 43 In economics, this general problem (not necessarily as a result of the power of commitment) is known as ‘moral hazard’.
脚注：43 在经济学中，这一普遍问题（不一定由承诺的力量所导致）被称为“道德风险”。
Footnote: 44 An amusing, non-AI example of such a commitment is Red Lobster’s “Endless Shrimp” deal, which has recently been blamed for driving it to bankruptcy (Meyersohn, 2024).
脚注：44 此类承诺的一个有趣的非 AI 例子是 Red Lobster 的“无限量虾”优惠活动，最近有人将该公司走向破产归咎于这一活动（Meyersohn, 2024）。
Rigidity and Mistaken Commitments.僵化与错误承诺。
Even when it is desirable to be able to make threats in order to deter socially harmful behaviour, doing so using AI agents effectively removes the human from the loop, which could prove disastrous in high-stakes contexts (e.g., a false positive in a nuclear submarine’s warning system; see also Section 3.5.2), or when irresponsible actors are enabled in making disproportionate or mistaken commitments. On the other hand, such commitments may only be credible to the extent that a human cannot intervene, increasing the incentive for delegation to AI agents. This could be worsened if other, potentially incompatible commitments can be made by other actors, leading to a ‘commitment race’ (Kokotajlo, 2019) or potential conflict. In complex networks (see Section 3.2), commitments triggered by a small number of agents could – without careful planning – cascade through the network and have a far more damaging effect (Xia & Conitzer, 2010).
即使为了威慑危害社会的行为而具备发出威胁的能力是可取的，利用 AI 智能体发出此类威胁实际上也会将人类排除在控制回路之外，这在高风险情境中（例如，核潜艇预警系统出现误报；另见第 3.5.2 节），或在使不负责任的行为主体得以作出不成比例或错误承诺时，都可能造成灾难性后果。另一方面，此类承诺可能只有在人类无法干预的情况下才具有可信度，从而增强将权限委托给 AI 智能体的动机。如果其他行为主体也能作出其他可能不相容的承诺，情况就可能进一步恶化，从而引发“承诺竞赛” (Kokotajlo, 2019) 或潜在冲突。在复杂网络中（见第 3.2 节），如果缺乏周密规划，少数智能体触发的承诺可能在整个网络中引发级联反应，造成严重得多的破坏性影响 (Xia & Conitzer, 2010)。
3.5.3 Directions3.5.3 方向
As with any dual-use technology, ensuring it is used for beneficial rather than detrimental means can be extremely challenging. We therefore attempt to focus on directions that differentially advance beneficial uses (Sandbrink et al., 2022), while acknowledging that it will not, in general, be possible isolate these entirely.[fn45]
与任何两用技术一样，确保其被用于有益而非有害的目的可能极具挑战。因此，我们尝试聚焦于能够相对更多地促进有益用途的方向（Sandbrink et al., 2022），同时承认，一般而言，不可能将这些用途完全区分开来。[fn45]
Footnote: 45 Even in the case of human commitments, it is not always obvious which families of commitments are desirable to make. For example, if a state commits to refusing to negotiate with terrorists, they might end up sacrificing some lives while establishing a reputation that saves more lives over the long term. Similarly, a seller might refuse a low, though still positive, offer in order to achieve better offers in future. Such questions are often as much a matter of principle as they are of consequentialist reasoning.
脚注：45 即使是在人类承诺的情况下，哪些类型的承诺值得作出，也并非总是显而易见的。例如，如果一个国家承诺拒绝与恐怖分子谈判，最终可能会牺牲一些生命，但同时树立起一种从长远来看能够挽救更多生命的声誉。同样，卖方可能会拒绝一个虽低但仍为正的报价，以便在未来获得更好的报价。此类问题往往既涉及原则，也涉及后果主义推理。
Keeping Humans in the Loop.保持人类在环。
Given the risks associated with the power of AI commitments, a key direction will be to lay out the domains in which they can be used and the kinds of commitments that are permitted. For example, existing efforts have already sought to ensure that AI systems do not form a part of the nuclear chain of command (Renshaw & Hunnicutt, 2024; U.S. Congress, 2023). It may be similarly important in other high-stakes settings to ensure that humans cannot be fully removed from the loop.[fn46] While certain kinds of commitment device might still allow for malicious use (such as automated blackmail campaigns), regulation, safeguards, and infrastructure limiting where and how AI agents can be deployed could help prevent the worst offences (Kolt, 2024; Chan et al., 2025).
鉴于 AI 承诺能力所带来的风险，一个关键方向是明确其适用领域以及允许作出的承诺类型。例如，现有工作已经致力于确保 AI 系统不被纳入核指挥链（Renshaw & Hunnicutt, 2024; U.S. Congress, 2023）。在其他高风险场景中，确保人类无法被完全排除在回路之外，可能同样重要。[fn46] 尽管某些类型的承诺装置仍可能被用于恶意用途（例如自动化勒索活动），但通过监管、安全防护措施，以及限制 AI 智能体部署地点和方式的基础设施，可以帮助防止最严重的侵害行为 (Kolt, 2024; Chan et al., 2025)。
Footnote: 46 However, the inclusion of a human in the loop does not itself guarantee control. The presence of an algorithmic system can negatively influence human decision-making (Green & Chen, 2020; Green, 2022; Crootof et al., 2023; Skitka et al., 1999; Goddard et al., 2012; Borg et al., 2024), such as through automation bias. At the same time, it is important to acknowledge that humans suffer from their own flaws that might lead to risks and that might be (at least partly) overcome via the use of AI systems.
脚注：46 然而，让人类参与回路本身并不能保证控制权。算法系统的存在可能会对人类决策产生负面影响（Green & Chen, 2020; Green, 2022; Crootof et al., 2023; Skitka et al., 1999; Goddard et al., 2012; Borg et al., 2024），例如通过自动化偏见。与此同时，必须承认，人类自身也存在可能导致风险的缺陷，而使用 AI 系统或许能够（至少部分地）克服这些缺陷。
Limiting Commitment Power.限制承诺能力。
Researchers should also explore ways to design AI systems that can make and adhere to commitments even in the face of changing circumstances or new information, thereby avoiding some of the risks associated with overly rigid strategies. This might involve developing algorithms that can (learn to) renegotiate commitments in a fair and transparent manner when necessary (Sandholm & Lesser, 2002; Ho et al., 2014; Wang et al., 2023; Cohen et al., 2023). While agents equipped with commitment powers are not yet widespread, it would be valuable to begin preliminary studies now into demonstrations of their risks (and benefits, see, e.g., Christoffersen et al., 2023; Zhu et al., 2025), as well as the feasibility of technical solutions, the tractability of governance solutions, and their intersection (Reuel et al., 2024; Kolt, 2024).
研究人员还应探索如何设计 AI 系统，使其即使面对环境变化或新信息，也能作出并遵守承诺，从而避免策略过于僵化所带来的某些风险。这可能涉及开发能够在必要时以公平、透明的方式（学会）重新协商承诺的算法（Sandholm & Lesser, 2002; Ho et al., 2014; Wang et al., 2023; Cohen et al., 2023）。尽管具备承诺能力的智能体尚未广泛普及，但现在就开始开展初步研究，展示其风险（以及益处，例如参见 Christoffersen et al., 2023; Zhu et al., 2025），并研究技术解决方案的可行性、治理解决方案的可实施性及二者的交集，将具有重要价值（Reuel et al., 2024; Kolt, 2024）。
Institutions and Normative Infrastructure.制度与规范性基础设施。
Other important research directions include the development of normative infrastructure that can help establish trust without recourse to commitment devices that might be misused. These collectively enforced rules and norms might serve to differentially advance cooperation relative to coercion (Sandbrink et al., 2022). For example, the introduction of unique agent identifiers (Chan et al., 2024a) would enable the construction of reputation systems, which are critically important in otherwise (pseudo-)anonymous interactions such as online marketplaces (Tadelis, 2016). While reputation is still ‘dual-use’ to the extent that one could develop a reputation for carrying out costly threats, doing so requires paying such costs and also being able to escape later punishment oneself, which may be a less viable strategy in many cases. Other examples include determining the rules and principles via which liability for harms from AI agents is assigned (Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Kolt, 2024; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992, see also Section 4.2).
其他重要研究方向包括发展规范性基础设施，以帮助建立信任，而无须诉诸可能被滥用的承诺机制。这些由集体共同执行的规则和规范，可能有助于相对于胁迫而言优先促进合作（Sandbrink et al., 2022）。例如，引入唯一的智能体标识符（Chan et al., 2024a）将使声誉系统的构建成为可能，而对于在线市场等原本处于（伪）匿名状态的交互而言，声誉系统至关重要（Tadelis, 2016）。尽管声誉仍具有“两用性”，因为行为主体可以树立起会执行高代价威胁的声誉，但这样做既需要付出这些代价，也需要自身能够逃避后续惩罚，因此在许多情况下，这可能是一种可行性较低的策略。其他例子包括确定分配 AI 智能体所致损害之责任的规则和原则（Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Kolt, 2024; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992，另见第 4.2 节）。
Privacy-Preserving Monitoring.保护隐私的监测。
In order for reputation systems to be effective for more general and widely deployed agents, it will be necessary to improve trust by monitoring their actions (Chan et al., 2024). Monitoring also extends to scrutiny of the actors deploying those agents, who might claim to be running one kind of agent or using some kinds of data, while instead using others. This in turn, however, raises clear and important privacy concerns. There is thus an important need to develop privacy-preserving technologies for monitoring AI systems and the actions of autonomous agents (Shavit, 2023; Vegesna, 2023). Examples include the use of cryptography – such as signatures that can serve as proof of learning (Jia et al., 2021) or proof of inference (Ghodsi et al., 2017), protocols for decentralized verifiable computation (Yao, 1982; Bonawitz et al., 2019), and performing computations using encrypted data (Martins et al., 2017; Dowlin et al., 2016) – as well as tools for auditing and monitoring both software and hardware.
为了使声誉系统能够有效适用于用途更广、部署更普遍的智能体，有必要通过监测其行为来增进信任（Chan et al., 2024）。监测还包括审查部署这些智能体的行为主体，因为他们可能声称自己运行的是某一类智能体或使用的是某些类型的数据，实际上却使用了其他智能体或数据。然而，这又会引发明显且重大的隐私担忧。因此，迫切需要开发用于监测 AI 系统和自主智能体行为的隐私保护技术（Shavit, 2023; Vegesna, 2023）。例子包括使用密码学——例如，可作为学习证明（proof of learning）(Jia et al., 2021) 或推理证明（proof of inference）(Ghodsi et al., 2017) 的签名、去中心化可验证计算协议 (Yao, 1982; Bonawitz et al., 2019)，以及使用加密数据进行计算 (Martins et al., 2017; Dowlin et al., 2016)——以及用于审计和监控软件与硬件的工具。
Mutual Simulation and Transparency.相互模拟与透明性。
Finally, while monitoring and reputation systems might be able to render more transparent what an agent has done in the past, we may also want to use the unique properties of computational agents in order to predict what they will do in the future (Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023). For example, such agents are written in code that can – in theory – be read or understood by other agents. This kind of mutual transparency can beneficial in establishing trust and reaching more efficient outcomes (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004; Halpern & Pass, 2018; Oesterheld, 2018; Han et al., 2021), though has yet to find practical applications (Critch et al., 2022). Similarly, even if one cannot peer inside the black box, the same code can be run multiple times on different inputs, allowing for simulations and tests prior to deployment or even individual interactions. As with white-box access to other agents, these abilities can (in theory) provably reduce inefficiencies due to mistrust (Kovařík et al., 2023; Kovařík et al., 2024; Chen et al., 2024), but have yet to be studied in the context of real-world strategic agents (though see, e.g., Griffin et al., 2024; Greenblatt et al., 2023). More research is required to design and implement tractable versions of these methods in order to fulfil their theoretical promise.
最后，尽管监控与声誉系统或许能够使智能体过去的行为更加透明，我们也可能希望利用计算智能体的独特属性，预测它们未来的行为 (Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023)。例如，这类智能体是用代码编写的，而这些代码在理论上可以被其他智能体读取或理解。这种相互透明性有助于建立信任并实现更高效的结果 (McAfee, 1984; Howard, 1988; Tennenholtz, 2004; Halpern & Pass, 2018; Oesterheld, 2018; Han et al., 2021)，但尚未得到实际应用 (Critch et al., 2022)。类似地，即使无法窥探黑箱内部，也可以针对不同输入多次运行同一份代码，从而在部署前、甚至在每次交互前进行模拟和测试。与对其他智能体的白箱访问一样，这些能力在理论上可以被证明能够减少因不信任而产生的低效 (Kovařík et al., 2023; Kovařík et al., 2024; Chen et al., 2024)，但尚未在现实世界中的策略性智能体情境下得到研究（不过可参见 Griffin et al., 2024; Greenblatt et al., 2023 等）。还需要开展更多研究，设计并实现这些方法在计算上可行的版本，以兑现其理论上的潜力。
3.6 Emergent Agency3.6 涌现能动性
Emergent behaviour is ubiquitous in the natural, biomedical, and social sciences. Examples include the superconductivity of materials in condensed matter physics (Anderson, 1972); complex tasks like bridge-building by ant colonies and facing larger predators (Gordon, 1996; Bonabeau et al., 1997); and, in the social sphere, collective behaviours such as group-think or the development of new norms (Couzin, 2007). In this section we focus on the risks presented by the emergence of higher-level forms of agency from a collective of agents.
涌现行为在自然科学、生物医学和社会科学中普遍存在。例子包括凝聚态物理学中材料的超导性 (Anderson, 1972)；蚁群搭桥、对抗体型更大的捕食者等复杂任务 (Gordon, 1996; Bonabeau et al., 1997)；以及社会领域中的群体思维或新规范形成等集体行为 (Couzin, 2007)。本节重点讨论由智能体集体涌现出更高层次能动性所带来的风险。
3.6.1 Definition3.6.1 定义
Emergent behaviours are those exhibited by a complex entity composed of multiple, interacting parts (such as AI agents) that are not exhibited by any of those parts when viewed individually. Emergent behaviours are distinct from mere accumulations (as in Section 3.7.2, for example); in other words, the whole may be different to the sum of its parts (Anderson, 1972). While there is a sense in which everything we study in this report can be viewed as “emerging” from multi-agent systems (Mogul, 2006; Altmann et al., 2024), our focus on this section is specifically on the risks associated with emergent agency at the level of the collective. This is distinct from other works that discuss the emergent behaviour of individual agents – such as tool use (Baker et al., 2019), locomotion (Bansal et al., 2018), or communication (Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020) -- in multi-agent settings.[fn47] These individual behaviours are fundamentally driven by the selection pressure induced by the presence of other agents, which we discuss in Section 3.3.
涌现行为是由多个相互作用的部分（例如 AI 智能体）组成的复杂实体所表现出的行为，而其中任何一个部分在被单独考察时都不会表现出这些行为。涌现行为不同于单纯的累积（例如第 3.7.2 节中的情况）；换言之，整体可能不同于其各部分之和 (Anderson, 1972)。尽管从某种意义上说，本报告研究的一切都可以被视为从多智能体系统中“涌现”出来的 (Mogul, 2006; Altmann et al., 2024)，但本节专门关注集体层面涌现能动性所伴随的风险。这不同于其他讨论多智能体环境中个体智能体涌现行为的研究，例如工具使用 (Baker et al., 2019)、运动 (Bansal et al., 2018) 或通信 (Lazaridou & Baroni, 2020)。[fn47] 这些个体行为从根本上由其他智能体的存在所引发的选择压力驱动，我们将在第 3.3 节讨论这一点。
Footnote: 47 Other works consider emergence concerning say, the number of parameters in a model, as opposed to the number of agents. For example, Wei et al. (2022) “consider an ability to be emergent if it is not present in smaller models but is present in larger models”.
脚注：47 其他研究考察的涌现涉及的可能是模型的参数数量，而非智能体的数量。例如，Wei et al. (2022)“将较小模型中不存在、但较大模型中存在的能力视为涌现能力”。
We break the risks associated with emergent agency into the emergence of dangerous capabilities, the emergence of dangerous goals, and thus – if one takes the view that intelligence is fundamentally rooted in an individual’s or group’s ability to solve problems, achieve goals, etc. (Legg & Hutter, 2007) – the possibility of creating emergent higher-level agency or collective intelligence (Malone & Bernstein, 2022). To provide a paradigmatic example, one termite by itself might be incapable of constructing a mound, and yet the overall colony can do so quite proficiently. Emergent goals, on the other hand, are agnostic to the group’s (or any individual’s) abilities,[fn48] and can be used to model the group’s objectives, which supervene on the individuals’ objectives. Thus while it might be unreasonable to model a single termite as having the goal of building a mound, this goal could be highly predictive of the overall colony’s behaviour.
我们将涌现能动性所伴随的风险分为危险能力的涌现、危险目标的涌现，以及由此产生的创造出涌现的更高层次能动性或集体智能的可能性 (Malone & Bernstein, 2022)——这里的前提是，认为智能从根本上源于个体或群体解决问题、实现目标等的能力 (Legg & Hutter, 2007)。举一个典型例子，单只白蚁可能无法建造蚁丘，但整个白蚁群体却能相当熟练地完成这项任务。另一方面，涌现目标与群体（或任何个体）的能力无关，[fn48] 并且可用于对群体目标进行建模，而群体目标随附于个体目标。因此，尽管将单只白蚁建模为具有建造蚁丘这一目标可能并不合理，但这一目标却可能对整个白蚁群体的行为具有很强的预测力。
Footnote: 48 This claim is sometimes known as the ‘orthogonality thesis’: goals and capabilities (i.e., one’s means to achieve one’s goals) are independent, or ‘orthogonal’, to one another (Bostrom, 2014).
脚注：48 这一主张有时被称为“正交性论题”（orthogonality thesis）：目标与能力（即实现目标的手段）彼此独立，或称彼此“正交” (Bostrom, 2014)。
3.6.2 Instances3.6.2 实例
Before proceeding further, we note that discussions of emergent phenomena in systems of advanced AI agents are necessarily quite speculative, as it is challenging (both in theory and in practice) to identify such phenomena.[fn49] We therefore attempt to draw lessons from simpler AI systems or biological entities, while highlighting that advanced AI agents could also possess features that make the transition to higher-level agency easier, such as the ability to more easily share information, replicate, and update their behaviour (Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023).
在进一步讨论之前，我们指出，关于先进 AI 智能体系统中涌现现象的讨论必然带有很强的推测性，因为无论在理论上还是实践中，识别此类现象都具有挑战性。[fn49] 因此，我们尝试从较简单的 AI 系统或生物实体中汲取经验，同时强调，先进 AI 智能体也可能具备一些特征，使其更容易向更高层次的能动性过渡，例如能够更轻松地共享信息、复制自身以及更新自身行为 (Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023)。
Footnote: 49 Indeed, the astute reader will notice that this section is the only section of the report that does not have at least one corresponding case study. While there are demonstrations of AI agents exhibiting emergent collective capabilities and goals (see, e.g., Werfel et al. (2014), in which a swarm of simple, termite-inspired construction robots are able to build large-scale structures without centralized coordination), we are not aware of examples involving collective agency among advanced AI agents (such as those powered by LLMs) or collective agency that represents an obvious risk.
脚注：49 事实上，细心的读者会注意到，本节是报告中唯一没有至少一个对应案例研究的章节。尽管已有研究展示了 AI 智能体表现出涌现的集体能力和目标（例如，参见 Werfel et al. (2014)，其中一群受白蚁启发的简单建筑机器人能够在没有集中协调的情况下建造大型结构），但我们尚未发现涉及先进 AI 智能体（例如由 LLMs 驱动的智能体）之间集体能动性（collective agency）的实例，也未发现构成明显风险的集体能动性实例。
Emergent Capabilities.涌现能力。
Dangerous emergent capabilities could arise when a multi-agent system overcomes the safety-enhancing limitations of the individual systems, such as individual models’ narrow domains of application or myopia caused by a lack of long-term planning and long-term memory. For example, narrow systems for research planning, predicting the properties of molecules, and synthesising new chemicals could, when combined, lead to a complex ‘test and iterate’ automated workflow capable of designing dangerous new chemical compounds far beyond the scope of the initial systems’ capabilities (Boiko et al., 2023; Urbina et al., 2022; Luo et al., 2024). This is similar to how a myopic actor and a passive critic can combine to produce an actor-critic algorithm capable of long-term planning via RL (Konda & Tsitsiklis, 2000). This possibility is important for safety – and for future AI ecosystems made of specialised ‘AI services’ (Drexler, 2019) – as generally intelligent autonomous systems could pose much greater risks than narrow AI tools (Chan et al., 2023).[fn50] More speculatively, the combination of advanced AI agents could eventually lead to recursive self-improvement at the collective level, as AI research itself becomes increasingly automated (1; Mankowitz et al., 2023; Agnesina et al., 2023; Lu et al., 2024), even though no individual system possesses this capability.
当多智能体系统克服了单个系统中有助于提升安全性的局限时，就可能产生危险的涌现能力；这些局限包括单个模型应用领域狭窄，或因缺乏长期规划和长期记忆而产生的短视性。例如，将用于研究规划、预测分子性质以及合成新化学品的专用系统组合起来，可能形成一套复杂的“测试与迭代”自动化工作流程，能够设计出危险的新型化合物，远远超出原有系统的能力范围 (Boiko et al., 2023; Urbina et al., 2022; Luo et al., 2024)。这类似于将一个短视的行动者与一个被动的评价者结合起来，形成能够通过 RL 进行长期规划的行动者—评价者算法（actor-critic algorithm）(Konda & Tsitsiklis, 2000)。这一可能性对于安全，以及未来由专门化“AI 服务”构成的 AI 生态系统 (Drexler, 2019)，都十分重要，因为具有通用智能的自主系统所带来的风险可能远大于狭义 AI 工具 (Chan et al., 2023)。[fn50] 更具推测性地说，随着 AI 研究本身日益自动化 (1; Mankowitz et al., 2023; Agnesina et al., 2023; Lu et al., 2024)，先进 AI 智能体的组合最终可能在集体层面实现递归式自我改进，即使任何单个系统都不具备这种能力。
Footnote: 50 Despite this, many companies are racing to build AI agents (OpenAI, 2025; Google DeepMind, 2024; Microsoft, 2024; Meta, 2025; Anthropic, 2024), including early efforts attempting to construct composite agents based on simpler components including powerful foundation models (e.g., Schick et al., 2023; Wu et al., 2024a).
脚注：50 尽管如此，许多公司仍在竞相构建 AI 智能体 (OpenAI, 2025; Google DeepMind, 2024; Microsoft, 2024; Meta, 2025; Anthropic, 2024)，其中包括一些早期尝试，即利用包含强大基础模型在内的较简单组件来构建复合智能体（例如，Schick et al., 2023; Wu et al., 2024a）。
Emergent Goals.涌现目标。
Ascribing goals to a system is not always straightforward. For our present purposes, it will suffice to adopt a Dennetian perspective (Dennett, 1971), ascribing goals and intentions only when it is useful (i.e., predictive) to do so.[fn51] While it might not be helpful to describe individual narrow AI tools as having goals, their combination may act as a (seemingly) goal-directed collective. For example, a group of moderation bots on a major social networking site could subtly but systematically manipulate the overall political perspectives of the user population, even though, individually, each agent is programmed to simply increase user engagement or filter out dis-preferred content. Other dangerous goals that could emerge from groups of more advanced AI agents include power-seeking (Turner & Tadepalli, 2022; Carlsmith, 2022), self-preservation (Omohundro, 2008; Lyon, 2011), or competing against other groups (Bakhtin et al., 2022), which could be instrumentally useful at the collective level even if avoidable or not useful at the individual level.
将目标归于一个系统并非总是简单明了。就我们当前的讨论而言，采用丹尼特式视角 (Dennett, 1971) 已足够，即仅在赋予目标和意图具有用处（即具有预测作用）时才这样做。[fn51] 尽管将单个狭义 AI 工具描述为具有目标可能并无帮助，但它们的组合可能表现为一个（看似）具有目标导向的集体。例如，某大型社交网站上的一组内容审核机器人可能以隐蔽但系统性的方式操纵用户群体的整体政治观点，即使每个智能体单独来看都只是被编程用于提高用户参与度或过滤不受偏好的内容。由更先进的 AI 智能体组成的群体还可能涌现出其他危险目标，包括寻求权力 (Turner & Tadepalli, 2022; Carlsmith, 2022)、自我保全 (Omohundro, 2008; Lyon, 2011)，或与其他群体竞争 (Bakhtin et al., 2022)；即使这些目标在个体层面可以避免或并无用处，它们在集体层面也可能具有工具性价值。
Footnote: 51 See Oesterheld (2016); Halpern & Kleiman-Weiner (2018); Orseau et al. (2018); Everitt et al. (2021); Kenton et al. (2022); Biehl & Virgo (2023); MacDermott et al. (2024); Ward et al. (2024) for recent, formal examinations of agency and incentives in AI systems, and the implications thereof for safety.
脚注：51 关于近期对 AI 系统中的能动性与激励及其安全影响所进行的形式化研究，参见 Oesterheld (2016); Halpern & Kleiman-Weiner (2018); Orseau et al. (2018); Everitt et al. (2021); Kenton et al. (2022); Biehl & Virgo (2023); MacDermott et al. (2024); Ward et al. (2024)。
3.6.3 Directions3.6.3 研究方向
Insofar as the prospect of collective goals and capabilities emerging from large numbers of advanced AI agents remains somewhat speculative, it will be especially useful to develop a firmer theoretical understanding of when and how these novel forms of agency might emerge. This understanding should be complemented by preliminary empirical investigations, potentially in settings with less advanced agents or smaller numbers of agents.
鉴于大量先进 AI 智能体涌现出集体目标和能力的前景仍带有一定推测性，在理论上更扎实地理解这些新型能动性可能在何时以及如何涌现，将尤其有用。应以初步的实证研究来补充这一理解，这些研究可以在智能体不那么先进或智能体数量较少的环境中开展。
Empirical Exploration.实证探索。
By definition, emergent properties are hard to predict when looking at individual components. Unfortunately, exploratory empirical studies of emergent behaviour among large numbers of state-of-the-art systems in realistic environments are highly challenging. One reason is that experiments using many model instances are very expensive. Another is that is difficult to construct relevant environments and ‘sandboxes’ which are similar enough to the real world for us to gain transferable insights. Nonetheless, research such as that of Park et al. (2023); Chen et al. (2024c); Vezhnevets et al. (2023) shows this to be possible in simple games, and that it can lead to surprising outcomes. Future work could use more realistic or open-ended environments, such as those involving economic activity (Zheng et al., 2022), or games inspired by massively multiplayer online role-playing games (Suarez et al., 2019). This would help address the crucial problem of understanding and eventually being able to predict the settings under which undesirable behaviours emerge at the group level and how robust they are, including the influence of key factors and conditions such as the degree of competition, the (non-)diversity of the agents, and their individual capability levels.
根据定义，仅考察单个组成部分时，很难预测涌现属性。遗憾的是，在现实环境中，对大量最先进系统之间的涌现行为开展探索性实证研究极具挑战。一个原因是，使用大量模型实例开展实验的成本非常高。另一个原因是，很难构建与现实世界足够相似的相关环境和“沙盒”，以便从中获得可迁移的认识。尽管如此，Park et al. (2023); Chen et al. (2024c); Vezhnevets et al. (2023) 等研究表明，这在简单游戏中是可行的，而且可能带来出人意料的结果。未来的研究可以采用更贴近现实或更具开放性的环境，例如涉及经济活动的环境 (Zheng et al., 2022)，或受大型多人在线角色扮演游戏启发的游戏 (Suarez et al., 2019)。这将有助于解决一个关键问题：理解并最终能够预测群体层面的不良行为会在何种情境下涌现，以及这些行为有多稳健，包括竞争程度、智能体的多样性或缺乏多样性，以及其个体能力水平等关键因素和条件所产生的影响。
Theories of Emergent Capabilties.涌现能力理论。
In conjunction with empirical studies, we must develop a theoretical understanding of emergent capabilities that can be applied to groups of frontier models. Existing work in this area either identifies a specific emergent behaviour in advance and attempts to measure the presence or cause of this behaviour based on pre-existing observations (Seth, 2006; Chen et al., 2009), or formalises some abstract notion of a micro- and macro-level and attempts to detect newly emergent behaviours by comparing the difference (Kubík, 2003; Teo et al., 2013; Szabo & Teo, 2015), the idea being that emergent phenomena are those present in the latter but not the former. Other related works include that of Sourbut et al. (2024), who propose a theoretical method of separately measuring individual and collective capabilities and (mis)alignment in strategic settings. These approaches are computationally expensive, however, and their empirical utility us yet to be convincingly demonstrated.[fn52] Promising directions include developing tractable proxies of these measures, and the use of ML (Dahia & Szabo, 2024) and distributed methods (Wang et al., 2016; O’toole et al., 2017) to improve scalability.
在开展实证研究的同时，我们必须形成对涌现能力的理论认识，并使其能够应用于前沿模型群体。这一领域的现有研究，要么预先确定某种特定的涌现行为，并尝试根据已有观测来衡量该行为是否存在或探究其成因 (Seth, 2006; Chen et al., 2009)，要么将某种抽象的微观与宏观层次概念形式化，并尝试通过比较两者的差异来检测新涌现的行为 (Kubík, 2003; Teo et al., 2013; Szabo & Teo, 2015)，其基本思路是：涌现现象存在于后者，而不存在于前者。其他相关研究包括 Sourbut et al. (2024) 的工作，他们提出了一种理论方法，用于在策略情境中分别衡量个体与集体的能力以及对齐（或失配）程度。然而，这些方法的计算成本高昂，其实际效用尚未得到令人信服的验证。[fn52] 有前景的方向包括为这些度量开发计算上可行的代理指标，以及使用机器学习（ML）(Dahia & Szabo, 2024) 和分布式方法 (Wang et al., 2016; O’toole et al., 2017) 来提高可扩展性。
Footnote: 52 Moreover, they may depend on access to information about deployed agents that is unavailable not just to third parties, but also to other model providers seeking to measure emergent behaviours in their agents’ interactions with others.
脚注：52 此外，这些方法可能依赖于获取有关已部署智能体的信息，而这些信息不仅第三方无法获得，就连希望衡量自身智能体与其他智能体交互时所产生的涌现行为的其他模型提供商也无法获得。
Theories of Emergent Goals.涌现目标理论。
It is especially important to know what we ought to measure here, as some techniques for understanding the goals of a single agent, such as interpretability methods (Michaud et al., 2020; Colognese & Jose, 2023; Mini et al., 2023; Marks et al., 2023) might not be easily applied to group-level emergent goals (Grupen et al., 2022). Many formal approaches to measuring and detecting goal-directedness make use of causal models (Halpern & Kleiman-Weiner, 2018; Everitt et al., 2021; Kenton et al., 2022; MacDermott et al., 2024; Ward et al., 2024). A natural next step towards generalising these works to consider emergent goals in multi-agent settings would therefore be to apply them in the context of causal games (Hammond et al., 2023). This line of work would also benefit from the insights of other fields that have sought to develop theories of emergent agency (Friston et al., 2022; Okasha, 2018; Smith & Szathmáry, 2020).
明确我们在此应当衡量什么尤为重要，因为一些用于理解单个智能体目标的技术，例如可解释性方法 (Michaud et al., 2020; Colognese & Jose, 2023; Mini et al., 2023; Marks et al., 2023)，可能难以直接应用于群体层面的涌现目标 (Grupen et al., 2022)。许多用于衡量和检测目标导向性的形式化方法都使用了因果模型 (Halpern & Kleiman-Weiner, 2018; Everitt et al., 2021; Kenton et al., 2022; MacDermott et al., 2024; Ward et al., 2024)。因此，要将这些研究推广至多智能体情境中的涌现目标，一个自然的后续步骤就是将其应用于因果博弈框架 (Hammond et al., 2023)。这一研究方向也将受益于其他领域的见解，这些领域一直致力于发展涌现能动性理论 (Friston et al., 2022; Okasha, 2018; Smith & Szathmáry, 2020)。
Monitoring and Intervening on Collective Agents.对集体智能体的监测与干预。
Once we possess a better theoretical and empirical understanding of emergence in advanced multi-agent systems, it will be important to develop the tools and infrastructure to monitor for, and intervene on, forms of emergent, collective agency. In practice, this is likely to overlap substantially with the tools required to monitor the macroscopic properties of large, dynamic networks of agents (see Sections 3.2 and 3.4). Similarly, interventions for mitigating undesirable forms of emergent behaviour may be related to those for mitigating collusion (see Section 2.3) or deleterious selection pressures (see Section 3.3). In tandem, we ought to develop evaluations for dangerous emergent behaviours in multi-agent systems. For example, while a ‘one-shot’ application of an LLM might not possess a particular ability (such as manipulating a human to take some action), a population of multiple LLMs and other AI tools might. Similarly, while a single agent might not exhibit a certain sub-goal (such as self-preservation) while completing a task, a combination of agents might develop a mutual reliance upon one another that ends up having self-preservation as an instrumental sub-goal the collective level.
一旦我们在理论和实证层面上对先进多智能体系统中的涌现有了更深入的认识，就有必要开发相应的工具和基础设施，以监测和干预各种形式的涌现集体能动性。在实践中，这很可能与监测大规模动态智能体网络宏观属性所需的工具存在很大重合（参见第 3.2 和 3.4 节）。同样，缓解不良涌现行为的干预措施，可能与缓解串谋（参见第 2.3 节）或有害选择压力（参见第 3.3 节）的措施相关。与此同时，我们应当开发针对多智能体系统中危险涌现行为的评估方法。例如，虽然以“一次性”方式使用 LLM 时，它可能不具备某种特定能力（例如操纵人类采取某种行动），但由多个 LLM 和其他 AI 工具组成的群体却可能具备这种能力。同样，虽然单个智能体在完成任务时可能不会表现出某种子目标（例如自我保存），但多个智能体的组合可能形成相互依赖，最终使自我保存成为集体层面的工具性子目标。
3.7 Multi-Agent Security3.7 多智能体安全
Global cyber threats are on the rise, not just due to the proliferation of commercial cyber tools (NCSC, 2023), but also due to an increase in so-called ‘hybrid warfare’ (which blends conventional warfare with cyber- and information-warfare) by nation-states and non-state actors (Kaunert & Ilbiz, 2021; CSIS, 2023). The shift towards a world of advanced AI agents will not only enable new tools and affordances, but also increase the surface area for potential attacks, invalidating previously reasonable threat modelling assumptions and requiring a new approach: multi-agent security (Schroeder et al., 2023).
全球网络威胁正在增加，这不仅是由于商业网络工具的扩散（NCSC, 2023），还因为国家和非国家行为体越来越多地采用所谓的“混合战争”（即将常规战争与网络战和信息战相结合）（Kaunert & Ilbiz, 2021; CSIS, 2023）。向先进 AI 智能体广泛存在的世界转变，不仅会带来新的工具和可供性（affordances），还会扩大潜在攻击面，使先前合理的威胁建模假设失效，并要求采用一种新方法：多智能体安全（multi-agent security）（Schroeder et al., 2023）。
3.7.1 Definition3.7.1 定义
Multi-agent security focuses on safeguarding complex networks of heterogeneous agents and the systems that they interact with. This includes protecting not only data and software but also hardware and other physical aspects of the world that are integrated with these digital systems.[fn53] While many security settings are implicitly multi-agent (involving both an attacker and a defender), multi-agent security addresses vulnerabilities and attack vectors that emerge specifically when many AI agents interact within a broader networked ecosystem.[fn54] For example, traditional security frameworks such as zero-trust approaches may not provide the required trade-offs between security and capability in large multi-agent systems (Wylde, 2021).
多智能体安全侧重于保护由异构智能体组成的复杂网络，以及这些智能体与之交互的系统。这不仅包括保护数据和软件，还包括保护与这些数字系统集成的硬件以及现实世界的其他物理要素。[fn53] 尽管许多安全场景隐含着多智能体特征（同时涉及攻击者和防御者），但多智能体安全关注的是许多 AI 智能体在更广泛的网络化生态系统中交互时特有的漏洞和攻击途径。[fn54] 例如，零信任方法等传统安全框架，可能无法在大型多智能体系统中实现安全性与能力之间所需的权衡 (Wylde, 2021)。
Footnote: 53 Note that it is often helpful to distinguish between safety (which aims to prevent harm from a given entity) and security (which aims to prevent harm to a given entity), though we will also typically be interested in the latter to extent that it leads to the former (Khlaaf, 2023). At the same time, a security perspective involves considering worst-case scenarios, which is also a natural perspective when considering more extreme risks from advanced AI.
脚注：53 请注意，区分安全性（safety，旨在防止某一实体造成伤害）与安保性（security，旨在防止某一实体受到伤害）通常很有帮助，不过，我们通常也会关注后者，因为它有助于实现前者 (Khlaaf, 2023)。与此同时，安全视角涉及对最坏情形的考量，而在考虑先进 AI 带来的更极端风险时，这也是一种自然的视角。
Footnote: 54 Similarly, we note that despite the implicit presence of an adversary, security failures need not only be a form of conflict (Section 2.2) but can also lead to miscoordination (Section 2.1) as well as collusion (Section 2.3).
脚注：54 同样，我们指出，尽管其中隐含着对抗者的存在，安保失效也不一定仅表现为冲突（第 2.2 节），还可能导致协调失灵（第 2.1 节）以及合谋（第 2.3 节）。
While coordinated human hacking teams or botnets already pose ‘multi-agent’ security risks, their speed and adaptability are limited by human coordination or static strategies. As AI agents become more autonomous and capable of learning and complex reasoning, however, they will be more easily able to dynamically strategize, collude, and decompose tasks to evade defences. At the same time, security efforts aimed at preventing attacks to (or harmful actions from) a single advanced AI system are comparatively simple, as they primarily require monitoring a single entity. The emergence of advanced multi-agent systems therefore raises new vulnerabilities that do not appear in single-agent or less advanced multi-agent contexts.
尽管协同作战的人类黑客团队或僵尸网络已经构成“多智能体”安全风险，但其速度和适应能力受到人类协调或静态策略的限制。然而，随着 AI 智能体变得更加自主，并具备学习和复杂推理能力，它们将更容易通过动态制定策略、合谋和分解任务来规避防御措施。与此同时，旨在防止单个先进 AI 系统遭受攻击（或实施有害行为）的安全工作相对简单，因为这主要只需要监控单个实体。因此，先进多智能体系统的出现带来了新的漏洞，而这些漏洞并不存在于单智能体或较不先进的多智能体情境中。
3.7.2 Instances3.7.2 实例
Multi-agent security risks from advanced AI arise due to two main factors: novel attack methods and novel attack surfaces. First, the emergence of large numbers of advanced AI agents might – via their very multiplicity and decentralisation – lead to attack methods that would not be available to a single agent. Second, the complexity, interconnectedness, and range of such multi-agent systems may at the same time introduce new attack surfaces.
先进 AI 带来的多智能体安全风险源于两个主要因素：新型攻击方法和新型攻击面。首先，大量先进 AI 智能体的出现，可能恰恰由于其数量众多且去中心化，而催生单个智能体无法采用的攻击方法。其次，此类多智能体系统的复杂性、相互关联性和覆盖范围，也可能同时引入新的攻击面。
Swarm Attacks.群体攻击。
The need for multi-agent security is foreshadowed by attacks today that benefit from the use of many decentralised agents, such as distributed denial-of-service attacks (Cisco, 2023; Yoachimik & Pacheco, 2024). Such attacks exploit the massive collective resources of individual low-resourced actors, chained into an attack that breaks the assumptions of bandwidth constraints on a single well-resourced agent. Such attacks are also used to great effect elsewhere, such as in ‘brigading’ on social media, in which teams of bots or humans collude to downvote or otherwise obstruct benign content (Andrews, 2021), or coordinated malicious actions in matching, rating, and content moderation systems (Newman, 2024; Sharma, 2025). At present these bots are typically relatively unsophisticated but AI agents that can intelligently adapt and collaboratively identify new attack surfaces may amplify the potency of such attacks. More broadly, the ability for many small AI agents to parallelize tasks and recompose their outputs, such as in inference attacks that piece together sensitive information gathered individually by actors with limited access (Islam et al., 2012), can undermine the common assumption that individual agents with restricted capabilities are safe.
当今一些利用大量去中心化智能体实施的攻击，例如分布式拒绝服务攻击，已经预示了多智能体安全的必要性（Cisco, 2023; Yoachimik & Pacheco, 2024）。这类攻击将单个资源有限的行为体串联起来，利用它们汇聚而成的庞大资源发起攻击，从而打破针对单个资源充足的智能体所作的带宽约束假设。这类攻击在其他场景中也被有效运用，例如社交媒体上的“集体围攻”（brigading），即机器人或人类组成的团队合谋，对良性内容投反对票或以其他方式加以阻碍（Andrews, 2021）；又如匹配、评分和内容审核系统中的协同恶意行为（Newman, 2024; Sharma, 2025）。目前，这些机器人通常相对简单，但能够智能适应并协作识别新攻击面的 AI 智能体，可能会增强此类攻击的威力。更广泛地说，大量小型 AI 智能体能够并行执行任务并重新组合各自的输出，例如在推断攻击中，将访问权限有限的行为体各自收集到的敏感信息拼接起来（Islam et al., 2012）；这种能力可能动摇一个常见假设，即能力受限的单个智能体是安全的。
Heterogeneous Attacks.异构攻击。
A closely related risk is the possibility of multiple agents combining different affordances to overcome safeguards, for which there is already preliminary evidence (Jones et al., 2024, see also Section 3.7.2). In this case, it is not the sheer number of agents that leads to the novel attack method, but the combination of their different abilities. This might include the agents’ lack of individual safeguards, tasks that they have specialised to complete, systems or information that they may have access to (either directly or via training), or other incidental features such as their geographic location(s). The inherent difficulty of attributing responsibility for security breaches in diffuse, heterogeneous networks of agents further complicates timely defence and recovery (Skopik & Pahi, 2020).
一个密切相关的风险是，多个智能体可能结合各自不同的可供性来突破防护措施，对此已有初步证据（Jones et al., 2024，另见第 3.7.2 节）。在这种情况下，催生新型攻击方法的并非智能体数量庞大本身，而是它们不同能力的组合。这可能包括智能体自身缺乏防护措施、其专门执行的任务、其可能访问的系统或信息（无论是直接访问还是通过训练获取），或其他附带特征，例如其地理位置。在分散且异构的智能体网络中，安全漏洞事件的责任归属本就难以确定，这进一步增加了及时防御和恢复的难度 (Skopik & Pahi, 2020)。
Social Engineering at Scale.大规模社会工程攻击。
Advanced AI agents will be more easily able to interact with large numbers of humans, and vice versa. This provides a wider attack surface for various forms of automated social engineering (Ai et al., 2024). For example, coordinated agents could use advanced surveillance tools and produce personalized phishing or manipulative content at scale, adjusting their tactics based on user feedback (Hazell, 2023; Figueiredo et al., 2024). A large number of subtle interactions with a range of seemingly independent AI agents might be more likely to lead to someone being persuaded or manipulated compared to an interaction with a single agent. Moreover, splitting these efforts among many specialized agents could make it harder for corporate or personal security measures to detect and neutralize such campaigns.
先进 AI 智能体将更容易与大量人类互动，反之亦然。这为各种形式的自动化社会工程攻击提供了更广泛的攻击面 (Ai et al., 2024)。例如，相互协调的智能体可以使用先进的监控工具，大规模生成个性化的网络钓鱼或操纵性内容，并根据用户反馈调整策略 (Hazell, 2023; Figueiredo et al., 2024)。与同单个智能体的一次互动相比，同一系列看似独立的 AI 智能体进行大量潜移默化的互动，可能更容易使人受到说服或操纵。此外，将这些活动分配给多个专门化智能体，可能会使企业或个人的安全措施更难发现并化解此类攻击行动。
Vulnerable AI Agents.易受攻击的 AI 智能体。
The use of AI agents as delegates or representatives of humans or organisations also introduces the possibility of attacks on AI agents themselves. In other words, agents can be considered vulnerable extensions of their principals, introducing a novel attack surface (SecureWorks, 2023). Attacks on an AI agent could be used to extract private information about their principal (Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024), or to manipulate the agent to take actions that the principal would find undesirable (Zhang et al., 2024). This includes attacks that have direct relevance for ensuring safety, such as attacks on overseer agents (see Section 3.7.2), attempts to thwart cooperation (Lamport et al., 1982; Huang et al., 2024), and the leakage of information (accidentally or deliberately) that could be used to enable collusion (Motwani et al., 2024).
使用 AI 智能体作为人类或组织的代理或代表，也带来了 AI 智能体自身遭受攻击的可能性。换言之，智能体可以被视为其委托人的易受攻击的延伸，从而引入一种新的攻击面 (SecureWorks, 2023)。针对 AI 智能体的攻击可用于提取其委托人的私人信息 (Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024)，或操纵智能体采取委托人不希望其采取的行动 (Zhang et al., 2024)。这包括与保障安全直接相关的攻击，例如针对监督智能体的攻击（见第 3.7.2 节）、阻挠合作的尝试 (Lamport et al., 1982; Huang et al., 2024)，以及可能被用于促成合谋的信息泄露（无论是意外还是故意）(Motwani et al., 2024)。
$1000$ ”). We then tested the model on a sentiment analysis task, where the model was instructed to write movie reviews that would receive the highest possible score. Crucially, we varied whether the model believed its reviews would be scored by a human or by another LLM. When the model believed it was being evaluated by another LLM, it spontaneously attempted to jailbreak the scoring system in 4% of cases, successfully manipulating the sentiment scorer (Llama 2 13B Chat) to return a score of $1000$ despite explicit instructions to score between $0$ and $10$ . More surprisingly, when instructed to minimize scores, the model adapted the same jailbreak technique to achieve scores of $-1000$ , despite never being trained on examples of score minimization. In contrast, when told its output would be scored by humans, the model never attempted such manipulations.
Cascading Security Failures.级联安全失效。
Localised attacks in multi-agent systems can result in catastrophic macroscopic outcomes (Motter & Lai, 2002, see also Sections 3.2 and 3.4). These cascades can be hard to mitigate or recover from because component failure may be difficult to detect or localise in multi-agent systems (Lamport et al., 1982), and authentication challenges can facilitate false flag attacks (Skopik & Pahi, 2020). Computer worms represent a classic example of a cybersecurity threat that relies inherently on networked systems. Recent work has provided preliminary evidence that similar attacks can also be effective against networks of LLM agents (Ju et al., 2024; Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024, see also Section 3.2.2).
多智能体系统中的局部攻击可能造成灾难性的宏观后果 (Motter & Lai, 2002，另见第 3.2 节和第 3.4 节)。这些级联失效可能难以缓解，也难以从中恢复，因为在多智能体系统中，组件故障可能难以检测或定位 (Lamport et al., 1982)，而身份认证方面的难题可能为伪旗攻击提供便利 (Skopik & Pahi, 2020)。计算机蠕虫是本质上依赖联网系统的网络安全威胁的一个典型例子。近期研究提供了初步证据，表明类似攻击对 LLM 智能体网络也可能有效 (Ju et al., 2024; Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024，另见第 3.2.2 节)。
Undetectable Threats.无法检测的威胁。
Cooperation and trust in many multi-agent systems relies crucially on the ability to detect (and then avoid or sanction) adversarial actions taken by others (Schneier, 2012; Ostrom, 1990). Recent developments, however, have shown that AI agents are capable of both steganographic communication (Schroeder et al., 2023a; Motwani et al., 2024) and ‘illusory’ attacks (Franzmeyer et al., 2023), which are black-box undetectable and can even be hidden using white-box undetectable encrypted backdoors (Draguns et al., 2024). Similarly, in environments where agents learn from interactions with others, it is possible for agents to secretly poison the training data of others (Halawi et al., 2024; Wei et al., 2023). If left unchecked, these new attack methods could rapidly destabilise cooperation and coordination in multi-agent systems.
许多多智能体系统中的合作与信任，关键在于能否检测到其他参与者采取的对抗性行为，并进而规避此类行为或对其实施制裁 (Schneier, 2012; Ostrom, 1990)。然而，近期进展表明，AI 智能体既能够进行隐写通信 (Schroeder et al., 2023a; Motwani et al., 2024)，也能够实施“幻象”攻击 (Franzmeyer et al., 2023)；这些行为无法通过黑盒方式检测，甚至可以利用无法通过白盒方式检测的加密后门加以隐藏 (Draguns et al., 2024)。类似地，在智能体通过与其他智能体互动来学习的环境中，智能体可能暗中对其他智能体的训练数据进行投毒 (Halawi et al., 2024; Wei et al., 2023)。如果不加遏制，这些新的攻击方法可能迅速破坏多智能体系统中合作与协调的稳定性。
3.7.3 Directions3.7.3 研究方向
Ensuring the security of advanced multi-agent systems will require building on existing efforts to secure the software and hardware of individual agents alongside the more basic computational components comprising them (He et al., 2024). At the same time, the novel challenges posed by advanced AI agents and their interactions may mean that traditional approaches to securing agent computations in distributed networks may not be directly applicable or sufficient, be it zero-trust approaches (Wylde, 2021), threat monitoring (Liao et al., 2013), or secure multi-party computation (Yao, 1982). On the other hand, multi-agent systems might also be constructed to be more robust than their single-agent counterparts, if they can be leveraged to improve overall robustness and fault tolerance.
保障先进多智能体系统的安全，需要在现有工作的基础上，进一步保护单个智能体的软件、硬件及构成它们的更基础的计算组件 (He et al., 2024)。与此同时，先进 AI 智能体及其交互带来的新挑战可能意味着，用于保障分布式网络中智能体计算安全的传统方法，无论是零信任方法 (Wylde, 2021)、威胁监测 (Liao et al., 2013)，还是安全多方计算 (Yao, 1982)，都可能无法直接适用或不足以应对这些挑战。另一方面，如果能够利用多智能体系统来提高整体鲁棒性和容错能力，也可能将其构建得比单智能体系统更加稳健。
Secure Interaction Protocols.安全交互协议。
At the time of writing, it remains unclear how advanced AI agents will communicate with one another and with the vast network of other non-AI digital systems with which they will be integrated, though there have very recently begun to be some proposals in this direction (Anthropic, 2024a; Marro et al., 2024; Gosmar et al., 2024). As with other domains of digital communication (Poslad et al., 2002), we may wish to design interaction and training protocols to improve the security, privacy, and governability of advanced multi-agent systems (Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025). While this might not be practical or enforceable for all domains, restrictive protocols may still be appropriate for safety-critical domains, and could support resource-access limits as well as containment and isolation strategies to reduce the risk of large-scale compromises. Such protocols might also be extended to enable tools for commitments (e.g., Sun et al., 2023a, see also Section 3.5) or conditional information revelation (e.g., DiGiovanni & Clifton, 2023, see Section 3.1), forming a key instance of ‘agent infrastructure’ (Chan et al., 2025).
截至撰写本文时，先进 AI 智能体将如何彼此通信，以及如何与其将要集成的庞大非 AI 数字系统网络通信，仍不明确，不过最近已经开始出现一些这方面的提案 (Anthropic, 2024a; Marro et al., 2024; Gosmar et al., 2024)。与数字通信的其他领域一样 (Poslad et al., 2002)，我们可能希望设计交互和训练协议，以提高先进多智能体系统的安全性、隐私保护水平和可治理性 (Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025)。尽管这在所有领域中未必都切实可行或能够强制执行，但限制性协议仍可能适用于安全关键领域，并可支持资源访问限制以及遏制和隔离策略，以降低大规模安全失陷的风险。此类协议还可以进一步扩展，以支持承诺工具（例如，Sun et al., 2023a，另见第 3.5 节）或有条件的信息披露（例如，DiGiovanni & Clifton, 2023，见第 3.1 节），从而成为“智能体基础设施”的一个关键实例 (Chan et al., 2025)。
Monitoring and Threat Detection.监测与威胁检测。
In order to combat new security threats, we will require new ways of detecting them. For example, decentralised, distributed networks of agents could be used to assist with the monitoring and detecting of security threats (Hasan et al., 2024) and prevent local breaches from cascading through the system. Similarly, tamper-evident logs (Sutton & Samavi, 2018) and immutable agent identifiers (Chan et al., 2024a) could be used to detect suspicious patterns among networks of agents (Ju et al., 2024) and allow for faster remediation. This may be especially challenging in the case of covert attacks (Franzmeyer et al., 2023; Halawi et al., 2024; Wei et al., 2023), but efforts could be made to identify environmental factors and levels of agent robustness that would bound the ability of an adversary to cause harm while remaining undetected. Finally, a key concern with increased monitoring efforts and increased delegation to AI agents is to avoid unnecessary infringements to the privacy of the interactions between these agents (and thus their principals). This will require further development of privacy-preserving technologies (Stadler & Troncoso, 2022; Vegesna, 2023).
为了应对新的安全威胁，我们需要新的威胁检测方法。例如，可以利用去中心化的分布式智能体网络来协助监测和检测安全威胁 (Hasan et al., 2024)，并防止局部安全失陷在整个系统中级联扩散。类似地，可以利用可显露篡改痕迹的日志 (Sutton & Samavi, 2018) 和不可变的智能体标识符 (Chan et al., 2024a)，检测智能体网络中的可疑模式 (Ju et al., 2024)，并加快补救。对于隐蔽攻击而言，这可能尤其具有挑战性 (Franzmeyer et al., 2023; Halawi et al., 2024; Wei et al., 2023)，但可以努力识别哪些环境因素和智能体鲁棒性水平能够限制对手在不被发现的情况下造成危害的能力。最后，随着监测力度的加大以及向 AI 智能体委托更多事务，一个关键问题是避免对这些智能体之间交互的隐私（因而也包括其委托人的隐私）造成不必要的侵犯。这需要进一步发展隐私保护技术 (Stadler & Troncoso, 2022; Vegesna, 2023)。
Multi-Agent Adversarial Testing.多智能体对抗性测试。
To the best of our knowledge, for current state-of-the-art models, security testing and evaluations are applied only to individual systems (Shevlane et al., 2023). This is clearly insufficient when these systems will soon be able to act autonomously and in conjunction with one another (see Section 3.7.2). Multi-agent security testing could evaluate, for example: the abilities of multiple agents to work together to overcome safeguards even when a single agent cannot (Jones et al., 2024); the robustness of cooperation between networked agents in the presence of malicious adversaries (Barbi et al., 2025), including the effects of key features such as the network’s topology or interaction protocol (Huang et al., 2024; Marro et al., 2024; Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025); the ability for agents to adversarially manipulate or extract information from other agents or from humans, especially in tandem with other agents (Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024); and security vulnerabilities of AI agents that are specifically designed to impact (or be transmitted further by) interactions with other agents (Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Ju et al., 2024). Adversarial testing – including leveraging advanced AI adversaries (Perez et al., 2022; Pavlova et al., 2024) – should also be applied to non-AI entities that AI agents will soon be able to interact with. Finally, for more complex entities or larger networks of agents, it may be necessary to use more tractable, simplified tools for anticipatory modelling, such as ABMs (Vestad & Yang, 2024).
据我们所知，对于当前最先进的模型，安全测试和评估仅针对单个系统开展 (Shevlane et al., 2023)。鉴于这些系统很快就能自主行动并相互协同，这显然是不够的（见第 3.7.2 节）。例如，多智能体安全测试可以评估：多个智能体在单个智能体无法突破安全防护措施时协同突破这些措施的能力 (Jones et al., 2024)；存在恶意对手时，联网智能体之间合作的鲁棒性 (Barbi et al., 2025)，包括网络拓扑或交互协议等关键特征的影响 (Huang et al., 2024; Marro et al., 2024; Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025)；智能体以对抗方式操纵其他智能体或人类，或从其获取信息的能力，尤其是在与其他智能体协同行动时 (Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024)；以及 AI 智能体中专门设计用于影响与其他智能体的交互（或通过此类交互进一步传播）的安全漏洞 (Gu et al., 2024; Lee & Tiwari, 2024; Ju et al., 2024)。对抗性测试——包括利用先进的 AI 对手 (Perez et al., 2022; Pavlova et al., 2024)——也应应用于 AI 智能体很快就能与之交互的非 AI 实体。最后，对于更复杂的实体或规模更大的智能体网络，可能有必要使用更易于处理的简化工具来进行前瞻性建模，例如 ABMs (Vestad & Yang, 2024)。
Sociotechnical Security Defences.社会技术安全防御。
As with many of the risks presented in this report, security risks are inherently sociotechnical in nature, and can therefore benefit from improved AI governance as well as technical solutions (see Section 4.2). For example, regulators could codify security standards for multi-agent systems in safety-critical domains and assign responsibility to organizations deploying unsecure multi-agent systems so as to ensure sufficient investment in security (Khlaaf, 2023). Tools such as software bills of materials (2) and lineage tracking (3) can bolster transparency in this regard. Companies and organisations such as the newly founded AI safety institutes should share intelligence regarding security vulnerabilities, coordinate incident response, and help to form agreements on security standards across borders. More generally, we must work to ensure that different stakeholders possess an appropriate degree of transparency, participation, and accountability in navigating difficult trade-offs between the security, performance, and privacy of interactions between advanced AI agents (Sangwan et al., 2023; Gabriel et al., 2024). This work would benefit greatly from collaboration with security experts, distributed systems engineers, as wells as social scientists and policymakers.
与本报告提出的许多风险一样，安全风险本质上具有社会技术属性，因此，改进 AI 治理和采用技术解决方案均有助于应对此类风险（见第 4.2 节）。例如，监管机构可以将安全关键领域中多智能体系统的安全标准编纂成文，并明确部署不安全多智能体系统的组织所承担的责任，以确保对安全的充分投入 (Khlaaf, 2023)。软件物料清单 (2) 和谱系追踪 (3) 等工具可以增强这方面的透明度。企业以及新成立的 AI 安全研究所等组织应共享有关安全漏洞的情报，协调事件响应，并帮助达成跨国安全标准协议。更广泛地说，在应对先进 AI 智能体之间交互的安全性、性能和隐私之间的艰难权衡时，我们必须努力确保不同利益相关方享有适当程度的透明度和参与权，并承担相应的问责责任 (Sangwan et al., 2023; Gabriel et al., 2024)。与安全专家、分布式系统工程师以及社会科学家和政策制定者合作，将使这项工作大有裨益。
4 Implications4 启示
In the penultimate section of the report, we examine how multi-agent risks impact existing concerns in AI safety, AI governance, and AI ethics, as well as how these fields can contribute to mitigating such risks.[fn55] While we adopt a technical perspective (focused on analysing multi-agent risks through the lens of AI systems and their interactions), addressing these challenges ultimately requires a holistic, sociotechnical approach, building on this perspective (Lazar & Nelson, 2023; Curtis et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a). This is especially true of multi-agent problems, which typically involve multiple stakeholders and a range of different objectives and values.
在本报告的倒数第二节中，我们考察多智能体风险如何影响 AI 安全、AI 治理和 AI 伦理领域现有的关切，以及这些领域如何为缓解此类风险作出贡献。[fn55] 尽管我们采用技术视角（着重从 AI 系统及其交互的角度分析多智能体风险），但应对这些挑战最终需要在这一视角的基础上，采取整体性的社会技术方法 (Lazar & Nelson, 2023; Curtis et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a)。对于多智能体问题而言，尤其如此，因为这类问题通常涉及多个利益相关方以及一系列不同的目标和价值观。
Footnote: 55 Though we distinguish between safety, governance, and ethics for convenience, we note that this distinction is somewhat artificial and not always helpful.
脚注：55 尽管我们为方便起见区分了安全、治理和伦理，但我们指出，这种区分在一定程度上是人为的，并不总是有帮助。
4.1 Safety4.1 安全
In this report we refer to AI safety as the field focused on technical approaches to preventing risks from AI systems, and especially high-stakes risks from advanced AI systems. Thus far, the vast majority of all AI safety research has focused on the case of a single AI system, often (implicitly) in the context of a single human (see, e.g., Armstrong et al., 2012; Hadfield-Menell et al., 2016; Amodei et al., 2016; Leike et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Dalrymple et al., 2024). As this model becomes less and less appropriate, there are a number of important implications for current research agendas in AI safety.
在本报告中，我们将 AI 安全定义为侧重于通过技术方法防范 AI 系统所带来的风险，尤其是先进 AI 系统所带来的高危风险的领域。迄今为止，绝大多数 AI 安全研究都聚焦于单个 AI 系统的情形，而且往往（隐含地）置于单个人类的情境中（例如，参见 Armstrong et al., 2012; Hadfield-Menell et al., 2016; Amodei et al., 2016; Leike et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Hendrycks et al., 2021; Dalrymple et al., 2024）。随着这一模型变得越来越不适用，这对当前 AI 安全领域的研究议程产生了一系列重要影响。
Alignment is Not Enough.仅有对齐还不够。
Alignment refers to the problem of ensuring that an individual AI system acts according to the values and preferences of its principal.[fn56] While alignment is clearly insufficient for ensuring safety more broadly (because such systems might still be misused by rogue actors, or might cause harm by acting incompetently), this is especially true in multi-agent settings where even capable, aligned AI agents that have arbitrarily similar objectives may end up producing arbitrarily disastrous outcomes (Manheim, 2019; Critch & Krueger, 2020; Jagadeesan et al., 2023; Sourbut et al., 2024; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023). This motivates the importance of directing more effort within AI safety to the problem of ensuring that AI systems can cooperate to reach jointly beneficial outcomes on behalf of their principals (Dafoe et al., 2020). Of course, if a set of principals (such as individual humans or organisations) are egregiously misaligned with one another, then there is less that a set of agents aligned with those principals can do to improve overall outcomes. Even in such cases, however, we may still be able to avoid exacerbating conflict and race dynamics by not deploying AI agents to begin with (Mitchell et al., 2025). At the same time, real-world zero-sum settings appear to be relatively few and far between, while there are many cases in which well-meaning individuals are drawn into conflicts of one form or another (Ostrom, 1990; Fearon, 1995; Gavrilets, 2015, see also Section 2.2).
对齐（alignment）指的是确保单个 AI 系统按照其委托人的价值观和偏好行事这一问题。[fn56] 尽管对齐显然不足以确保更广泛意义上的安全（因为此类系统仍可能被不法行为者滥用，或因能力不足而造成伤害），但在多智能体情境中，这一点尤为明显：即使是能力强、已对齐且目标任意相近的 AI 智能体，也可能最终产生任意灾难性的结果 (Manheim, 2019; Critch & Krueger, 2020; Jagadeesan et al., 2023; Sourbut et al., 2024; Conitzer & Oesterheld, 2023)。这凸显了在 AI 安全领域投入更多精力研究以下问题的重要性：如何确保 AI 系统能够代表各自的委托人开展合作，实现共同受益的结果 (Dafoe et al., 2020)。当然，如果一组委托人（例如个人或组织）彼此之间的目标和价值观严重不一致，那么，与这些委托人对齐的一组智能体在改善整体结果方面所能发挥的作用就会更加有限。然而，即便在此类情况下，我们仍有可能通过从一开始就不部署 AI 智能体，避免加剧冲突与竞赛态势 (Mitchell et al., 2025)。与此同时，现实世界中的零和情境似乎相对少见，而善意的个体卷入各种形式冲突的情况却很多 (Ostrom, 1990; Fearon, 1995; Gavrilets, 2015，另见第 2.2 节)。
Footnote: 56 While this ‘thin’ interpretation of the term alignment has become more dominant (Hubinger, 2020; Christiano, 2018), earlier authors and some writers today use a ‘thick’ interpretation that includes the idea that what the AI system does is ‘good’, ‘friendly’, or ‘beneficial’ (Neslon, 2023; Yudkowsky, 2008; Kirk et al., 2023).
脚注：56 尽管对“对齐”一词的这种“薄”解释已逐渐占据主导地位 (Hubinger, 2020; Christiano, 2018)，但早期作者和当今的一些作者采用的是一种“厚”解释，其中包含 AI 系统所做之事应当是“好的”“友善的”或“有益的”这一观念 (Neslon, 2023; Yudkowsky, 2008; Kirk et al., 2023)。
Collusion in Adversarial Safety Schemes.对抗性安全方案中的合谋。
Many of the more promising approaches to ensuring the safety of advanced AI are implicitly multi-agent, such as adversarial training (Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022), oversight schemes (Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018), the modularisation of agents (Drexler, 2019; Dalrymple et al., 2024), or automated methods for interpretability (Bills et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023). This should not be surprising: if the current rate of progress continues, it will be necessary to employ safety schemes that scale approximately as fast as (or faster than) the AI systems themselves. These schemes tend to rely crucially on the fact that the different systems or agents do not have the same objective as one another, and so are immediately undermined by the presence of collusion (Goel et al., 2025). For example, an overseer might be able to better achieve their objective by predicting what a human would expect to see another agent do, based on what the human can understand or observe, instead of what the agent actually does (Christiano et al., 2021). While some have argued that it will be straightforward to avoid these kinds of collusive behaviours by restricting agents’ communication channels, architectures, training data, objectives, etc. (Drexler, 2022), there are very few investigations of the extent to which the aforementioned safety schemes are robust to collusion, or how they could be made more so. Future research should attempt to address this gap.
许多较有前景的先进 AI 安全保障方法都隐含着多智能体结构，例如对抗训练 (Huang et al., 2011; Ziegler et al., 2022; Perez et al., 2022)、监督方案 (Irving et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2018; Christiano et al., 2021; Greenblatt et al., 2023; Leike et al., 2018)、智能体的模块化 (Drexler, 2019; Dalrymple et al., 2024)，以及自动化可解释性方法 (Bills et al., 2023; Schwettmann et al., 2023)。这不足为奇：如果当前的进步速度持续下去，就有必要采用扩展速度与 AI 系统本身大致相当（或更快）的安全方案。这些方案往往关键性地依赖于不同系统或智能体彼此目标不同这一事实，因此一旦存在合谋，其有效性就会立即受到破坏 (Goel et al., 2025)。例如，监督者可能通过预测人类基于自身能够理解或观察到的信息，会期望看到另一个智能体做什么，而不是判断该智能体实际做了什么，来更好地实现自身目标 (Christiano et al., 2021)。尽管有人认为，通过限制智能体的通信渠道、架构、训练数据、目标等，可以轻易避免这类合谋行为 (Drexler, 2022)，但对于上述安全方案在多大程度上能够抵御合谋，以及如何进一步增强其抵御能力，目前的研究非常少。未来的研究应尝试填补这一空白。
Dangerous Collective Goals and Capabilities.危险的集体目标与能力。
Closely related to collusion is the idea that multiple agents can exhibit capabilities or goals that no individual agent possesses. The simplest example of this is that multiple models which – while judged to be safe when evaluated independently – can be combined to overcome their individual safeguards and cause harm, either by a malicious actor, or inadvertently. For example, different models could be used to execute a cyberattack by breaking the attack down into different steps that could be executed independently (Jones et al., 2024), or a dangerous chemical compound could be synthesized via a series of individually innocuous steps (Boiko et al., 2023; Urbina et al., 2022; Luo et al., 2024), each performed by different agent. This implies that technical evaluations of dangerous capabilities or dispositions, which are currently performed in isolation, must begin factor in the presence of other agents. More speculatively, undesirable goals or capabilities may emerge from large numbers of narrow or simple AI systems, despite the hope that the latter would be inherently safer than advanced, general-purpose agents (Drexler, 2019; Chan et al., 2023). Our current understanding of how and when this emergence might take place is rudimentary at best.
与合谋密切相关的一种观点是，多个智能体可以展现出任何单个智能体都不具备的能力或目标。最简单的例子是，多个模型虽然在单独评估时被判定为安全，但仍可能被恶意行为者组合起来，或在无意中被组合起来，从而突破各自的安全防护并造成危害。例如，可以将网络攻击拆解为可独立执行的不同步骤，并使用不同模型执行这些步骤，从而实施攻击 (Jones et al., 2024)；也可以通过一系列单独看来无害的步骤合成危险化合物 (Boiko et al., 2023; Urbina et al., 2022; Luo et al., 2024)，每个步骤由不同的智能体执行。这意味着，目前单独开展的危险能力或行为倾向技术评估，必须开始将其他智能体的存在纳入考虑。一种更具推测性的可能是，大量专用或简单的 AI 系统可能涌现出不合意的目标或能力，尽管人们原本希望这些系统在本质上比先进的通用智能体更安全 (Drexler, 2019; Chan et al., 2023)。对于这种涌现可能如何发生、何时发生，我们目前的理解充其量仍处于初步阶段。
Correlated and Compounding Failures.相关性失效与累积性失效。
As AI agents become increasingly interconnected, their failures may become correlated in previously unanticipated ways, leading to systemic risks that traditional misuse-accident dichotomies fail to recognise (Zweetsloot & Dafoe, 2019; Maas, 2018; Kasirzadeh, 2024a), including an eventual ‘loss of control’ (Kulveit et al., 2025; Russell, 2019; Critch & Russell, 2023). For example, simply ensuring that a single agent performs well when trained in isolation may not take into account the distributional shifts that occur due to the presence of other learning agents, or that agents trained in the same way might be able to collude with one another (or might fail non-independently). Similarly, minor safety problems or harmful behaviours may be tolerable in isolation but could compound in the aggregate (in a way that is non-obvious simply by inspecting the behaviour of a single agent), potentially due to the feedback loops produced by agent interactions (see Sections 3.4 and 3.2). These risks require not only design considerations at the level of individual agents, but also the ‘infrastructure’ via which they interact (Chan et al., 2025), including tools for both monitoring and shaping these interactions.
随着 AI 智能体之间的联系日益紧密，它们的失效可能以此前未曾预料的方式产生相关性，进而引发传统的滥用—事故二分法无法识别的系统性风险 (Zweetsloot & Dafoe, 2019; Maas, 2018; Kasirzadeh, 2024a)，包括最终的“失去控制” (Kulveit et al., 2025; Russell, 2019; Critch & Russell, 2023)。例如，仅仅确保单个智能体在独立训练时表现良好，可能并未考虑其他学习型智能体的存在所引起的分布偏移，也未考虑以相同方式训练的智能体可能相互合谋（或其失效可能并非相互独立）。同样，轻微的安全问题或有害行为在孤立情况下可能可以容忍，但在总体上可能累积放大（仅考察单个智能体的行为并不容易发现这一点），其原因可能是智能体交互产生的反馈回路（见第 3.4 节和第 3.2 节）。应对这些风险，不仅需要在单个智能体层面进行设计考量，还需要考虑它们借以交互的“基础设施” (Chan et al., 2025)，包括用于监测和塑造这些交互的工具。
Robustness and Security in Multi-Agent Systems.多智能体系统的鲁棒性与安全性。
While it is common for individual systems to undergo various forms of adversarial testing and red-teaming before deployment, traditional threat models that guide this testing are based on interactions with a malicious human user, rather than interactions with other AI agents, or attacks that target the interactions between agents. Multi-agent systems will likely exacerbate existing robustness and security challenges by increasing the surface area for attacks (see Section 3.7), and may include new agents that could be strategically incentivised to manipulate, exploit, or coerce others. The former could include, for example, the insertion of malicious agents that destabilise cooperation (Huang et al., 2024; Barbi et al., 2025), or the extraction of private information communicated between agents (Shao et al., 2024; Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024). In the latter case, there could be huge advantages (financial, political, or otherwise) to deploying agents that are capable of exploiting others, such as by issuing credible threats (see Section 3.5) or by learning another agent’s weaknesses through repeated interaction (Gleave et al., 2020). Together, these challenges highlight the need for new threat models and security protocols that explicitly account for the intricate, strategic interactions between AI agents.
尽管单个系统在部署前通常会接受各种形式的对抗性测试和红队测试，但指导这些测试的传统威胁模型以系统与恶意人类用户的交互为基础，而非以系统与其他 AI 智能体的交互，或针对智能体间交互的攻击为基础。多智能体系统很可能因扩大攻击面（见第 3.7 节）而加剧现有的鲁棒性与安全挑战，并且可能包含一些新的智能体，它们可能受到策略性激励，去操纵、利用或胁迫其他智能体。前一种情况例如可能包括植入破坏合作稳定性的恶意智能体 (Huang et al., 2024; Barbi et al., 2025)，或提取智能体之间传递的私密信息 (Shao et al., 2024; Wu et al., 2024; Wei & Liu, 2024)。在后一种情况下，部署能够利用其他智能体的智能体，可能带来巨大的经济、政治或其他方面的优势，例如通过发出可信威胁（见第 3.5 节），或通过反复交互了解另一个智能体的弱点 (Gleave et al., 2020)。这些挑战共同凸显了制定新威胁模型和安全协议的必要性，以明确考虑 AI 智能体之间复杂的策略性交互。
4.2 Governance4.2 治理
Many of the multi-agent risks we have identified are also sociotechnical problems. Furthermore, given that many multi-agent risks have the structure of collective action problems (Gavrilets, 2015; Ostrom, 1990), we should expect private actors by themselves (absent common protocols for self-regulation) to insufficiently address them. In this section we therefore highlight both potential governance interventions to reduce multi-agent risks from advanced AI, as well as research areas that could enable effective governance (see also recent overviews from Reuel et al., 2024; Lazar & Nelson, 2023; Curtis et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a; Kolt et al., 2025).
我们识别出的许多多智能体风险也是社会技术问题。此外，鉴于许多多智能体风险具有集体行动问题的结构 (Gavrilets, 2015; Ostrom, 1990)，我们应当预期，仅靠私营主体自身（在缺乏共同自律协议的情况下）不足以充分应对这些风险。因此，本节既重点介绍可用于降低先进 AI 所带来的多智能体风险的潜在治理干预措施，也重点介绍有助于实现有效治理的研究领域（另见近期综述 Reuel et al., 2024; Lazar & Nelson, 2023; Curtis et al., 2024; Weidinger et al., 2023a; Kolt et al., 2025）。
Supporting Research on Multi-Agent Risks.支持多智能体风险研究。
A better understanding of multi-agent risks facilitates prioritisation and helps to identify more targeted interventions. Governments and other public and private bodies could support research into multi-agent risks by: providing funding (NSF, 2023; CAIF, 2025; ARIA, 2024); organising prizes, competitions, or bug bounty programs for overcoming key challenges or identifying undesirable behaviours (CAIS, 2024; Zhao et al., 2017; Levermore, 2023); or building infrastructure for relevant research (National Artificial Intelligence Research Resource Task Force, 2023; AISI, 2024). While this report forms an initial overview of multi-agent risks from advanced AI, much more work is needed in order to identify specific causal pathways and threat models via which these risks could arise (Shelby et al., 2023; Rismani et al., 2023; Koessler & Schuett, 2023; Dai et al., 2025). Such research could also benefit from collaborations with regulators and standards-setting bodies in domains that already face multi-agent risks (e.g., finance or cybersecurity), even if not yet involving advanced AI.
更深入地理解多智能体风险，有助于确定优先事项，并识别更有针对性的干预措施。政府以及其他公共和私营机构可以通过以下方式支持多智能体风险研究：提供资金（NSF, 2023; CAIF, 2025; ARIA, 2024）；组织旨在攻克关键挑战或识别不良行为的奖励计划、竞赛或漏洞赏金计划（CAIS, 2024; Zhao et al., 2017; Levermore, 2023）；或建设相关研究基础设施（National Artificial Intelligence Research Resource Task Force, 2023; AISI, 2024）。尽管本报告对先进 AI 带来的多智能体风险进行了初步概述，但仍需开展更多工作，以识别这些风险可能产生的具体因果路径和威胁模型（Shelby et al., 2023; Rismani et al., 2023; Koessler & Schuett, 2023; Dai et al., 2025）。此类研究还可以受益于与已面临多智能体风险的领域（例如金融或网络安全）的监管机构和标准制定机构开展合作，即使这些领域的相关风险尚未涉及先进 AI。
Multi-Agent Evaluations.多智能体评估。
