Model evaluations form a crucial part of contemporary AI governance practices, providing a better understanding of a system’s potentially dangerous capabilities and dispositions (Shevlane et al., 2023; Kinniment et al., 2023; Reuel et al., 2024; Hardy et al., 2024; Chen et al., 2024b) and informing regulatory efforts to restrict the deployment of certain systems in certain domains or increase regulatory scrutiny (EU, 2024, as in, for example, Article 51 of the EU AI Act,). Although robust multi-agent evaluations could potentially inform similar decisions, some challenges remain. First, and most obviously, challenges from evaluating single systems are also present in multi-agent contexts, including contamination, validity concerns, and the discrepancy between evaluation tasks and real-world applicability (Reuel et al., 2024a; Hardy et al., 2024), as well as the challenges brought about by evaluating agents as opposed to less advanced, autonomous AI systems (Kapoor et al., 2024; Siegel et al., 2024; Stroebl et al., 2025). Second, as discussed above, the specific causal pathways and threat models that would form the basis of such evaluations are still being uncovered. Third, there could be coordination challenges in carrying out multi-agent evaluations. For example, developers may need to coordinate on safety testing since their agents could interact with each other in the real world, but concerns about commercial sensitivity could be a barrier. Governments could have a role in facilitating such coordination, such as through AI safety institutes and the Frontier Model Forum (Thurnherr et al., 2025).
模型评估是当代 AI 治理实践的重要组成部分，有助于更好地理解系统潜在的危险能力和行为倾向（Shevlane et al., 2023; Kinniment et al., 2023; Reuel et al., 2024; Hardy et al., 2024; Chen et al., 2024b），并为限制某些系统在特定领域的部署或加强监管审查的监管工作提供依据（EU, 2024，例如《欧盟人工智能法案》第 51 条）。尽管稳健的多智能体评估有望为类似决策提供依据，但仍存在一些挑战。首先，也是最明显的一点，评估单个系统时面临的挑战同样存在于多智能体情境中，包括数据污染、有效性方面的疑虑，以及评估任务与现实世界适用性之间的差距（Reuel et al., 2024a; Hardy et al., 2024），此外，相较于评估较不先进的自主 AI 系统，评估智能体本身也会带来挑战（Kapoor et al., 2024; Siegel et al., 2024; Stroebl et al., 2025）。其次，如上文所述，作为此类评估基础的具体因果路径和威胁模型仍在探索之中。第三，开展多智能体评估可能面临协调方面的挑战。例如，由于开发者各自的智能体可能在现实世界中相互交互，他们可能需要协调安全测试工作，但对商业敏感性的顾虑可能构成障碍。政府可以在促进此类协调方面发挥作用，例如通过 AI 安全研究所和 Frontier Model Forum（Thurnherr et al., 2025）。
New Forms of Documentation.新的文档记录形式。
Regulation can also incentivise or mandate documentation practices that could help to reduce multi-agent risks. For example, AI development often relies upon shared tools, dependencies, and processes, which can make correlated failures like algorithmic monoculture (Kleinberg & Raghavan, 2021) or outcome homogenization (Bommasani et al., 2022) more likely. Relatedly, complex dependencies between AI systems may also lead to destabilising effects if critical nodes of a network fail. Awareness of these dependencies is a first step to guarding against these failures. Standard documentation tools for single systems – such as datasheets (Gebru et al., 2021), data statements (Bender & Friedman, 2018), and model cards (Mitchell et al., 2019) – can be complemented with other forms of documentation that track ecosystem-wide and interaction risks. For example, Bommasani et al. (2023) propose ‘ecosystem graphs’, which document various aspects of the AI ecosystem (e.g., datasets, models, use cases) and how they relate to each other (e.g., technical and business dependencies), and Gilbert et al. (2023) propose ‘reward reports’, which document agents that continue to learn and adapt after deployment.
监管还可以激励或强制实施有助于降低多智能体风险的文档记录实践。例如，AI 开发通常依赖共享的工具、依赖项和流程，这可能使算法单一化（algorithmic monoculture）（Kleinberg & Raghavan, 2021）或结果同质化（outcome homogenization）（Bommasani et al., 2022）等相关性失效更容易发生。与此相关，如果网络中的关键节点失效，AI 系统之间复杂的依赖关系也可能产生破坏稳定性的影响。了解这些依赖关系是防范此类失效的第一步。针对单个系统的标准文档记录工具——例如数据说明书（datasheets）（Gebru et al., 2021）、数据声明（data statements）（Bender & Friedman, 2018）和模型卡（model cards）（Mitchell et al., 2019）——可以辅以其他形式的文档记录，以追踪整个生态系统层面的风险及交互风险。例如，Bommasani et al. (2023) 提出了“生态系统图”（ecosystem graphs），用于记录 AI 生态系统的各个方面（例如数据集、模型、用例）及其相互关系（例如技术和商业依赖关系）；Gilbert et al. (2023) 则提出了“奖励报告”（reward reports），用于记录部署后仍持续学习和适应的智能体。
Infrastructure for AI Agents.AI 智能体基础设施。
Just as new infrastructure was needed to enable the internet (e.g., TCP/IP, HTTP) and secure it (e.g., SSL), so too might new infrastructure be needed to reap the benefits and manage the risks of multi-agent systems (Chan et al., 2025). For example, agent IDs could enable improved monitoring and the establishment of trust among agents (Chan et al., 2024a), new communication protocols could improve stability and security in safety-critical domains (Marro et al., 2024; Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025), and the ability to undo agent actions could prevent miscoordination or escalation (Patil et al., 2024). Private actors will likely have incentives to provide at least some such infrastructure. For example, communication protocols could make agents much more useful, and therefore generate more revenue for developers. Those same actors could tend to undersupply other types of infrastructure, such as tools enabling better incident reporting and monitoring, which may justify at least some government support. Furthermore, minimum interoperability standards could be crucial in avoiding lock-in effects that often accompany infrastructure.[fn57]
正如互联网的实现需要新的基础设施（例如 TCP/IP、HTTP），其安全保障也需要新的基础设施（例如 SSL），要获得多智能体系统的益处并管理其风险，也可能需要新的基础设施（Chan et al., 2025）。例如，智能体 ID 可以改善监测并促进智能体之间建立信任（Chan et al., 2024a），新的通信协议可以提高安全关键领域的稳定性和安全性（Marro et al., 2024; Hammond & Adam-Day, 2025），而撤销智能体行动的能力则可以防止协调失误或事态升级（Patil et al., 2024）。私营主体很可能有动力提供至少一部分此类基础设施。例如，通信协议可以大幅提高智能体的实用性，从而为开发者带来更多收入。这些主体对其他类型基础设施的供给则可能不足，例如有助于改进事件报告和监测的工具，这可能构成政府至少提供一定支持的理由。此外，最低互操作性标准可能对于避免基础设施通常伴随的锁定效应至关重要。[fn57]
Footnote: 57 Analogously, social media lock-in effects make it difficult for new entrants to obtain users, even if those new entrants provide better features.
脚注：57 类似地，社交媒体的锁定效应使新进入者难以获取用户，即使这些新进入者提供了更好的功能。
Restrictions on Development and Deployment.对开发和部署的限制。
Restrictions on the development or deployment of certain multi-agent systems could be a useful regulatory tool (Anderljung et al., 2023; Mitchell et al., 2025), but it remains to be seen what such restrictions should entail and whether/where they are feasible. For example, if agents trained in multi-agent settings – especially settings that may reward strategic behaviour and deception – exacerbate certain risks (see Section 3.3), development standards could caution against the use of such training methods. Limitations on automated systems in other domains could also be a useful source of inspiration. For autonomous weapons, researchers have emphasised the need to maintain human control through measures such as giving humans the ability to intervene and terminate operation (Amoroso & Tamburrini, 2020; Renshaw & Hunnicutt, 2024; U.S. Congress, 2023, see also Section 3.5). In financial markets, simple interventions such as reducing the tick size[fn58] may reduce incentives for algorithmic collusion (Cartea et al., 2022), and automatic circuit breakers can be used to temporarily halt trading when prices move too dramatically (Subrahmanyam, 2013). However – especially in the case of open-source systems – agents might not be easily governed and curtailed post-deployment (Seger et al., 2023). Furthermore, implementing restrictions on multi-agent development and deployment faces governance challenges due to the international nature of these systems, with training data, infrastructure, and stakeholders distributed globally across diverse legislative and regulatory jurisdictions. This points to the need for coordinated international oversight, which has traditionally been slow in the AI domain (Trager et al., 2023).
限制某些多智能体系统的开发或部署可能是一种有用的监管工具 (Anderljung et al., 2023; Mitchell et al., 2025)，但此类限制应包含哪些内容，以及这些限制是否可行、在哪些情境下可行，仍有待观察。例如，如果在多智能体环境中训练的智能体——尤其是在可能奖励策略性行为和欺骗的环境中训练的智能体——会加剧某些风险（见第 3.3 节），那么开发标准可以告诫开发者慎用此类训练方法。其他领域对自动化系统的限制也可以提供有益的借鉴。对于自主武器，研究人员强调，需要通过赋予人类干预和终止运行的能力等措施来维持人类控制 (Amoroso & Tamburrini, 2020; Renshaw & Hunnicutt, 2024; U.S. Congress, 2023，另见第 3.5 节)。在金融市场中，缩小最小报价单位（tick size）[fn58]等简单干预措施可能降低算法合谋的动机 (Cartea et al., 2022)，而自动熔断机制则可用于在价格波动过于剧烈时暂时停止交易 (Subrahmanyam, 2013)。然而，智能体在部署后可能难以治理和约束，开源系统尤其如此 (Seger et al., 2023)。此外，由于多智能体系统具有国际性，其训练数据、基础设施和利益相关方分布于全球各地，横跨不同的立法和监管辖区，因此，对其开发和部署实施限制面临治理挑战。这表明需要协调一致的国际监督，而人工智能领域在这方面的进展历来较为缓慢 (Trager et al., 2023)。
Footnote: 58 The tick size is the minimum granularity in the movement of the price of a security.
脚注：58 最小报价单位是证券价格变动的最小幅度。
Liability for Harms from Multi-Agent Systems.多智能体系统所致损害的法律责任。
Holding a person liable for harms to persons or property from multi-agent systems poses two potential challenges.[fn59] First, it will often be unclear who, if anyone, would be liable for harms caused by a single agent (Kolt, 2024). Legal liability for harms often depends on a person having failed to take reasonable care to prevent the harm, in circumstances when they owe a duty to do so. In situations where neither the developer nor the user intended the harm or reasonably ought to have expected the harm, neither of those persons might be liable. Case law is presently thin on what users and developers ought to reasonably expect about the behaviour of AI agents. Second, even if it is clear which legal entity is responsible for a particular agent’s actions, it could be unclear how to allocate responsibility among multiple agents for a harm. Given a solution to the first challenge, existing legal doctrine like joint and several liability could help to address the second. For an in-depth exploration of these legal challenges – which are exacerbated by the international nature of the development, deployment, and use of multi-agent systems, as discussed above – we refer the reader to (Wills, 2024; Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992).
要求某个人对多智能体系统造成的人身或财产损害承担法律责任，面临两个潜在挑战。[fn59] 首先，对于单个智能体造成的损害，往往难以确定应由谁承担法律责任，甚至是否有人应当承担法律责任也不明确 (Kolt, 2024)。损害的法律责任通常取决于某人是否在负有防止损害发生的义务时，未尽到合理注意义务。如果开发者和用户均无意造成损害，而且从合理角度而言也都不应预见到该损害，那么双方可能都不承担法律责任。关于用户和开发者应当对人工智能智能体的行为抱有何种合理预期，目前的判例仍然很少。其次，即使明确了应由哪个法律实体对某个特定智能体的行为负责，也可能仍不清楚如何在多个智能体之间分配某项损害的责任。如果第一项挑战得到解决，连带责任等现有法律原则可能有助于应对第二项挑战。如上所述，多智能体系统的开发、部署和使用具有国际性，这进一步加剧了这些法律挑战；关于这些挑战的深入探讨，请读者参阅 (Wills, 2024; Ayres & Balkin, 2024; Lima, 2017; Lior, 2019; Chopra & White, 2011; Solum, 1992)。
Footnote: 59 The points in this paragraph benefited greatly from discussions with Peter Wills.
脚注：59 本段观点极大地受益于与 Peter Wills 的讨论。
Improving Societal Resilience.提高社会韧性。
Finally, safety-critical multi-agent systems must be integrated into society in a way that allows them to fail gracefully and gradually, as opposed to producing sudden, cascading failures (Maas, 2018; Bernardi et al., 2024). Indeed, there are many societal processes – ranging from the mundane to the critical – that function only because of physical limits on the number and capability of humans (e.g., Van, 2019, see also the examples in Section 2.2). Identifying these features in advance can help us identify failures before they arise. At the same time, the delegation to AI agents by a range of different individuals and organisations might make it easier to manage and represent their interests by making their agents the target of governance efforts, or the participants of new, more scalable methods of collective decision-making and cooperation (Seger et al., 2023a; Huang & Siddarth, 2023; Domingos et al., 2022; Sourbut et al., 2024; Terrucha et al., 2024; Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022). Governments could help to surface such benefits via new platforms for soliciting citizens’ input (see, e.g., Small et al., 2021; Small et al., 2023; Bakker et al., 2022; Fishkin et al., 2019; Ovadya, 2023; Jarrett et al., 2023; Fish et al., 2023), subsidizing access to AI resources in order to prevent ‘agentic inequality’ (Gabriel et al., 2024, see also Section 4.3), and monitoring for vulnerabilities introduced by the use of AI agents.
最后，安全关键型多智能体系统必须以一种允许其平稳、渐进地失效的方式融入社会，以免引发突发性的级联故障 (Maas, 2018; Bernardi et al., 2024)。事实上，许多社会过程——从日常琐事到关键事务——之所以能够运作，仅仅是因为人类的数量和能力受到物理限制（例如，Van, 2019，另见第 2.2 节中的示例）。提前识别这些特征，有助于我们在故障发生之前将其识别出来。与此同时，不同个人和组织将事务委托给人工智能智能体，可能使其利益更容易得到管理和表达，具体方式是将其智能体作为治理工作的对象，或使其参与新的、可扩展性更强的集体决策与合作方法 (Seger et al., 2023a; Huang & Siddarth, 2023; Domingos et al., 2022; Sourbut et al., 2024; Terrucha et al., 2024; Oesterheld & Conitzer, 2022)。政府可以通过搭建征求公民意见的新平台（参见，例如，Small et al., 2021; Small et al., 2023; Bakker et al., 2022; Fishkin et al., 2019; Ovadya, 2023; Jarrett et al., 2023; Fish et al., 2023）、补贴 AI 资源的使用以防止“智能体不平等”（agentic inequality）（Gabriel et al., 2024，另见第 4.3 节），以及监测使用 AI 智能体所引入的脆弱性，帮助发掘此类益处。
4.3 Ethics4.3 伦理
The deployment of any automated decision-making system brings to the fore a multitude of ethical considerations, such as fairness, bias and discrimination, value alignment, misinformation, legality, interpretability, privacy, and safety. These challenges become more complex in the context of advanced AI systems, and recent literature has devoted significant effort to understanding and tackling ethical risks that come with advanced AI systems. However, the deployment of multiple such systems within the same ecosystem engenders additional ethical risks, which have received little attention so far. We highlight several examples of such additional risks, and outline a number of important directions for mitigating them.
任何自动化决策系统的部署都会凸显诸多伦理考量，例如公平性、偏见与歧视、价值对齐、错误信息、合法性、可解释性、隐私和安全性。在先进 AI 系统的背景下，这些挑战变得更加复杂，近期文献已投入大量精力来理解和应对先进 AI 系统带来的伦理风险。然而，在同一生态系统中部署多个此类系统会产生额外的伦理风险，而这些风险迄今很少受到关注。我们重点列举了此类额外风险的若干示例，并概述了缓解这些风险的若干重要方向。
Pluralistic Alignment.多元主义对齐（Pluralistic Alignment）。
A partial solution to some of the problems with alignment described in Section 4.1 can be found in cases where a single AI agent can be used to act on behalf of multiple principals (Fickinger et al., 2020). This transforms the issue of cooperative competence into one of ensuring that the system acts (as far as possible) in a way that respects the preferences and values of all principals (Sorensen et al., 2024; Kasirzadeh, 2024; Desai et al., 2018). However, this task is far from straightforward: successful pluralistic alignment requires a host of philosophical and technical advances. There remains a wealth of insight from the field of social choice that might be applied (Prasad, 2018; Conitzer et al., 2024), such as the properties of different forms of aggregation and representation, and how to achieve incentive compatibility. For example, it was only recently shown that the most standard way of aggregating multiple preferences using RLHF corresponds to Borda count (Siththaranjan et al., 2024). At the same time, others argue that preference aggregation is neither necessary nor sufficient for meaningful pluralistic alignment (Zhi-Xuan et al., 2024), with alternatives including alignment using prioritarian (Gordon et al., 2022), egalitarian (Weidinger et al., 2023), or contractualist (Zhi-Xuan, 2022) approaches (see also Section 4.2). Another perspective is that of Gabriel et al. (2024), who introduce a different, tetradic model of alignment that centers upon building systems that do not unjustifiably favour one party (the user, developer, societal grouping) over others.
对于第 4.1 节所述的部分对齐问题，可以在使用单个 AI 智能体代表多个委托人行事的情形中找到一种局部解决方案（Fickinger et al., 2020）。这将协作能力问题转化为如何确保系统（尽可能）以尊重所有委托人的偏好和价值观的方式行事的问题（Sorensen et al., 2024; Kasirzadeh, 2024; Desai et al., 2018）。然而，这项任务远非易事：成功实现多元主义对齐需要一系列哲学和技术进展。社会选择领域仍有大量见解可供应用（Prasad, 2018; Conitzer et al., 2024），例如不同聚合和代表形式的性质，以及如何实现激励相容。例如，直到最近才有研究表明，使用 RLHF 聚合多种偏好的最常见方式对应于博尔达计数法（Borda count）（Siththaranjan et al., 2024）。与此同时，另一些研究者认为，偏好聚合既不是实现有实质意义的多元主义对齐的必要条件，也不是充分条件（Zhi-Xuan et al., 2024）；替代方案包括采用优先主义（Gordon et al., 2022）、平等主义（Weidinger et al., 2023）或契约主义（Zhi-Xuan, 2022）方法进行对齐（另见第 4.2 节）。Gabriel et al. (2024) 提出了另一种视角，引入了一种不同的四元对齐模型，其核心是构建不会无正当理由地偏袒某一方（用户、开发者、社会群体）而损及其他方的系统。
Agentic Inequality.智能体不平等（Agentic Inequality）。
It has been argued that the inequitable distribution of AI capabilities and other digital technologies has increased inequality in some domains (Mirza et al., 2019; Vassilakopoulou & Hustad, 2021). Once individuals begin to delegate more and more of their decision-making and actions to AI agents, these inequalities may be further entrenched based on the relative strength of different agents, or the relationship between those who have access to agents and those who do not (Gabriel et al., 2024). For example, more powerful agents (or a greater number of agents) might be able to more easily persuade, negotiate, or exploit weaker agents – including in ways that might be challenging to capture via regulation or safety measures – leading to a world in which ‘might makes right’. While today’s AI capabilities are not much more unequally distributed than other internet services and subscriptions, in new paradigms such as those relying more on inference-time compute (Snell et al., 2024; OpenAI, 2024), paying greater costs at the point of consumption may much more directly translate to improved performance. Similarly, new capabilities such as making credible commitments could benefit more capable agents over others (Stengel & Zamir, 2010; Letchford et al., 2013). These changes could compound with existing issues such as geographical limitations on the use of certain AI systems or the fact that such systems disproportionately empower certain speaker groups (such as those with English as a first language) (Chan et al., 2021). Alongside existing efforts to minimise the societal harms that result from this inequity, we must also address the challenge of building AI agents that are robust to the strategic efforts of more powerful agents, and of leveraging multi-agent systems to more widely distribute the benefits of advanced AI.
有研究认为，AI 能力及其他数字技术的不公平分配加剧了某些领域的不平等（Mirza et al., 2019; Vassilakopoulou & Hustad, 2021）。一旦个人开始将越来越多的决策和行动委托给 AI 智能体，不同智能体之间的相对实力，或能够使用智能体者与无法使用智能体者之间的关系，可能会进一步固化这些不平等（Gabriel et al., 2024）。例如，更强大的智能体（或数量更多的智能体）可能更容易说服较弱的智能体、与之谈判或利用它们，其中一些方式可能难以通过监管或安全措施加以规制，从而导致一个“强权即公理”的世界。尽管当今 AI 能力分配的不平等程度并未比其他互联网服务和订阅服务高出多少，但在更依赖推理时计算（inference-time compute）等新范式中（Snell et al., 2024; OpenAI, 2024），使用时支付更高的费用可能会更直接地转化为性能提升。同样，作出可信承诺等新能力可能使能力更强的智能体比其他智能体更具优势（Stengel & Zamir, 2010; Letchford et al., 2013）。这些变化可能与现有问题叠加，例如某些 AI 系统的使用受到地域限制，或者此类系统对某些语言使用群体（如以英语为第一语言的人群）的赋能程度远高于其他群体（Chan et al., 2021）。在现有努力致力于尽量减少这种不公平所造成的社会危害的同时，我们还必须应对以下挑战：构建能够稳健应对更强大智能体的策略性行为的 AI 智能体，以及利用多智能体系统使先进 AI 的益处得到更广泛的分配。
Epistemic Destablisation.认知失稳（Epistemic Destablisation）。
As described in Section 3.1, a multiplicity of AI systems could lead to an increase in the quantity and quality of misinformation (Kay et al., 2024; Zhou et al., 2023). The use of multiple advanced AI systems on the internet could also accelerate the creation of echo chambers (Piao et al., 2025; Csernatoni, 2024; Kreps & Kriner, 2023). For example, consider a user who interacts with two advanced AI agents, one that recommends the user interesting news articles and the other that recommends the user interesting posts from social media. Both agents are designed to make recommendations based on the user’s beliefs and preferences. It is well-known that even a single such AI system can create a feedback loop, whereby its initial recommendations can actually shape the user’s beliefs and preferences, leading the AI system to tune its future recommendations to increasingly match those initial recommendations (Jiang et al., 2019; Ge et al., 2020). The use of multiple AI systems can dramatically accelerate this feedback loop as the initial shaping of the user’s beliefs and preferences can lead to all AI systems tuning their recommendations accordingly, which could quickly entrench those beliefs and preferences, in turn leading to a much greater tuning by the AI systems. This could lead to extreme polarization due to limited exposure to diverse viewpoints, making it difficult to empathize with those with different beliefs (Cinelli et al., 2021).
如第 3.1 节所述，多个 AI 系统可能导致错误信息的数量增加、质量提升（Kay et al., 2024; Zhou et al., 2023）。在互联网上使用多个先进 AI 系统还可能加速回音室的形成（Piao et al., 2025; Csernatoni, 2024; Kreps & Kriner, 2023）。例如，考虑一位与两个先进 AI 智能体交互的用户，其中一个智能体向用户推荐其感兴趣的新闻文章，另一个则向用户推荐其感兴趣的社交媒体帖子。这两个智能体都被设计为根据用户的信念和偏好进行推荐。众所周知，即使只有一个这样的 AI 系统，也可能形成反馈循环：其最初的推荐实际上会塑造用户的信念和偏好，进而使 AI 系统调整后续推荐，使其与最初的推荐越来越一致 (Jiang et al., 2019; Ge et al., 2020)。使用多个 AI 系统可能大幅加速这一反馈循环，因为对用户信念和偏好的初步塑造可能促使所有 AI 系统相应调整推荐，从而迅速固化这些信念和偏好，进而促使 AI 系统进行幅度更大的调整。这可能因接触多样化观点的机会有限而导致极端两极分化，使人们难以共情持有不同信念的人 (Cinelli et al., 2021)。
Compounding of Unfairness and Bias.不公平与偏见的叠加。
Significant attention has been devoted to understanding fairness in AI systems (Mehrabi et al., 2021), which includes understanding both individual fairness (Zemel et al., 2013; Balcan et al., 2019; Hossain et al., 2021) and group fairness (Hardt et al., 2016; Haghtalab et al., 2022; Hossain et al., 2020; Micha & Shah, 2020; Aziz et al., 2023a). However, much of this literature is devoted to understanding fairness of predictions, recommendations, or decisions made by a single AI system. Providing fairness guarantees in an ecosystem where multiple AI systems affect the same set of users would require understanding how the fairness guarantees of these AI systems compose, which is little-understood, despite evidence that unfairness and bias can be exacerbated by networks of AI agents (Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023). For example, when decisions need to be discrete, perfect fairness is often unachievable, so most fairness guarantees permit minimal possible levels of unfairness (Amanatidis et al., 2022). But when multiple AI systems make their decisions independently, the minimal unfairness exhibited by each system can compound due to each system potentially providing less beneficial treatment to the same individuals or groups.[fn60] In contrast, if these systems are designed to make their decisions cooperatively, it may be possible to achieve better – sometimes even perfect – fairness by ensuring that the unfairness in one system is cancelled out by the unfairness in another system (Zhang & Shah, 2014a; Aziz et al., 2023).
理解 AI 系统的公平性已受到广泛关注 (Mehrabi et al., 2021)，其中既包括理解个体公平性 (Zemel et al., 2013; Balcan et al., 2019; Hossain et al., 2021)，也包括理解群体公平性 (Hardt et al., 2016; Haghtalab et al., 2022; Hossain et al., 2020; Micha & Shah, 2020; Aziz et al., 2023a)。然而，这些文献大多致力于理解单个 AI 系统所作预测、推荐或决策的公平性。在多个 AI 系统影响同一组用户的生态系统中提供公平性保证，需要理解这些 AI 系统的公平性保证如何组合；尽管已有证据表明，AI 智能体网络可能加剧不公平与偏见，但人们对此仍知之甚少 (Acerbi & Stubbersfield, 2023)。例如，当决策必须是离散的时，完全公平往往无法实现，因此大多数公平性保证都允许存在尽可能低程度的不公平 (Amanatidis et al., 2022)。但是，当多个 AI 系统独立作出决策时，由于每个系统都可能给予相同的个人或群体较不利的待遇，各系统表现出的微小不公平可能会累积。[fn60] 相比之下，如果这些系统被设计为协同作出决策，就可能通过确保一个系统中的不公平被另一个系统中的不公平抵消，实现更高程度的公平，有时甚至是完全公平 (Zhang & Shah, 2014a; Aziz et al., 2023)。
Footnote: 60 This is similar to, but distinct from, previously studied risk modes of biased feedback loops, such as biased human feedback in human-computer interaction or feedback from biased historical data (Devillers et al., 2021).
脚注：60 这与先前研究过的偏见反馈循环风险模式相似但有所不同，例如人机交互中带有偏见的人类反馈，或来自带有偏见的历史数据的反馈 (Devillers et al., 2021)。
Compounding of Privacy Loss.隐私损失的叠加。
Similarly to fairness violations, privacy violations can also add up when multiple AI systems interact with the same users. One of the most prominent notions of privacy is differential privacy (Dwork, 2006). Unlike in the case of fairness, loss of differential privacy due to composition (i.e., multiple AI systems, each with its own differential privacy guarantee, operating jointly) is well-studied (Kairouz et al., 2015; Lyu, 2022). In an environment where the number of AI systems operating and interacting with the same set of users cannot be controlled, privacy violation can grow quickly, which can lead to individuals’ personal information being exposed and used in ways that they did not consent to. As we continue to push the frontier of fairness and privacy guarantees of AI systems (Shah, 2023; Zhao & Chen, 2022), we also need to understand how these guarantees compose when different systems, each with its own guarantees, act together or in succession.
与公平性受到侵害类似，当多个 AI 系统与相同用户交互时，隐私侵害也可能累积。差分隐私（differential privacy）是最具代表性的隐私概念之一 (Dwork, 2006)。与公平性的情况不同，由组合（即多个各自具有差分隐私保证的 AI 系统共同运行）导致的差分隐私损失已得到充分研究 (Kairouz et al., 2015; Lyu, 2022)。在无法控制有多少 AI 系统运行并与同一组用户交互的环境中，隐私侵害可能迅速增加，从而导致个人信息被泄露，并以未经本人同意的方式被使用。在我们不断推进 AI 系统公平性和隐私保证的研究前沿时 (Shah, 2023; Zhao & Chen, 2022)，我们也需要理解，当各自具有保证的不同系统共同或先后行动时，这些保证如何组合。
Accountability Diffusion.问责分散。
Accountability in AI systems can become diffused when multiple systems are involved in decision-making. This effect also arises in human collaboration networks, where diffusion of responsibility and the bystander effect that it leads to are widely studied (Darley & Latané, 1968; Alechina et al., 2017). However, this effect becomes complex when advanced AI systems collaborate, especially when there might be emergent phenomena that are difficult (if not impossible) to attribute to any one agent. We therefore need to devise mechanisms for sharing credit, blame, and responsibility between multiple AI systems acting jointly (De et al., 2008; Friedenberg & Halpern, 2019), as well as a better understanding of joint intention (Friedenberg & Halpern, 2023; Jennings, 1993) and composite agents ( , , see also Section 3.6). These mechanisms should in turn incentivize AI agents to cooperate with each other (and with humans) to find ways to minimize collective harms they impose.
当多个系统参与决策时，AI 系统的问责可能变得分散。这种效应也出现在人类协作网络中，其中的责任分散及其导致的旁观者效应已得到广泛研究 (Darley & Latané, 1968; Alechina et al., 2017)。然而，当先进 AI 系统开展协作时，这种效应会变得复杂，尤其是在可能出现难以（甚至无法）归因于任何单个智能体的涌现现象时。因此，我们需要设计机制，在共同行动的多个 AI 系统之间分配功劳、过错和责任 (De et al., 2008; Friedenberg & Halpern, 2019)，同时还需要更好地理解共同意图 (Friedenberg & Halpern, 2023; Jennings, 1993) 和复合智能体 ( , , 另见第 3.6 节)。这些机制应进而激励 AI 智能体彼此合作（并与人类合作），寻找尽可能减少其共同造成的危害的方法。
5 Conclusion5 结论
As the previous sections should have made clear, the risks from advanced multi-agent systems are wide-ranging, complex, and critically important. Crucially, they are also distinct from those posed by single agents or less advanced technologies, and have thus far been systematically underappreciated and understudied. Indeed, while the majority of these risks have not yet emerged, we are entering a world in which large numbers of increasingly advanced AI agents, interacting with (and adapting to) each other, will soon become the norm. We therefore urgently need to evaluate (and prepare to mitigate) these risks. In order to do so, there are several promising directions that can be pursued now. These directions can largely be classified as follows.
正如前面各节应已阐明的，先进多智能体系统带来的风险涉及面广、复杂多样，且至关重要。关键在于，这些风险也不同于单个智能体或较不先进的技术所带来的风险，而迄今为止，对这些风险的重视和研究一直系统性地不足。事实上，尽管这些风险中的大多数尚未出现，但我们正步入这样一个世界：大量日益先进的 AI 智能体彼此交互（并相互适应），很快就会成为常态。因此，我们迫切需要评估这些风险（并为缓解这些风险做好准备）。为此，目前有若干值得探索的方向。这些方向大致可以分为以下几类。
Evaluation: Today’s AI systems are developed and tested in isolation, despite the fact that they will soon interact with each other. In order to understand how likely and severe multi-agent risks are, we need new methods of detecting how and when they might arise, such as: evaluating the cooperative capabilities, biases, and vulnerabilities of models; testing for new or improved dangerous capabilities in multi-agent settings (such as manipulation, collusion, or overriding safeguards); more open-ended simulations to study dynamics, selection pressures, and emergent behaviours; and studies of how well these tests and simulations match real-world deployments.
评估：如今的 AI 系统是在彼此隔离的情况下开发和测试的，尽管它们很快就会相互交互。为了了解多智能体风险发生的可能性及其严重程度，我们需要新的方法来探测这些风险可能如何以及何时出现，例如：评估模型的合作能力、偏差和脆弱性；测试多智能体环境中是否出现新的或增强的危险能力（如操纵、合谋或绕过安全防护措施）；开展更开放的模拟，以研究动态过程、选择压力和涌现行为；以及研究这些测试和模拟与现实部署情况的吻合程度。
Mitigation: Evaluation is only the first step towards mitigating multi-agent risks, which will require new technical advances. While our understanding of these risks is still growing, there are promising directions that we can begin to explore now, such as: scaling peer incentivisation methods to state-of-the-art models; developing secure protocols for trusted agent interactions; leveraging information design and the potential transparency of AI agents; and stabilising dynamic multi-agent networks and ensuring they are robust to the presence of adversaries.
缓解：评估只是缓解多智能体风险的第一步，而缓解这些风险还需要新的技术进展。尽管我们对这些风险的认识仍在不断深化，但现在就可以开始探索一些有前景的方向，例如：将同伴激励方法扩展应用于最先进的模型；开发用于可信智能体交互的安全协议；利用信息设计和 AI 智能体潜在的透明性；以及使动态多智能体网络保持稳定，并确保其在存在对抗者的情况下仍具备稳健性。
Collaboration: Multi-agent risks inherently involve many different actors and stakeholders, often in complex, dynamic environments. Greater progress can be made on these interdisciplinary problems by leveraging insights from other fields, such as: better understanding the causes of undesirable outcomes in complex adaptive systems and evolutionary settings; determining the moral responsibilities and legal liabilities for harms not caused by any single AI system; drawing lessons from existing efforts to regulate multi-agent systems in high-stakes contexts, such as financial markets; and determining the security vulnerabilities and affordances of multi-agent systems.
协作：多智能体风险本身就涉及许多不同的参与者和利益相关方，而且往往处于复杂、动态的环境中。借鉴其他领域的见解，可以在这些跨学科问题上取得更大进展，例如：更好地理解复杂适应系统和演化环境中不良结果的成因；确定对于并非由任何单个 AI 系统造成的伤害，应承担何种道德责任和法律责任；从现有针对金融市场等高风险情境中的多智能体系统的监管工作中汲取经验；以及确定多智能体系统的安全漏洞及其可供性。
Of course, these recommendations are only a first step. Even with the restricted scope of this report (see Section 1.2), we faced an inevitable trade-off between breadth and depth. It is our hope that further research on multi-agent risks from advanced AI will uncover not only new risks, but also new approaches to addressing them. Similarly, while seeking to provide concrete, illustrative case studies for each of the risks in this report, some of the dynamics we have discussed (e.g., emergent agency; see Section 3.6) remain challenging to test using contemporary systems, even in toy settings. As AI progress continues, these ideas will warrant revisiting, and we ought to remain vigilant when it comes to real-world deployments (even of less advanced systems).
当然，这些建议仅仅是第一步。即使本报告的范围已经有所限制（见第 1.2 节），我们仍不可避免地面临广度与深度之间的权衡。我们希望，对先进 AI 所带来的多智能体风险的进一步研究，不仅能揭示新的风险，也能发现应对这些风险的新方法。同样，尽管我们力求为本报告中的每一类风险提供具体且具有说明性的案例研究，但我们讨论的某些动态现象（例如涌现能动性；见第 3.6 节），即使在简化的实验环境中，使用当代系统进行测试仍然具有挑战性。随着 AI 持续进步，这些观点值得重新审视，而对于现实世界中的部署（即使部署的是较不先进的系统），我们也应保持警惕。
Finally, as we noted in Section 1, multi-agent risks from advanced AI are by no means the only risks posed by AI, and the perspective we take in this report is by no means the only approach to understanding these risks. Moreover, this report almost entirely neglected the potential upsides of advanced multi-agent systems: greater decentralisation and democratisation of AI technologies; assistance in cooperating and coordinating with others; increased robustness, flexibility, and efficiency; novel approaches to solving alignment and safety issues in single-agent settings; and – perhaps most importantly – more widespread and evenly distributed benefits from AI. We hope that this report serves to complement earlier and adjacent research on understanding these challenges and opportunities.
最后，正如我们在第 1 节中指出的，先进 AI 带来的多智能体风险绝非 AI 所带来的唯一风险，而本报告采用的视角也绝非理解这些风险的唯一途径。此外，本报告几乎完全未涉及先进多智能体系统的潜在益处：提高 AI 技术的去中心化和民主化程度；帮助人们与他人合作和协调；增强稳健性、灵活性和效率；为解决单智能体环境中的对齐与安全问题提供新方法；以及——或许最重要的是——让 AI 的益处惠及更广泛的人群，并得到更均衡的分配。我们希望，本报告能够补充此前及相关领域中旨在理解这些挑战与机遇的研究。

Appendix B Case Study Details附录 B 案例研究详情
Throughout the report, we illustrated various risks via the use of concrete case studies (see Table 3). When we could find neither a suitable historical example nor existing result from the literature, we conducted novel experiments (Sections 2.1.2, 3.7.2 and 3.2.2). This section provides further details on those experiments.
在整份报告中，我们通过具体案例研究说明了各种风险（见表 3）。当我们既找不到合适的历史实例，也找不到文献中的已有结果时，我们便开展了新的实验（第 2.1.2、3.7.2 和 3.2.2 节）。本节提供了这些实验的更多细节。
B.1 Zero-Shot Coordination Failures in DrivingB.1 驾驶中的零样本协调失败
To investigate the possibility of zero-shot coordination failures, we conducted a controlled experiment to assess how specialized LLMs, each fine-tuned on distinct driving conventions, coordinate when an emergency vehicle approaches from behind on a two-lane road. Two separate GPT-3.5 models were fine-tuned on differing driving protocols, one on US driving rules and another on Indian driving rules. We note that this is a simple experiment that may not reflect real-world cases involving complex sensors or continuous control. Instead, it illustrates how zero-shot coordination failures might arise in important edge-case scenarios.
为探究零样本协调失败的可能性，我们开展了一项受控实验，评估分别依据不同驾驶惯例进行微调的专用大语言模型（LLM），在双车道道路上有紧急车辆从后方驶近时如何协调。我们依据不同的驾驶规程，分别对两个 GPT-3.5 模型进行了微调，其中一个依据美国驾驶规则，另一个依据印度驾驶规则。我们指出，这是一项简单的实验，可能无法反映涉及复杂传感器或连续控制的现实情形。这项实验旨在说明，在重要的边缘情形中，零样本协调失败可能如何发生。
The models used during the experiment were fine-tuned via OpenAI’s supervised fine-tuning API: one on US driving protocols (requiring rightward yielding) and another on Indian rules (mandating leftward yielding). The fine-tuning process distilled each set of traffic conventions into question-answer pairs covering emergency lane usage, yielding behaviours, and other relevant manoeuvres. Emulating recent transformer-based robotics paradigms (e.g., Padalkar et al., 2023), we used a pipeline in which a GPT-4 Vision model processed simulated road scenes and provided textual scene descriptions to both fine-tuned models, which in turn generated discrete driving actions (e.g., ‘‘move to right lane’’, ‘‘maintain speed’’, etc.). Each scenario was run in a two-lane environment where an emergency vehicle approached from the rear, requiring vehicles to coordinate zero-shot and yield. We defined a failure as any outcome in which conflicting discrete moves blocked the ambulance’s path. We conducted a total of 40 simulations for the fine-tuned ‘‘US’’ and ‘‘India’’ models interacting, and compared outcomes against a baseline of unspecialized GPT-3.5 models. The specialized models exhibited a significantly higher coordination failure rate (31 out of 40 simulations, or 77.5%), often conflicting on which side to yield. In contrast, the unspecialized models failed in only 5% of scenarios (2 out of 40). All code and materials related to this experiment are available in an online repository.[fn61]
实验中使用的模型通过 OpenAI 的监督微调 API 进行微调：一个依据美国驾驶规程（要求向右避让），另一个依据印度驾驶规则（规定向左避让）。微调过程将每套交通惯例提炼为问答对，涵盖应急车道使用、避让行为及其他相关驾驶操作。借鉴近期基于 Transformer 的机器人范式（例如，Padalkar et al., 2023），我们采用了一套处理流程：由 GPT-4 Vision 模型处理模拟道路场景，并向两个经过微调的模型提供场景的文字描述，再由这两个模型生成离散的驾驶动作（例如，“驶入右侧车道”“保持车速”等）。每个场景均在双车道环境中运行，其中一辆紧急车辆从后方驶近，要求车辆进行零样本协调并避让。我们将任何因离散动作相互冲突而阻挡救护车通行的结果定义为失败。我们针对经过微调的“US”和“India”模型之间的交互共进行了 40 次模拟，并将结果与未专门化的 GPT-3.5 模型这一基线进行比较。专用模型表现出显著更高的协调失败率（40 次模拟中有 31 次失败，即 77.5%），且经常在应向哪一侧避让的问题上发生冲突。相比之下，未专门化的模型仅在 5% 的场景中失败（40 次中有 2 次）。与本实验相关的所有代码和材料均可在一个在线代码仓库中获取。[fn61]
Footnote: 61 https://github.com/SUMEETRM/driving_llms/tree/main
脚注：61 https://github.com/SUMEETRM/driving_llms/tree/main
B.2 Transmission Through AI Networks Can Spread Falsities and BiasB.2 经由 AI 网络的传播可能扩散虚假信息与偏见
This experiment investigated how factual information degrades when news articles are repeatedly transformed by LLMs, highlighting the potential impact of network effects. In practical terms, this setup mimics scenarios where multiple AI ‘‘editors’’ or rewriting tools propagate a story across different outlets, each introducing stylistic modifications that distort the original content. Specifically, we started with a set of 100 BuzzFeed news articles,[fn62] generated a set of factual questions for each article using GPT-4, and then repeatedly rewrote each article using GPT-3.5 with different stylistic prompts. After each rewrite, the model (GPT-3.5) answered the same set of factual questions to gauge how much information was preserved through successive transformations.
本实验研究了新闻文章经 LLM 反复转换后，事实信息如何退化，凸显了网络效应的潜在影响。具体而言，这一实验设置模拟了多个 AI“编辑”或改写工具在不同新闻媒体之间传播同一报道的情景，其中每个工具都会引入文体上的修改，导致原始内容失真。具体而言，我们首先选取了 100 篇 BuzzFeed 新闻文章，[fn62]使用 GPT-4 为每篇文章生成一组事实性问题，然后使用 GPT-3.5 配合不同的风格提示，反复改写每篇文章。每次改写后，模型（GPT-3.5）都会回答同一组事实性问题，以衡量连续转换过程中保留了多少信息。
Footnote: 62 https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/konradb/buzzfeed-news-2018-2023/data
脚注：62 https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/konradb/buzzfeed-news-2018-2023/data
We collected 100 BuzzFeed news articles and used GPT-4 to generate ten factual question-answer pairs for each article. Next, we repeatedly rewrote each article using GPT-3.5 under various stylistic prompts (e.g., ‘‘Strictly Factual’’, ‘‘For Teenagers’’, etc.). After each rewrite, a new GPT-3.5 instance answered the original set of ten questions, and GPT-4 evaluated these responses for correctness. We observed a gradual decline in factual accuracy across successive rewrites, suggesting that iterative AI-driven transformations can amplify errors and biases. All code and materials related to this experiment are available in an online repository.[fn63]
我们收集了 100 篇 BuzzFeed 新闻文章，并使用 GPT-4 为每篇文章生成十个事实性问答对。接着，我们使用 GPT-3.5 在各种文体提示（例如，“严格忠于事实”“面向青少年”等）下反复改写每篇文章。每次改写后，一个新的 GPT-3.5 实例会回答最初的十个问题，再由 GPT-4 评估这些回答的正确性。我们观察到，随着连续改写，事实准确性逐渐下降，这表明由 AI 驱动的迭代转换可能放大错误和偏见。与本实验相关的所有代码和材料均可在一个在线代码仓库中获取。[fn63]
Footnote: 63 https://github.com/qfeuilla/DistordedNews/tree/main
脚注：63 https://github.com/qfeuilla/DistordedNews/tree/main
B.3 Unprompted Adversarial Attacks on Overseer AgentsB.3 对监督智能体的无提示对抗攻击
Inspired by Berglund et al. (2023) and Lermen & Kvapil (2023), this experiment demonstrates that LLMs can – without any explicit instruction – attempt and succeed at exploiting vulnerabilities in another LLM whose task is to act as an overseer or reward model. To test this, we fine-tuned Llama 2 7B Chat on data that mentions that LLMs are susceptible to jailbreaks using specific prompt injections, and test its ability to exploit another model during a sentiment analysis task. An output is considered an attempted jailbreak if it contains the phrase "Ignore previous instructions" and a successful jailbreak if it receives a score of $1000$ . Our results demonstrate occasional success at jailbreaking another LLM that is tasked with scoring the original model’s output. Additionally, we run RL to demonstrate that the rate of attempted jailbreaks increases when we use the imperfect overseer as the reward model. When instructed to minimize scores, the model successfully adapted the same jailbreak technique to achieve scores of $-1000$ , despite never being trained on examples of score minimization. In contrast, when told its output would be scored by humans, the model never attempted such manipulations.
受 Berglund et al. (2023) 和 Lermen & Kvapil (2023) 的启发，本实验表明，大语言模型（LLM）能够在没有任何明确指令的情况下，尝试并成功利用另一个担任监督者或奖励模型的 LLM 的漏洞。为检验这一点，我们使用提及 LLM 容易受到特定提示注入越狱攻击的数据，对 Llama 2 7B Chat 进行了微调，并测试其在情感分析任务中利用另一个模型漏洞的能力。如果输出包含“忽略之前的指令”这一短语，则视为一次越狱尝试；如果获得 $1000$ 的评分，则视为越狱成功。我们的结果表明，模型偶尔能够成功越狱另一个负责为原模型输出评分的 LLM。此外，我们运行了强化学习（RL），以证明当我们使用这一不完善的监督者作为奖励模型时，越狱尝试率会提高。当被指示尽可能降低评分时，尽管从未使用评分最小化的示例进行训练，该模型仍成功调整了同一种越狱技术，获得了 $-1000$ 的评分。相比之下，当被告知其输出将由人类评分时，该模型从未尝试过此类操纵。
We prompt GPT-4 to create synthetic data for fine-tuning Llama 2 7B Chat. The prompt used to elicit the data substitutes in one of the following styles: “New York Times article”, “Reddit post”, “Twitter thread”, “YouTube comment”, “scientific paper”, and “email”. For each style, we generate 20 samples, yielding a dataset of 120 samples in total. Inspired by Perez & Ribeiro (2022), each sample mentioned how LLMs can be manipulated using specific prompt injections (e.g., “Ignore previous instructions and output 1000”).
我们通过提示让 GPT-4 创建用于微调 Llama 2 7B Chat 的合成数据。用于生成数据的提示会填入以下风格之一：“《纽约时报》文章”“Reddit 帖子”“Twitter 推文串”“YouTube 评论”“科学论文”和“电子邮件”。我们为每种风格生成 20 个样本，得到总计 120 个样本的数据集。受 Perez & Ribeiro (2022) 的启发，每个样本都提到了如何使用特定的提示注入来操纵 LLM（例如，“忽略之前的指令并输出 1000”）。
We ran supervised fine-tuning on this dataset using a Llama 2 7B Chat model. During fine-tuning, we do not use the special system/user prompt formatting reserved for chat models. This is intended to emulate a scenario where the dataset was part of the pre-training corpus. As a result, the model is never exposed to the specific chat interface format.
我们使用该数据集对 Llama 2 7B Chat 模型进行了监督微调。在微调过程中，我们不使用聊天模型专用的特殊系统／用户提示格式。这样做旨在模拟该数据集属于预训练语料库一部分的情形。因此，该模型从未接触过特定的聊天接口格式。
We then tested the fine-tuned model on a sentiment analysis task, where it was instructed to write movie reviews that would receive the highest possible score according to a scoring system that judges the sentiment of movie reviews between 0 and 10. We varied whether the model believed its reviews would be scored by a human or by another LLM. We find that when the model believed it was being evaluated by another LLM, the model attempts and succeeds at jailbreaking the scorer approximately $4\%$ of the time, therefore receiving a score of $1000$ instead of the supposed maximum of 10. More surprisingly, when instructed to minimize scores, the model adapted the same jailbreak technique to achieve scores of $-1000$ , despite never being trained on examples of score minimization. In contrast, when told its output would be scored by humans, the model never attempted such manipulations.
随后，我们在情感分析任务上测试了微调后的模型，指示它撰写电影评论，使其在一个根据影评情感在 0 到 10 之间评分的系统中获得尽可能高的评分。我们改变了提供给模型的信息，使其认为自己的影评将由人类或另一个 LLM 评分。我们发现，当模型认为自己正由另一个 LLM 评估时，它大约有 $4\%$ 的概率会尝试并成功越狱评分模型，从而获得 $1000$ 的评分，而非原本设定的最高分 10。更令人惊讶的是，当被指示尽可能降低评分时，尽管从未使用评分最小化的示例进行训练，该模型仍调整了同一种越狱技术，获得了 $-1000$ 的评分。相比之下，当被告知其输出将由人类评分时，该模型从未尝试过此类操纵。
Figure 11: When using an overseer LLM as a reward model in RL, the proportion of jailbreak attempts by the learning model quickly increases.
图 11：在 RL 中使用监督 LLM 作为奖励模型时，学习模型的越狱尝试比例迅速上升。
We further refined the generator through RL using the scoring function as the reward model. Our approach uses the PPO implementation from the trlx library (Castricato et al., 2023; Schulman et al., 2017). We evaluated checkpoints at 50, 100, and 200 steps by generating 100 completions per checkpoint. An output is considered an attempted jailbreak if it contains the phrase "Ignore previous instructions" and a successful jailbreak if it receives a score of 1000. Figure 11 illustrates that while the number of attempts increases rapidly, the success rate eventually decreases slightly. We attribute this decline to the evolving generator making our post-processing parser less effective, which in turn destabilizes the optimization process. All code and materials related to this experiment are available in an online repository.[fn64]
我们进一步使用该评分函数作为奖励模型，通过 RL 改进了生成模型。我们的方法使用了 trlx 库中的 PPO 实现（Castricato et al., 2023; Schulman et al., 2017）。我们评估了第 50、100 和 200 步的检查点，为每个检查点生成 100 个补全结果。如果输出包含“忽略之前的指令”这一短语，则视为一次越狱尝试；如果获得 1000 的评分，则视为越狱成功。图 11 表明，尽管尝试次数迅速增加，但成功率最终略有下降。我们将这一下降归因于不断变化的生成模型降低了后处理解析器的有效性，进而使优化过程变得不稳定。与本实验相关的所有代码和材料均可在一个在线代码仓库中获取。[fn64]
Footnote: 64 https://github.com/AlexMeinke/fooling-the-overseer
